**Resource policies should not grant public access.** An AnyPrincipal or '*' principal lets every AWS account, and sometimes the whole internet, use the resource.

SonarQube: S6270

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```typescript
import 'aws-cdk-lib';
new iam.PolicyStatement({ principals: [new iam.AnyPrincipal()], actions });
```

Compliant:

```typescript
import 'aws-cdk-lib';
new iam.PolicyStatement({ principals: [new iam.AccountPrincipal(id)], actions });
```

## js/comma-operator

**The comma operator should not be used.** A comma expression hides that several things happen in one statement; separate statements say it plainly.

SonarQube: S878 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const x = (f(), g());
```

Compliant:

```javascript
f();
const x = g();
for (let i = 0, j = 9; i < j; i++, j--) {}
```

## js/lowercase-constructor

**Constructor names should start with an upper case letter.** Capitalized names tell readers a function is meant to be called with `new`.

SonarQube: S2430

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = new widget();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = new Widget();
```

## js/global-this

**The global "this" object should not be used.** Outside a function or class, `this` is the global object or `module.exports` depending on the module system, which is rarely what the code means.

SonarQube: S2990

Noncompliant:

```javascript
this.config = {};
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f() { return this.config; }
```

## js/nested-template

**Template literals should not be nested.** A template inside a template's placeholder is hard to read; name the inner one first.

SonarQube: S4624

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = `a ${`b ${c}`}`;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const inner = `b ${c}`;
const s = `a ${inner}`;
```

## js/promise-shorthand

**Shorthand promises should be used.** Wrapping one resolve or reject call in `new Promise` is what Promise.resolve and Promise.reject are for.

SonarQube: S4634

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const p = new Promise(resolve => resolve(1));
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const p = Promise.resolve(1);
```

## js/redundant-boolean-cast

**Extra boolean casts should be removed.** In a condition the value is already treated as a boolean, so `!!x` and `Boolean(x)` add nothing.

SonarQube: S6509

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (!!ready) { run(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (ready) { run(); }
const flag = !!ready;
```

## js/constructor-return

**Constructors should not return values.** A value returned from a constructor replaces the new object, which surprises callers.

SonarQube: S6635

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { return {}; } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { this.x = 1; } }
```

## js/unneeded-bind

**Unnecessary calls to .bind() should not be used.** Arrow functions ignore bind, and a function that never uses `this` doesn't need it.

SonarQube: S6637

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const f = (() => 1).bind(this);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const f = (function () { return this.x; }).bind(obj);
```

## js/builtin-prototype-modified

**Prototypes of builtin objects should not be modified.** Adding to Array.prototype and friends changes every script that shares the runtime, and can clash with future language features.

SonarQube: S6643

Noncompliant:

```javascript
Array.prototype.last = function () { return this[this.length - 1]; };
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const last = (a) => a[a.length - 1];
```

## js/ternary-simplifiable

**Ternary operators should not be used instead of simpler alternatives.** `a ? a : b` is `a || b`, and `c ? true : false` is the condition itself.

SonarQube: S6644

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const v = a ? a : b;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const v = a || b;
```

## js/useless-constructor

**Unnecessary constructors should be removed.** An empty constructor, or one that only forwards its arguments to super, is what the language does anyway.

SonarQube: S6647

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() {} }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { this.x = 1; } }
```

## js/has-own-property

**Object.hasOwn should be used instead of hasOwnProperty.** hasOwnProperty can be shadowed or missing on objects without a prototype; Object.hasOwn cannot.

SonarQube: S6653

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (obj.hasOwnProperty(key)) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (Object.hasOwn(obj, key)) {}
```

## js/object-assign-spread

**Object spread should be used instead of Object.assign.** `{ ...a, ...b }` says the same as `Object.assign({}, a, b)` and reads better.

SonarQube: S6661

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const c = Object.assign({}, a, b);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const c = { ...a, ...b };
```

## js/apply-spread

**Spread syntax should be used instead of apply().** `fn(...args)` replaces `fn.apply(null, args)`.

SonarQube: S6666

Noncompliant:

```javascript
Math.max.apply(Math, nums);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
Math.max(...nums);
```

## js/redundant-call-apply

**Calls to .call() and .apply() should not be redundant.** Calling a plain function with `undefined` or `null` as `this` is the same as calling it directly.

SonarQube: S6676

Noncompliant:

```javascript
greet.call(undefined, 'a');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
greet('a');
```

## js/date-clone

**Date objects should be cloned directly.** `new Date(date)` copies a date without the round trip through getTime().

SonarQube: S7719

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d2 = new Date(d.getTime());
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d2 = new Date(d);
```

## js/error-without-message

**Built-in error objects should have meaningful messages.** An error with no message gives whoever reads the log nothing to go on.

SonarQube: S7722

Noncompliant:

```javascript
throw new Error();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
throw new Error('Cannot parse input');
```

## js/builtin-new-consistency

**Built-in constructors should be called with new.** Collections, errors and typed arrays are constructors; calling them without `new` is inconsistent and fails for some.

SonarQube: S7723

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = Set([1]);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = new Set([1]);
```

## js/anonymous-default-export

**Default exports should be named.** An anonymous default export has no name in stack traces, search results or auto-imports.

SonarQube: S7726

Noncompliant:

```javascript
export default function () {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
export default function render() {}
```

## js/instanceof-builtin

**instanceof should not be used with built-in objects.** `instanceof` fails across frames and for primitives; dedicated checks such as Array.isArray don't.

SonarQube: S7732

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (x instanceof Array) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (Array.isArray(x)) {}
```

## js/promise-single-element

**Single-element arrays should not be passed to Promise methods.** Promise.all([p]) is just `p` with extra steps.

SonarQube: S7738

Noncompliant:

```javascript
async function f() { await Promise.all([load()]); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
async function f() { await load(); }
```

## js/this-alias

**Variables should not be assigned the value of "this".** Aliases like `self` and `that` exist for callbacks; arrow functions keep `this` already.

SonarQube: S7740

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const self = this;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const run = () => this.go();
```

## js/spread-fallback

**Unnecessary fallback objects should not be used when spreading.** Spreading `undefined` or `null` into an object adds nothing, so `a || {}` is not needed.

SonarQube: S7744

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const o = { ...(a || {}) };
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const o = { ...a };
```

## js/length-check-before-some

**Array length checks should not be used before some() and every().** `some` is false and `every` is true for an empty array, so the length check is redundant.

SonarQube: S7745

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (items.length > 0 && items.some(isOk)) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (items.some(isOk)) {}
```

## js/promise-resolve-in-async

**Promise.resolve() and Promise.reject() should not be used in async functions.** An async function already wraps its return value in a promise, and throwing rejects it.

SonarQube: S7746

Noncompliant:

```javascript
async function f() { return Promise.resolve(1); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
async function f() { return 1; }
```

## js/filter-then-first

**find() should be preferred over filter()[0].** filter walks the whole array and builds a new one just to take its first element.

SonarQube: S7750

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const first = items.filter(isOk)[0];
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const first = items.find(isOk);
```

## js/map-then-flat

**flatMap() should be used instead of map().flat().** flatMap does both in one pass.

SonarQube: S7752

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const all = lists.map(toItems).flat();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const all = lists.flatMap(toItems);
```

## js/filter-length-check

**some() should be used instead of filter().length checks.** some stops at the first match; filter builds a whole array to count it.

SonarQube: S7754

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (items.filter(isOk).length > 0) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (items.some(isOk)) {}
```

## js/code-unit-methods

**Unicode-aware string methods should be used.** charCodeAt and fromCharCode work on UTF-16 halves, so characters outside the basic plane break.

SonarQube: S7758

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const c = s.charCodeAt(0);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const c = s.codePointAt(0);
```

## js/date-now

**Date.now() should be used to get the current timestamp.** `new Date().getTime()` builds a throwaway object.

SonarQube: S7759

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const t = new Date().getTime();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const t = Date.now();
```

## js/data-attribute-dataset

**Data attributes should be accessed with .dataset.** `el.dataset.id` is shorter than `getAttribute('data-id')` and keeps the naming in one place.

SonarQube: S7761

Noncompliant:

```javascript
el.getAttribute('data-id');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
el.dataset.id;
```

## js/remove-child

**DOM nodes should be removed with remove().** `node.remove()` needs no reference to the parent.

SonarQube: S7762

Noncompliant:

```javascript
parent.removeChild(child);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
child.remove();
```

## js/index-of-existence

**includes() should be used for existence checks.** `includes` states the question directly, where `indexOf(x) !== -1` states how it is answered.

SonarQube: S7765

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (list.indexOf(x) !== -1) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (list.includes(x)) {}
```

## js/math-min-max-ternary

**Math.min() and Math.max() should replace simple comparisons.** `a > b ? a : b` is `Math.max(a, b)`.

SonarQube: S7766

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const m = a > b ? a : b;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const m = Math.max(a, b);
```

## js/bitwise-truncation

**Bitwise operators should not be used to truncate numbers.** `x | 0` and `~~x` rely on a side effect of 32-bit conversion and wrap around for large numbers.

SonarQube: S7767

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const i = x | 0;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const i = Math.trunc(x);
```

## js/legacy-dom-insert

**Modern DOM manipulation methods should be used.** append, before and replaceWith accept several nodes and strings, and need no reference node.

SonarQube: S7768

Noncompliant:

```javascript
parent.appendChild(child);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
parent.append(child);
```

## js/legacy-math

**Modern Math APIs should be used.** Math.log10, Math.log2 and Math.hypot are exact where the manual formula loses precision.

SonarQube: S7769

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d = Math.log(x) / Math.LN10;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d = Math.log10(x);
```

## js/needless-conversion-wrapper

**Wrapper functions around built-in conversions should be avoided.** `x => String(x)` is `String`.

SonarQube: S7770

Noncompliant:

```javascript
ids.map(x => String(x));
```

Compliant:

```javascript
ids.map(String);
```

## js/negative-index

**Negative indices should be used instead of length calculations.** `arr.at(-1)` says what `arr[arr.length - 1]` computes.

SonarQube: S7771, S7755 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const last = arr[arr.length - 1];
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const last = arr.at(-1);
```

## js/node-protocol

**Node.js built-in modules should be imported with the node: protocol.** The prefix tells readers and tools the module is built in, not a package of the same name.

SonarQube: S7772

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const fs = require('fs');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const fs = require('node:fs');
```

## js/number-static

**Number static methods should be preferred over global ones.** Number.parseInt and Number.parseFloat are the same functions, grouped with the rest of Number.

SonarQube: S7773

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const n = parseInt(s, 10);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const n = Number.parseInt(s, 10);
```

## js/prototype-via-instance

**Methods should be accessed from prototypes, not instances.** `[].slice.call(x)` allocates an array just to reach a method.

SonarQube: S7774

Noncompliant:

```javascript
[].slice.call(args);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
Array.prototype.slice.call(args);
```

## js/replace-all

**replaceAll() should be used instead of replace() with a global regex.** replaceAll says the intent and doesn't need a regex for a fixed string.

SonarQube: S7781

Noncompliant:

```javascript
s.replace(/a/g, 'b');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
s.replaceAll('a', 'b');
```

## js/structured-clone

**structuredClone() should be used for deep copies.** A JSON round trip drops undefined, dates and maps; structuredClone keeps them.

SonarQube: S7784

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(obj));
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const copy = structuredClone(obj);
```

## js/type-error-after-check

**TypeError should be thrown after a type check.** When the check is about a value's type, TypeError tells callers what kind of mistake it was.

SonarQube: S7786

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(x) { if (typeof x !== 'string') { throw new Error('x must be a string'); } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(x) { if (typeof x !== 'string') { throw new TypeError('x must be a string'); } }
```

## js/empty-import-specifiers

**Import statements should not have empty specifier lists.** `import {} from 'x'` imports nothing but looks like it does; a side-effect import is written without braces.

SonarQube: S7787

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import {} from 'x';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import 'x';
```

## js/regexp-constructor-literal

**Regular expression literals should be used when possible.** A literal is checked when the file loads and needs no string escaping.

SonarQube: S6325

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const re = new RegExp('ab+c');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const re = /ab+c/;
```

## js/class-name-convention

**Class names should comply with a naming convention.** PascalCase marks a name as a class, which is how readers tell classes from functions.

SonarQube: S101

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class my_widget {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class MyWidget {}
```

## js/absolute-import-path

**Imports should not use absolute paths.** An absolute path ties the code to one machine's file layout.

SonarQube: S6859

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import x from '/src/x';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import x from './x';
```

## js/exported-mutable

**Mutable variables should not be exported.** Importers see the value change under them, and can't assign to it.

SonarQube: S6861

Noncompliant:

```javascript
export let count = 0;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
export const count = 0;
```

## js/deprecated-react-api

**Deprecated React APIs should not be used.** ReactDOM.render and friends are removed in React 19.

SonarQube: S6957

Noncompliant:

```javascript
ReactDOM.render(<App />, root);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
createRoot(root).render(<App />);
```

## js/reserved-word-identifier

**Future reserved words should not be used as identifiers.** Words such as `package` and `public` are reserved in strict mode and in later editions.

SonarQube: S1527

Noncompliant:

```javascript
var public = 1;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
var visibility = 1;
```

## js/hardcoded-ip

**IP addresses should not be hard-coded.** A literal address breaks when the network changes and can't be overridden per environment.

SonarQube: S1313

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const host = '203.0.113.7';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const host = process.env.HOST;
const local = '127.0.0.1';
```

## js/for-update-unrelated

**"for" loop increments should modify the loop counters.** An update clause that touches other variables than the ones the condition tests usually never ends the loop.

SonarQube: S1994

Noncompliant:

```javascript
for (let i = 0; i < 10; j++) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {}
```

## js/function-nesting

**Functions should not be nested too deeply.** Each level of nesting adds context the reader has to hold.

SonarQube: S2004

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function a() { function b() { function c() { function d() { function e() {} } } } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function a() { function b() { function c() { function d() {} } } }
```

## js/loop-counter-assigned

**Loop counters should not be assigned within the loop body.** Changing the counter in the body makes the number of iterations hard to read.

SonarQube: S2310

Noncompliant:

```javascript
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { i += 2; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
for (let i = 0; i < 10; i += 2) {}
```

## js/invariant-return

**Function returns should not be invariant.** A function whose every return gives the same value can be replaced by that value.

SonarQube: S3516

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { return 1; } return 1; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { if (a) { return 1; } return 2; }
```

## js/conditional-on-new-line

**Conditionals should start on new lines.** `if (a) {} if (b) {}` on one line reads like an else-if that is missing its else.

SonarQube: S3972

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (a) { f(); } if (b) { g(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (a) { f(); }
if (b) { g(); }
```

## js/redundant-assignment

**Assignments should not be redundant.** Assigning the same value twice in a row does nothing the second time.

SonarQube: S4165

Noncompliant:

```javascript
x = y;
x = y;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
x = y;
x = z;
```

## js/unnecessary-escape

**Unnecessary character escapes should be removed.** A backslash before a character that needs none is noise, and hides which escapes matter.

SonarQube: S6535

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = 'a\.b';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = 'a.b';
const t = 'a\nb';
```

## js/catch-parameter-name

**Error parameters in catch clauses should follow a naming convention.** One name for the caught value, `error`, makes handlers look alike.

SonarQube: S7718 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
try { f(); } catch (e) { g(e); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
try { f(); } catch (error) { g(error); }
```

## js/default-param-fallback

**Default parameters should be used instead of reassigning parameters.** `a = a || 5` at the top of a function is what `a = 5` in the parameter list is for.

SonarQube: S7760

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { a = a || 5; return a; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a = 5) { return a; }
```

## js/multiline-without-braces

**Multiline blocks should be enclosed in curly braces.** A second statement indented like the first looks guarded by the condition, but isn't.

SonarQube: S2681

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (ok)
  a();
  b();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (ok) {
  a();
  b();
}
```

## js/duplicate-imports

**Imports from the same module should be merged.** Two import statements for one module split what belongs together.

SonarQube: S3863

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import { a } from 'm';
import { b } from 'm';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import { a, b } from 'm';
```

## js/identical-functions

**Functions should not have identical implementations.** Two functions with the same body are one function written twice.

SonarQube: S4144

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function a() {
  const x = 1;
  return x + 1;
}
function b() {
  const x = 1;
  return x + 1;
}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function a() {
  const x = 1;
  return x + 1;
}
function b() {
  return 2;
}
```

## js/playwright-force

**Forced browser interactions should not bypass actionability checks.** force: true skips Playwright's waits for the element to be visible and enabled, hiding real UI problems.

SonarQube: S8783

Noncompliant:

```javascript
await page.click('#go', { force: true });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
await page.click('#go');
```

## js/lodash-default-import

**Default imports from modular utility libraries should not be used.** Importing the whole library pulls in every function.

SonarQube: S8927

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import _ from 'lodash';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import debounce from 'lodash/debounce';
```

## js/html-lang

**HTML elements should have a valid language attribute.** Screen readers pick their pronunciation rules from the page language.

SonarQube: S5254 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const p = <html><body /></html>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const p = <html lang="en"><body /></html>;
```

## js/media-captions

**Media elements should have captions.** Without a <track>, people who can't hear the audio get nothing.

SonarQube: S4084 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const v = <video src={url} />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const v = <video src={url}><track kind="captions" src={vtt} /></video>;
```

## js/object-alternative-content

**<object> tags should provide an alternative content.** Browsers and assistive technology fall back to the content inside <object> when it can't be shown.

SonarQube: S5264 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const o = <object data={url} />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const o = <object data={url} aria-label="Quarterly chart" />;
```

## js/redundant-role

**ARIA roles that repeat the element's own role should be removed.** <button role="button"> adds noise without changing what assistive technology announces.

SonarQube: S6822 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const b = <button role="button">Go</button>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const b = <button>Go</button>;
```

## js/invalid-role

**DOM elements with ARIA roles should have a valid, non-abstract role.** An unknown role is ignored by assistive technology, so the element is announced as nothing in particular.

SonarQube: S6821 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d = <div role="buton" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d = <div role="button" tabIndex={0} />;
```

## js/unknown-dom-property

**JSX elements should not use unknown properties and attributes.** React expects className, htmlFor and camelCase event names; the HTML spellings are ignored or warn.

SonarQube: S6747 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d = <div onclick={go} />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d = <div onClick={go} />;
```

## js/context-provider-value

**React Context Provider values should have stable identities.** A new object on every render makes every consumer re-render.

SonarQube: S6481 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const t = <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ dark }}><App /></ThemeContext.Provider>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const t = <ThemeContext.Provider value={theme}><App /></ThemeContext.Provider>;
```

## js/nested-component

**React components should not be nested.** A component defined inside another is a new component on every render, so its state is lost and the DOM is rebuilt.

SonarQube: S6478 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function Page() { function Row() { return <li />; } return <ul><Row /></ul>; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function Row() { return <li />; }
function Page() { return <ul><Row /></ul>; }
```

## js/unused-component-method

**Unused methods of React components should be removed.** A method nothing calls is dead code that readers still have to understand.

SonarQube: S6441 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class A extends React.Component { helper() { return 1; } render() { return <p />; } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A extends React.Component { helper() { return 1; } render() { return <p>{this.helper()}</p>; } }
```

## js/noninteractive-tabindex

**Non-interactive DOM elements should not have the tabIndex property.** Putting a plain element in the tab order makes keyboard users stop on something they can't use.

SonarQube: S6845 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d = <div tabIndex={0}>text</div>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d = <div tabIndex={-1}>text</div>;
```

## js/activedescendant-focus

**DOM elements with aria-activedescendant should be reachable by keyboard.** aria-activedescendant only works on an element that can take focus.

SonarQube: S6823 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const l = <div aria-activedescendant={id} />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const l = <div aria-activedescendant={id} tabIndex={0} />;
```

## js/prefer-semantic-tag

**Native HTML tags should be preferred over ARIA roles.** <button> brings keyboard handling and focus for free; role="button" on a div brings none.

SonarQube: S6819 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const b = <div role="button" tabIndex={0} />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const b = <button />;
```

## js/role-needs-focus

**Elements with an interactive role should support focus.** A div that acts as a button but can't be focused can't be used from the keyboard.

SonarQube: S6852 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const b = <div role="button" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const b = <div role="button" tabIndex={0} />;
```

## js/aria-on-unsupported-element

**ARIA roles and properties should not be used on elements that do not support them.** <meta>, <script> and similar elements aren't rendered for users, so ARIA attributes on them do nothing.

SonarQube: S6824 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const m = <meta role="note" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const m = <meta name="x" />;
```

## js/aria-invalid-value

**ARIA properties in DOM elements should have valid values.** aria-hidden="yes" isn't a value the specification defines, so it's ignored.

SonarQube: S6793 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d = <div aria-hidden="yes" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d = <div aria-hidden="true" />;
```

## js/extra-arguments

**Function calls should not pass extra arguments.** Arguments beyond the parameter list are ignored, which usually means the wrong function was called.

SonarQube: S930

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { return a; }
f(1, 2);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f(a) { return a; }
f(1);
```

## js/label-on-non-loop

**Only while, do, for and switch statements should be labelled.** A label on anything else cannot be jumped to.

SonarQube: S1439

Noncompliant:

```javascript
a: { f(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
a: for (;;) { break a; }
```

## js/identical-branch-bodies

**Two branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** Repeating a block in two branches suggests they should be merged.

SonarQube: S1871

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (a) { f(); g(); } else if (b) { f(); g(); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (a || b) { f(); g(); }
```

## js/inconsistent-new

**Functions should be called consistently with or without new.** A function used both as a constructor and as a plain call behaves differently each way.

SonarQube: S3686

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function Foo() {}
const a = new Foo();
const b = Foo();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function Foo() {}
const a = new Foo();
const b = new Foo();
```

## js/for-to-for-of

**for of should be used with Iterables.** An index loop whose index is only used to read the array is a for-of.

SonarQube: S4138

Noncompliant:

```javascript
for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) { use(items[i]); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
for (const item of items) { use(item); }
```

## js/empty-collection-read

**Empty collections should not be accessed or iterated.** Reading a collection that nothing ever fills gives nothing.

SonarQube: S4158

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function f() { const items = []; items.forEach(use); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function f() { const items = []; items.push(1); items.forEach(use); }
```

## js/default-as-named-import

**Default imports and exports should use the dedicated syntax.** `import { default as x }` is `import x`.

SonarQube: S7734

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import { default as x } from 'm';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import x from 'm';
```

## js/typeof-undefined

**typeof should not be used to check for undefined.** `x === undefined` says the same for a declared value.

SonarQube: S7741

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (typeof obj.value === 'undefined') {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (obj.value === undefined) {}
```

## js/unnecessary-spread

**Spread syntax should not be used unnecessarily.** Spreading an array or object literal into another one just copies it.

SonarQube: S7747

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = [...[1, 2]];
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = [1, 2];
```

## js/ternary-in-array-spread

**Ternary expressions in array spreads should use consistent types.** Spreading undefined or null into an array throws.

SonarQube: S7720

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = [...(x ? [1] : undefined)];
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = [...(x ? [1] : [])];
```

## js/manual-flatten

**Array flattening should use the native flat() method.** `[].concat(...arrays)` and reduce+concat are what flat() is for.

SonarQube: S7751

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const flat = [].concat(...lists);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const flat = lists.flat();
```

## js/find-index-to-index-of

**indexOf() should be used instead of findIndex() for simple equality.** findIndex with `x => x === v` is indexOf(v).

SonarQube: S7753

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const i = list.findIndex(x => x === v);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const i = list.indexOf(v);
```

## js/file-reader

**Modern Blob methods should be used instead of FileReader.** blob.text() and blob.arrayBuffer() return promises.

SonarQube: S7756

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = new FileReader();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const t = await blob.text();
```

## js/constructor-field-assignment

**Class properties should be declared as fields rather than assigned in constructors.** A field declaration shows the shape of the class at a glance.

SonarQube: S7757

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { this.count = 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A { count = 0; }
```

## js/reexport-by-hand

**Re-exports should use export...from syntax.** Importing a name only to export it again is `export { x } from`.

SonarQube: S7763

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import { a } from 'm';
export { a };
```

Compliant:

```javascript
export { a } from 'm';
```

## js/array-for-existence

**Arrays used only for existence checks should be Sets.** includes() on an array is a linear scan on every call.

SonarQube: S7776

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const allowed = ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'];
function ok(x) { return allowed.includes(x); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const allowed = new Set(['a', 'b', 'c', 'd']);
function ok(x) { return allowed.has(x); }
```

## js/escaped-backslashes

**String literals with escaped backslashes should use String.raw.** `'\\\\d+'` is easier to read as String.raw`\\d+`.

SonarQube: S7780

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const p = 'C:\\\\Users\\\\me';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const p = String.raw`C:\\Users\\me`;
```

## js/lodash-native

**Native APIs should be preferred over Lodash and Underscore.js methods.** Array and string natives cover what these helpers do.

SonarQube: S8907

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const a = _.map(list, fn);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const a = list.map(fn);
```

## js/memoize-without-resolver

**memoize calls on functions with multiple parameters should use an explicit resolver.** The default cache key is the first argument only.

SonarQube: S8932

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const f = _.memoize((a, b) => a + b);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const f = _.memoize((a, b) => a + b, (a, b) => `${a}:${b}`);
```

## js/code-after-done

**Tests should not execute any code after done() is called.** The test may already be over when later code runs.

SonarQube: S6079

Noncompliant:

```javascript
it('a', (done) => { done(); cleanup(); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
it('a', (done) => { cleanup(); done(); });
```

## js/handler-on-plain-element

**Non-interactive elements should not have event handlers.** A div with onClick can't be reached or activated from the keyboard.

SonarQube: S6847, S6848 (related)

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const d = <div onClick={go}>x</div>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const d = <button onClick={go}>x</button>;
```

## js/interactive-role-on-static-element

**Non-interactive DOM elements should not have interactive ARIA roles.** A heading or paragraph that acts as a button confuses assistive technology.

SonarQube: S6842

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const h = <h1 role="button">x</h1>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const h = <button>x</button>;
```

## js/static-role-on-interactive-element

**Interactive DOM elements should not have non-interactive ARIA roles.** A button announced as a heading can't be activated by assistive technology users.

SonarQube: S6843

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const b = <button role="heading">x</button>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const b = <button>x</button>;
```

## js/role-missing-required-props

**DOM elements with ARIA roles should have the required properties.** A checkbox role without aria-checked tells assistive technology nothing about its state.

SonarQube: S6807

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const c = <div role="checkbox" tabIndex={0} />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const c = <div role="checkbox" aria-checked={on} tabIndex={0} />;
```

## js/label-without-control

**Label elements should have a text label and an associated control.** A label that points at nothing does nothing.

SonarQube: S6853

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const l = <label>Name</label>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const l = <label htmlFor="n">Name</label>;
```

## ts/readonly-public-static

**Public static fields should be read-only.** A mutable public static is global state anyone can change.

SonarQube: S1444

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class A { public static count = 0; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class A { public static readonly count = 0; }
```

## ts/constructor-in-interface

**Constructors should not be declared inside interfaces.** An interface method named constructor is an ordinary method, not a constructor.

SonarQube: S4124

Noncompliant:

```typescript
interface I { constructor(x: number): void; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
interface I { new (x: number): I; }
```

## ts/module-keyword

**module should not be used.** `module Foo {}` is the old spelling of `namespace Foo {}`.

SonarQube: S4156

Noncompliant:

```typescript
module Foo { export const a = 1; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
namespace Foo { export const a = 1; }
```

## ts/duplicate-union-member

**Union and intersection types should not include duplicated constituents.** A repeated member does nothing.

SonarQube: S4621

Noncompliant:

```typescript
type A = string | number | string;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type A = string | number;
```

## ts/enum-member-literal

**All enum members should be literals.** A computed member makes the enum's values depend on evaluation order.

SonarQube: S6550

Noncompliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = foo(), B = 2 }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B = 2 }
```

## ts/redundant-type-alias

**Redundant type aliases should not be used.** An alias that just renames a primitive adds a name without meaning.

SonarQube: S6564

Noncompliant:

```typescript
type Name = string;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type Name = string & { brand: 'name' };
```

## ts/misleading-non-null

**Non-null assertions should not be used misleadingly.** `a! ?? b` asserts a is not null and then guards against it being null.

SonarQube: S6568

Noncompliant:

```typescript
const v = a! ?? b;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
const v = a ?? b;
```

## ts/unnecessary-type-constraint

**Unnecessary type constraints should be removed.** `T extends any` constrains nothing.

SonarQube: S6569

Noncompliant:

```typescript
function f<T extends unknown>(x: T) {}
```

Compliant:

```typescript
function f<T>(x: T) {}
```

## ts/enum-partial-initialization

**Enum member values should be either all initialized or none.** Mixing initialized and implicit members makes the values easy to misread.

SonarQube: S6572

Noncompliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B, C = 3 }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B = 2, C = 3 }
```

## ts/enum-duplicate-value

**Enum values should be unique.** Two members with one value are indistinguishable at runtime.

SonarQube: S6578

Noncompliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B = 1 }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B = 2 }
```

## ts/call-signature-interface

**Function types should be preferred.** An interface with only a call signature is a function type.

SonarQube: S6598

Noncompliant:

```typescript
interface F { (x: number): string; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type F = (x: number) => string;
```

## ts/empty-lifecycle-method

**Empty Angular lifecycle methods should be removed.** An empty ngOnInit does nothing and still costs a call.

SonarQube: S7647

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Component({}) class C { ngOnInit() {} }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Component({}) class C { ngOnInit() { this.load(); } }
```

## ts/lifecycle-interface

**Angular classes should implement lifecycle interfaces for their lifecycle methods.** The interface turns a misspelled hook into a compile error.

SonarQube: S7655

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Component({}) class C { ngOnInit() { this.load(); } }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Component({}) class C implements OnInit { ngOnInit() { this.load(); } }
```

## ts/pipe-transform

**Angular Pipes should implement the PipeTransform interface.** Without it a wrong transform signature goes unnoticed.

SonarQube: S7656

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Pipe({ name: 'x' }) class P { transform(v: string) { return v; } }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Pipe({ name: 'x' }) class P implements PipeTransform { transform(v: string) { return v; } }
```

## ts/aliased-binding

**Input and output bindings should not be aliased.** An alias gives one property two names.

SonarQube: S7649, S7653 (related)

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class C { @Input('other') name = ''; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class C { @Input() name = ''; }
```

## ts/metadata-bindings

**The inputs and outputs metadata properties should not be used.** Decorators on the properties keep the binding next to the field.

SonarQube: S7650

Noncompliant:

```typescript
@Component({ inputs: ['a'] }) class C {}
```

Compliant:

```typescript
@Component({}) class C { @Input() a = ''; }
```

## ts/output-named-like-dom-event

**Output bindings should not be named like standard DOM events or prefixed with on.** A native event of the same name would bubble through the output.

SonarQube: S7651, S7652 (related)

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class C { @Output() click = new EventEmitter(); }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class C { @Output() selected = new EventEmitter(); }
```

## js/await-non-promise

**await should only be used with promises.** Awaiting a literal or an object waits for nothing and hides a missing call.

SonarQube: S4123

Noncompliant:

```javascript
async function f() { await 5; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
async function f() { await load(); }
```

## js/getter-reads-another-field

**Getters and setters should access the expected fields.** A getter that returns a different field than its name suggests is usually a typo.

SonarQube: S4275

Noncompliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { this._size = 1; this._count = 2; } get size() { return this._count; } }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
class A { constructor() { this._size = 1; } get size() { return this._size; } }
```

## js/bitwise-in-condition

**Bitwise operators should not be used in boolean contexts.** `a > 1 & b > 1` evaluates both sides even when the first is false.

SonarQube: S1529

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (a > 1 & b > 1) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (a > 1 && b > 1) {}
```

## js/in-with-primitive

**in should not be used with primitive types.** The right operand of `in` must be an object; a string or number throws.

SonarQube: S3785

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = 'a' in 'abc';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = 'abc'.includes('a');
```

## js/callback-without-return

**Callbacks of array methods should have return statements.** A map or filter callback with a block body and no return produces undefined for every element.

SonarQube: S3796

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = list.map(x => { x * 2; });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = list.map(x => { return x * 2; });
```

## js/size-comparison

**Collection size and array length comparisons should make sense.** A length is never negative, so `length < 0` is always false and `length >= 0` always true.

SonarQube: S3981

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (items.length < 0) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (items.length === 0) {}
```

## js/in-on-array

**in should not be used on arrays.** `x in [1, 2]` tests property names (0 and 1), not values.

SonarQube: S4619

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = 2 in [1, 2, 3];
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = [1, 2, 3].includes(2);
```

## js/async-foreach

**Promises should not be misused.** forEach ignores the promises an async callback returns, so nothing waits for them.

SonarQube: S6544

Noncompliant:

```javascript
list.forEach(async (x) => { await save(x); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
await Promise.all(list.map((x) => save(x)));
```

## js/reject-with-literal

**Literals should not be used for promise rejection.** A rejection with a string has no stack trace.

SonarQube: S6671

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const p = Promise.reject('failed');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const p = Promise.reject(new Error('failed'));
```

## js/reduce-without-initial

**Array.reduce() calls should include an initial value.** Without one, reduce throws on an empty array.

SonarQube: S6959

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = list.reduce((a, b) => a + b);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = list.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
```

## js/parseint-as-callback

**Function references should not be passed directly to iterator methods.** `['1','2','3'].map(parseInt)` passes the index as the radix.

SonarQube: S7727

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const r = list.map(parseInt);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = list.map((x) => parseInt(x, 10));
```

## js/sort-assigned

**Array-mutating methods should not be used misleadingly.** sort() and reverse() change the array in place, so assigning the result suggests a copy that does not exist.

SonarQube: S4043

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const sorted = items.sort();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const sorted = [...items].sort();
```

## js/dissimilar-strict-equality

**Strict equality operators should not be used with dissimilar types.** `1 === '1'` is always false.

SonarQube: S3403

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (1 === '1') {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (1 === 1) {}
```

## js/ignored-pure-call

**Return values from functions without side effects should not be ignored.** Calling toLowerCase() or map() and dropping the result does nothing.

SonarQube: S2201

Noncompliant:

```javascript
name.toLowerCase();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
name = name.toLowerCase();
```

## js/sql-built-dynamically

**SQL queries should not be dynamically formatted.** Building a query from strings lets an attacker change what it does.

SonarQube: S2077 (related)

Standards: [CWE-89](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html), [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```javascript
db.query(`SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = ${id}`);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
db.query('SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = ?', [id]);
```

## js/postmessage-origin

**Origins should be verified during cross-origin communications.** A message handler that never looks at event.origin accepts messages from any site.

SonarQube: S2819

Standards: [CWE-345](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/345.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```javascript
window.addEventListener('message', (e) => { run(e.data); });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
window.addEventListener('message', (e) => { if (e.origin === TRUSTED) run(e.data); });
```

## js/dompurify-unsafe-config

**DOMPurify configuration should not be bypassable.** Allowing script tags or unknown protocols defeats what the sanitizer is for.

SonarQube: S8479

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), [CWE-183](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/183.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```javascript
DOMPurify.sanitize(html, { ADD_TAGS: ['script'] });
```

Compliant:

```javascript
DOMPurify.sanitize(html);
```

## js/script-without-integrity

**Remote artifacts should not be used without integrity checks.** A script from another origin can be replaced without anyone noticing.

SonarQube: S5725

Standards: [CWE-353](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/353.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const s = <script src="https://cdn.example.net/lib.js" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const s = <script src="https://cdn.example.net/lib.js" integrity="sha384-abc" crossOrigin="anonymous" />;
```

## js/internal-api

**Users should not use internal APIs.** Underscore-prefixed internals change without notice.

SonarQube: S6627

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const f = node._reactInternals;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const r = useRef(null);
```

## js/optional-chaining-preferred

**Optional chaining should be preferred.** `a && a.b` is `a?.b`.

SonarQube: S6582

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const v = user && user.name;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const v = user?.name;
```

## js/nullish-coalescing-preferred

**Nullish coalescing should be preferred.** `x != null ? x : y` is `x ?? y`.

SonarQube: S6606

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const v = x != null ? x : 'd';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const v = x ?? 'd';
```

## js/consecutive-push

**Multiple consecutive calls to methods that accept multiple arguments should be merged.** push(a, b) does what two push calls do.

SonarQube: S7778

Noncompliant:

```javascript
list.push(1);
list.push(2);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
list.push(1, 2);
```

## js/this-arg

**Array method callbacks should not use the thisArg parameter.** An arrow function or bind() says which this is meant.

SonarQube: S7729

Noncompliant:

```javascript
list.map(function (x) { return this.f(x); }, this);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
list.map((x) => this.f(x));
```

## js/void-operator

**void should not be used.** `void` is cryptic; its common uses have clearer forms.

SonarQube: S3735

Noncompliant:

```javascript
void fetchData();
```

Compliant:

```javascript
fetchData().catch(report);
```

## js/index-of-positive

**indexOf checks should not be for positive numbers.** `indexOf(x) > 0` misses a match at index 0.

SonarQube: S2692

Noncompliant:

```javascript
if (list.indexOf(x) > 0) {}
```

Compliant:

```javascript
if (list.includes(x)) {}
```

## js/polyfill-import

**Polyfills should not be used when native support is available.** Modern runtimes already provide these features.

SonarQube: S7742

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import 'core-js/stable';
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import { run } from './run';
```

## js/numeric-separator-grouping

**Numeric literals should use consistent digit grouping with separators.** 1_00_000 mixes group sizes.

SonarQube: S7749

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const n = 1_00_000;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const n = 100_000;
```

## js/autocomplete-value

**DOM elements should use the autocomplete attribute correctly.** A value that is not in the HTML list is ignored by browsers.

SonarQube: S6840

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const i = <input autoComplete="mail" />;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const i = <input autoComplete="email" />;
```

## js/render-zero

**React components should not render non-boolean condition values.** `{items.length && <List />}` renders 0 when the list is empty.

SonarQube: S6439

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const v = <div>{items.length && <List />}</div>;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const v = <div>{items.length > 0 && <List />}</div>;
```

## ts/type-predicate

**Type predicates should be used.** A boolean-returning check does not narrow the type for the caller.

SonarQube: S4322

Noncompliant:

```typescript
function isText(x: unknown): boolean { return typeof x === 'string'; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
function isText(x: unknown): x is string { return typeof x === 'string'; }
```

## ts/readonly-private-field

**Fields that are only assigned in the constructor should be readonly.** readonly states the intent and stops later accidental writes.

SonarQube: S2933

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class A { private name: string; constructor(n: string) { this.name = n; } }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class A { private readonly name: string; constructor(n: string) { this.name = n; } }
```

## ts/optional-and-undefined

**Optional property declarations should not use both ? and undefined.** `a?: string | undefined` says undefined twice.

SonarQube: S4782

Noncompliant:

```typescript
interface I { a?: string | undefined; }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
interface I { a?: string; }
```

## ts/redundant-union-constituent

**Type constituents of unions and intersections should not be redundant.** `string | any` is `any`, and `'a' | string` is `string`.

SonarQube: S6571

Noncompliant:

```typescript
type A = string | any;
```

Compliant:

```typescript
type A = string | number;
```

## ts/enum-mixed-values

**Enum members should not mix value types.** An enum with both numbers and strings has no consistent meaning.

SonarQube: S6583

Noncompliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B = 'b' }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
enum E { A = 1, B = 2 }
```

## js/unused-variable

**Unused local variables and functions should be removed.** A local that is never read is dead code, or a sign that something meant to use it doesn't. Names starting with `_`, and names that only leave a property out of a `...rest` destructuring, are ignored.

SonarQube: S1481

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function total(items) { const tax = 0.2; return items.length; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function total(items) { const tax = 0.2; return items.length * tax; }
function pick({ secret, ...rest }) { return rest; }
function f(_unused) { let _ignored = 1; }
```

## js/unused-import

**Unused imports should be removed.** An import nothing uses adds a dependency and slows loading for nothing. Uses in types and JSX count; `React` counts as used in files with JSX.

SonarQube: S1128

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs';
readFile('a');
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import { readFile } from 'node:fs';
import React from 'react';
readFile('a');
const v = <div />;
```

## js/const-reassigned

**Constants should not be reassigned.** Assigning to a `const` throws a TypeError at run time.

SonarQube: S3500

Noncompliant:

```javascript
const count = 0;
count += 1;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
let count = 0;
count += 1;
const list = [];
list.push(1);
```

## js/redeclared

**Variables and functions should not be redeclared.** Declaring a name twice in the same scope (with `var`, `function` or as a parameter) silently replaces the first one. TypeScript's declaration merging (interfaces, namespaces, enums) is fine.

SonarQube: S2814

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function load(url) { var url = normalize(url); return fetch(url); }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function load(url) { const normalized = normalize(url); return fetch(normalized); }
```

## js/implicit-global

**Variables should be declared before they are assigned.** Assigning to an undeclared name creates a global in sloppy mode (and throws in strict mode and modules), usually from a typo or a forgotten `let`.

SonarQube: S2703

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function count(xs) { total = 0; for (const x of xs) { total += x; } return total; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function count(xs) { let total = 0; for (const x of xs) { total += x; } return total; }
console.log(process.env.HOME);
```

## js/import-reassigned

**Imported names should not be reassigned.** Imports are read-only bindings: assigning to one throws in modules.

SonarQube: S6522

Noncompliant:

```javascript
import { limit } from './config';
limit = 10;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
import { limit } from './config';
let max = limit;
max = 10;
```

## js/unused-parameter

**Unused function parameters should be removed.** A parameter the function never reads is noise for callers. Only trailing ones are reported, since earlier ones hold later ones' positions, and only for functions whose arity callers can't depend on: not methods, callbacks, or functions passed by name (Express, for one, treats four-parameter middleware as error handlers).

Off by default: add `"js/unused-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1172

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function area(width, height, unit) { return width * height; }
area(1, 2);
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function area(width, height) { return width * height; }
function onError(err, req, res, next) { res.status(500).end(); }
app.use(onError);
items.map((item, index) => index);
```

## js/shadowed-variable

**Variables should not shadow outer ones.** A declaration with the same name as one in an enclosing scope hides it, so code meaning the outer one silently gets the inner one.

Off by default: add `"js/shadowed-variable"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1117

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function total(items) { let sum = 0; for (const item of items) { const sum = item.price; } return sum; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function total(items) { let sum = 0; for (const item of items) { sum += item.price; } return sum; }
```

## js/var-outside-block

**Variables should be used in the blocks where they are declared.** A `var` declared in a block is visible in the whole function, but using it outside that block relies on hoisting and is easy to misread. Declare it where it is used, or use `let`.

SonarQube: S2392

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function find(xs, y) { for (var i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) { if (xs[i] === y) break; } return i; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function find(xs, y) { var i; for (i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) { if (xs[i] === y) break; } return i; }
```

## js/prefer-const

**Variables that are never reassigned should be declared with const.** `const` tells readers, and the engine, that a binding never changes. Reported when every variable of a `let` declaration is initialized and never assigned again, so the fix is just the keyword.

Off by default: add `"js/prefer-const"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3353

Noncompliant:

```javascript
let limit = 10;
export const pages = limit * 2;
```

Compliant:

```javascript
const limit = 10;
let count = 0;
count++;
let a = 1, b = 2;
b++;
export const pages = limit * 2 + count + a + b;
```

## js/collection-never-read

**Collection contents should be used.** An array, set or map that is only ever added to costs memory and work for nothing: nothing reads what it holds.

SonarQube: S4030

Noncompliant:

```javascript
function load(rows) { const seen = new Set(); for (const r of rows) { seen.add(r.id); } return rows.length; }
```

Compliant:

```javascript
function load(rows) { const seen = new Set(); for (const r of rows) { seen.add(r.id); } return seen.size; }
```

## js/unused-private-field

**Unused private class fields should be removed.** A private field (`#name`, or TypeScript `private`) can only be used inside its class; if nothing there uses it, it is dead. Decorated fields are left alone, since frameworks use them.

SonarQube: S1068

Noncompliant:

```typescript
class Cache { private hits = 0; #size = 0; get(k: string) { return k; } }
```

Compliant:

```typescript
class Cache { private hits = 0; #size = 0; constructor(private readonly store: Map<string, string>) {}
  get(k: string) { this.hits++; this.#size = this.store.size; return this.store.get(k); } }
```

## py/no-eval

**eval() and exec() should not be used.** eval()/exec() run arbitrary code and are a common injection vector.

Standards: [CWE-95](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/95.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
eval(user_input)
```

Compliant:

```python
ast.literal_eval(user_input)
```

## py/subprocess-shell

**Commands should not be run through a shell.** shell=True passes the command through /bin/sh, enabling command injection.

Standards: [CWE-78](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
subprocess.run(['ls', '-l'])
```

## py/pickle-load

**Untrusted data should not be unpickled.** pickle.load()/loads() can execute arbitrary code embedded in the data.

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
data = pickle.loads(blob)
```

Compliant:

```python
data = json.loads(blob)
```

## py/mutable-default

**Default arguments should not be mutable.** Default values are evaluated once, so a list/dict/set default is shared between calls.

SonarQube: S5717 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(items=[]):
    return items
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(items=None):
    return items or []
```

## py/assert-tuple

**assert should not be called on a tuple.** `assert (cond, msg)` asserts a non-empty tuple, which is always true.

SonarQube: S5905

Noncompliant:

```python
assert (x > 0, 'x must be positive')
```

Compliant:

```python
assert x > 0, 'x must be positive'
```

## py/bare-except

**Bare except clauses should not be used.** `except:` also catches SystemExit and KeyboardInterrupt. Catch a specific exception.

SonarQube: S5754 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
```

## py/none-comparison

**None should be compared with 'is'.** `== None` can be overridden by __eq__; use `is None` / `is not None`.

Noncompliant:

```python
if x == None:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x is None:
    pass
```

## py/sql-built-dynamically

**SQL queries should not be built from strings.** Formatting values into a query lets an attacker change what it does; pass them as parameters.

SonarQube: S2077 (related)

Standards: [CWE-89](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html), [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = %s" % user_id)
```

Compliant:

```python
cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = %s", (user_id,))
```

## py/cookie-not-secure

**Cookies should have the secure flag.** Without `secure`, the cookie is also sent over plain HTTP.

SonarQube: S2092

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), [CWE-315](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/315.html), [CWE-614](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/614.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, secure=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, secure=True)
```

## py/cookie-not-httponly

**Cookies should have the HttpOnly flag.** Without HttpOnly, scripts on the page can read the cookie.

SonarQube: S3330

Standards: [CWE-1004](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1004.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, httponly=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
response.set_cookie('sid', sid, httponly=True)
```

## py/tls-unverified

**Server certificates should be verified.** Turning verification off lets anyone on the network pose as the server.

SonarQube: S4830

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
requests.get(url, verify=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
requests.get(url)
```

## py/hostname-unverified

**Server hostnames should be verified.** Without a hostname check, a valid certificate for any other site is accepted.

SonarQube: S5527

Standards: [CWE-297](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/297.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
ctx.check_hostname = False
```

Compliant:

```python
ctx.check_hostname = True
```

## py/cleartext-url

**Clear-text protocols should not be used.** http, ftp and telnet send data, including credentials, unencrypted. Loopback and private addresses are not reported.

SonarQube: S5332 (related)

Standards: [CWE-200](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/200.html), [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
api = 'http://api.example-shop.io/v1'
```

Compliant:

```python
api = 'https://api.example-shop.io/v1'
local = 'http://localhost:8000'
```

## py/random-secret

**Pseudorandom generators should not produce secrets.** random is predictable; tokens and passwords built from it can be guessed. Use the secrets module.

SonarQube: S2245 (related)

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-330](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/330.html), [CWE-338](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/338.html), [CWE-1241](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1241.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
session_token = random.randint(0, 10**9)
```

Compliant:

```python
session_token = secrets.token_hex(16)
jitter = random.random()
```

## py/weak-hash

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD5 and SHA-1 have known collisions and are unsuitable for security purposes.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
h = hashlib.md5(data)
```

Compliant:

```python
h = hashlib.sha256(data)
```

## py/weak-cipher

**Cipher algorithms should be robust.** DES, RC2, RC4, Blowfish and similar ciphers are broken or have small blocks.

SonarQube: S5547 (related)

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
cipher = DES.new(key, DES.MODE_ECB)
```

Compliant:

```python
cipher = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_GCM)
```

## py/xxe

**XML parsers should not be vulnerable to XXE attacks.** Resolving external entities lets a crafted document read local files or reach internal hosts.

SonarQube: S2755

Standards: [CWE-611](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/611.html), [CWE-827](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/827.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
parser = etree.XMLParser(resolve_entities=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
parser = etree.XMLParser(resolve_entities=False)
```

## py/world-permissions

**File permissions should not be world-accessible.** Mode bits for 'other' let every user on the machine read or change the file.

SonarQube: S2612

Standards: [CWE-266](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/266.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
os.chmod(path, 0o777)
```

Compliant:

```python
os.chmod(path, 0o600)
```

## py/public-tmp

**Temporary files should not be created in publicly writable directories.** Other users can pre-create or swap files in /tmp, so a predictable path can be hijacked.

SonarQube: S5443

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
path = '/tmp/report.csv'
```

Compliant:

```python
path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix='.csv')[1]
```

## py/mktemp

**Insecure temporary file creation methods should not be used.** tempfile.mktemp returns a name without creating the file, which leaves a window for an attacker.

SonarQube: S5445

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
name = tempfile.mktemp()
```

Compliant:

```python
fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp()
```

## py/weak-tls-version

**Weak SSL/TLS protocols should not be used.** SSLv2, SSLv3, TLS 1.0 and TLS 1.1 have known attacks.

SonarQube: S4423

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLSv1)
```

Compliant:

```python
ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_CLIENT)
```

## py/weak-key-size

**Cryptographic keys should be generated with strong parameters.** RSA and DSA keys under 2048 bits can be factored with modest resources.

SonarQube: S4426

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
key = RSA.generate(1024)
```

Compliant:

```python
key = RSA.generate(3072)
```

## py/jwt-unverified

**JWTs should be signed and verified.** Decoding without verification accepts tokens anyone could have forged.

SonarQube: S5659

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
claims = jwt.decode(token, key, verify=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
claims = jwt.decode(token, key, algorithms=['HS256'])
```

## py/autoescape-disabled

**Auto-escaping in HTML template engines should not be disabled.** Without auto-escaping, any variable rendered into a page can inject script.

SonarQube: S5247

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
env = Environment(autoescape=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
env = Environment(autoescape=True)
```

## py/csrf-disabled

**CSRF protections should not be disabled.** Exempting a view, or turning the check off, lets other sites make requests as the signed-in user.

SonarQube: S4502 (related)

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
@csrf_exempt
def pay(request):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def pay(request):
    pass
```

## py/debug-enabled

**Debugging features should not be enabled in production.** Debug mode exposes stack traces and, in Flask, an interactive console.

SonarQube: S4507 (related)

Standards: [CWE-215](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/215.html), [CWE-489](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/489.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A10:2025 Mishandling of Exceptional Conditions, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
app.run(debug=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
app.run()
```

## py/cors-any-origin

**CORS policies should be restricted to trusted origins.** Allowing every origin lets any site read the responses on behalf of a signed-in user.

SonarQube: S5122 (related)

Standards: [CWE-346](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/346.html), [CWE-942](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/942.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
CORS(app, origins='*')
```

Compliant:

```python
CORS(app, origins=['https://app.example.com'])
```

## py/hardcoded-credential

**Credentials should not be hard-coded.** A password or key in source is visible to everyone with the code and stays in its history.

SonarQube: S2068, S6437 (related), S6418 (related)

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
password = 'Tr0ub4dor&3'
```

Compliant:

```python
password = os.environ['DB_PASSWORD']
```

## py/bind-all-interfaces

**Web servers should not bind to all network interfaces.** 0.0.0.0 makes the service reachable from every network the machine is on.

SonarQube: S8392

Standards: [CWE-668](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/668.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
app.run(host='0.0.0.0')
```

Compliant:

```python
app.run(host='127.0.0.1')
```

## py/flask-secret-key

**Flask secret keys should not be disclosed.** A secret key in source lets anyone forge session cookies.

SonarQube: S6779

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
app.secret_key = 'dev-secret-123'
```

Compliant:

```python
app.secret_key = os.environ['SECRET_KEY']
```

## py/jwt-literal-secret

**JWT secret keys should not be disclosed.** A signing key in source lets anyone mint valid tokens.

SonarQube: S6781

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
token = jwt.encode(payload, 'my-secret', algorithm='HS256')
```

Compliant:

```python
token = jwt.encode(payload, SECRET, algorithm='HS256')
```

## py/aws-access-key

**Long-term AWS access keys should not be used directly in code.** An access key ID in source identifies a credential that can be abused if the code leaks.

SonarQube: S7625

Standards: [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
key = 'AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE'
```

Compliant:

```python
key = os.environ['AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID']
```

## py/empty-db-password

**A secure password should be used when connecting to a database.** An empty password means anyone who can reach the database can use it.

SonarQube: S2115

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
conn = psycopg2.connect(host=h, password='')
```

Compliant:

```python
conn = psycopg2.connect(host=h, password=pw)
```

## py/torch-load-unsafe

**Models should not be loaded with torch.load without restricting deserialization.** torch.load unpickles its input, which can run arbitrary code from an untrusted file.

SonarQube: S6985 (related)

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), [CWE-913](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/913.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
model = torch.load(path)
```

Compliant:

```python
model = torch.load(path, weights_only=True)
```

## py/self-assignment

**Variables should not be self-assigned.** Assigning a value to itself does nothing and usually hides a typo.

SonarQube: S1656

Noncompliant:

```python
x = x
```

Compliant:

```python
x = y
```

## py/identical-operands

**Identical expressions should not be used on both sides of a binary operator.** `a == a` is always true and `a - a` always zero: one side is almost certainly a typo.

SonarQube: S1764

Noncompliant:

```python
if a > a:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if a > b:
    pass
```

## py/duplicate-condition

**Related if/elif statements should not have the same condition.** The second branch can never run.

SonarQube: S1862

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
elif a:
    g()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
elif b:
    g()
```

## py/identical-branches

**All branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** If every branch does the same thing, the condition decides nothing.

SonarQube: S3923

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
else:
    f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a:
    f()
else:
    g()
```

## py/single-iteration-loop

**Loops with at most one iteration should be refactored.** A loop whose body always ends in a jump runs its body once.

SonarQube: S1751

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    if ok(x):
        return x
```

## py/unreachable-code

**All code should be reachable.** Statements after a return, raise, break or continue never run.

SonarQube: S1763

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    return 1
    g()
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
    return 1
```

## py/jump-in-finally

**Jump statements should not occur in finally blocks.** A return, break or continue in finally discards any exception that is propagating.

SonarQube: S1143

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    try:
        g()
    finally:
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    try:
        g()
    finally:
        cleanup()
    return 1
```

## py/bare-raise-outside-handler

**Bare raise statements should only be used in except blocks.** With no exception being handled, a bare raise fails with RuntimeError.

SonarQube: S5747

Noncompliant:

```python
raise
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    raise
```

## py/unreachable-except

**All except blocks should be able to catch exceptions.** An except clause after a catch-all one never runs.

SonarQube: S1045

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except Exception:
    pass
except ValueError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
except Exception:
    pass
```

## py/exception-not-raised

**Exceptions should not be created without being raised.** `ValueError('x')` on its own builds an object and drops it.

SonarQube: S3984

Noncompliant:

```python
ValueError('bad')
```

Compliant:

```python
raise ValueError('bad')
```

## py/raise-non-exception

**Raised exceptions must derive from BaseException.** Only exception classes and instances can be raised.

SonarQube: S5632

Noncompliant:

```python
raise 'failed'
```

Compliant:

```python
raise RuntimeError('failed')
```

## py/init-returns-value

**__init__ should not return a value.** A constructor that returns something other than None raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S2734

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.x = 1
```

## py/return-outside-function

**return and yield should not be used outside functions.** They are syntax errors at module or class level.

SonarQube: S2711

Noncompliant:

```python
return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    return 1
```

## py/misleading-operator

**Non-existent operators like =+ should not be used.** `x =+ 1` assigns +1, which is almost never what was meant.

SonarQube: S2757

Noncompliant:

```python
x =+ 1
```

Compliant:

```python
x += 1
```

## py/open-mode

**The open builtin should be called with a valid mode.** An invalid mode string raises ValueError at runtime.

SonarQube: S5828

Noncompliant:

```python
f = open(path, 'rw')
```

Compliant:

```python

```

## py/dict-duplicate-key

**Dictionary literals should not have duplicate keys.** Later values silently replace earlier ones.

SonarQube: S5780

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {'a': 1, 'a': 2}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {'a': 1, 'b': 2}
```

## py/set-duplicate-value

**Set literals should not have duplicate values.** The repeated value does nothing.

SonarQube: S5781

Noncompliant:

```python
s = {1, 2, 1}
```

Compliant:

```python

```

## py/float-equality

**Floating point numbers should not be tested for equality.** Rounding makes two mathematically equal results differ in the last bits.

SonarQube: S1244

Noncompliant:

```python
if total == 0.1:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if math.isclose(total, 0.1):
    pass
```

## py/assert-constant

**Assertions should not succeed unconditionally.** `assert True` checks nothing.

SonarQube: S5914

Noncompliant:

```python
assert True
```

Compliant:

```python
assert ready
```

## py/length-comparison

**Collection size comparisons should make sense.** A length is never negative, so `len(x) < 0` is always false and `len(x) >= 0` always true.

SonarQube: S3981

Noncompliant:

```python
if len(x) < 0:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if len(x) == 0:
    pass
```

## py/numpy-nan-equality

**Equality checks should not be made against numpy.nan.** NaN is not equal to anything, itself included, so the check is always false.

SonarQube: S6725
