Noncompliant:

```python
if x == np.nan:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if np.isnan(x):
    pass
```

## py/isclose-without-abs-tol

**The abs_tol parameter should be provided when comparing to zero.** With the default relative tolerance only, nothing but zero is close to zero.

SonarQube: S6727

Noncompliant:

```python
math.isclose(x, 0)
```

Compliant:

```python
math.isclose(x, 0, abs_tol=1e-9)
```

## py/dict-fromkeys-mutable

**Mutable default values should not be used with dict.fromkeys().** Every key shares the same list or dict.

SonarQube: S8508

Noncompliant:

```python
d = dict.fromkeys(keys, [])
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {k: [] for k in keys}
```

## py/enum-dataclass

**Enum classes should not be decorated with @dataclass.** The decorator generates methods that clash with Enum's.

SonarQube: S8490

Noncompliant:

```python
@dataclass
class Color(Enum):
    RED = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class Color(Enum):
    RED = 1
```

## py/typevar-variance

**TypeVars should not be both covariant and contravariant.** The two are mutually exclusive.

SonarQube: S8515

Noncompliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('T', covariant=True, contravariant=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('T', covariant=True)
```

## py/stopiteration-in-generator

**StopIteration should not be raised inside generators.** Since Python 3.7 it becomes RuntimeError.

SonarQube: S8493

Noncompliant:

```python
def g():
    yield 1
    raise StopIteration
```

Compliant:

```python
def g():
    yield 1
    return
```

## py/property-without-return

**Property methods should have a return statement.** A property that returns nothing always reads as None.

SonarQube: S8504

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def size(self):
        self.x = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def size(self):
        return 1
```

## py/async-blocking-sleep

**Non-blocking sleep functions should be used in asynchronous code.** time.sleep stops the whole event loop.

SonarQube: S7488

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    time.sleep(1)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await asyncio.sleep(1)
```

## py/async-blocking-subprocess

**Async functions should not contain synchronous subprocess calls.** A blocking subprocess call stops the event loop until the process ends.

SonarQube: S7487

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    subprocess.run(['ls'])
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec('ls')
```

## py/async-blocking-http

**Async functions should not contain synchronous HTTP client calls.** requests blocks the event loop while waiting for the response.

SonarQube: S7499

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    requests.get(url)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await client.get(url)
```

## py/async-blocking-open

**Async functions should not contain synchronous file operations.** open() blocks the event loop on slow disks and network mounts.

SonarQube: S7493

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    data = open(p).read()
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    data = await aiofiles_read(p)
```

## py/async-blocking-input

**Async functions should not contain input() calls.** input() waits for the user and blocks everything else.

SonarQube: S7501

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    name = input()
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    name = await ask()
```

## py/async-blocking-os

**Async functions should not contain synchronous OS calls.** os.system and os.popen block until the command finishes.

SonarQube: S7489

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    os.system('ls')
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await run('ls')
```

## py/task-not-saved

**Asyncio tasks should be saved to prevent premature garbage collection.** The event loop keeps only a weak reference to a task, so it can be collected before it finishes.

SonarQube: S7502

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    asyncio.create_task(work())
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    task = asyncio.create_task(work())
```

## py/torch-module-init

**Subclasses of torch.nn.Module should call the initializer.** Without super().__init__(), the module's parameters aren't registered.

SonarQube: S6978

Noncompliant:

```python
class Net(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        self.fc = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class Net(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.fc = 1
```

## py/fastapi-reload-object

**FastAPI applications should be passed as import strings when using reload.** uvicorn can only reload an app it can import by name.

SonarQube: S8397

Noncompliant:

```python
uvicorn.run(app, reload=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
uvicorn.run('main:app', reload=True)
```

## py/flask-header-keyerror

**HTTP headers should be accessed safely.** A missing header makes `request.headers['X']` raise KeyError, and the client sees a 500.

SonarQube: S8371

Noncompliant:

```python
token = request.headers['X-Token']
```

Compliant:

```python
token = request.headers.get('X-Token')
```

## py/doubled-prefix-operator

**Doubled prefix operators not and ~ should not be used.** `not not x` and `~~x` cancel out, or are a clumsy way of casting.

SonarQube: S2761

Noncompliant:

```python
y = not not x
```

Compliant:

```python
y = bool(x)
```

## py/needless-pass

**pass should not be used needlessly.** A block that has other statements doesn't need pass.

SonarQube: S2772

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
```

## py/loop-else-without-break

**Loops without break should not have else clauses.** else on a loop runs when the loop wasn't broken out of, so without a break it always runs.

SonarQube: S2836

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    f(x)
else:
    g()
```

Compliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    if ok(x):
        break
else:
    g()
```

## py/nested-conditional

**Conditional expressions should not be nested.** A ternary inside a ternary is hard to read.

SonarQube: S3358

Noncompliant:

```python
x = a if p else (b if q else c)
```

Compliant:

```python
if p:
    x = a
elif q:
    x = b
else:
    x = c
```

## py/redundant-jump

**Jump statements should not be redundant.** A return at the end of a function, or a continue at the end of a loop, changes nothing.

SonarQube: S3626

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
    return
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    g()
```

## py/empty-function

**Functions and methods should not be empty.** An empty body gives no hint whether it is unfinished or deliberate.

SonarQube: S1186

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    # Deliberately empty: subclasses override.
    pass
```

## py/empty-nested-block

**Nested blocks of code should not be left empty.** An empty if, loop or except hides whether the case was forgotten.

SonarQube: S108

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    log.warning('ignored')
```

## py/generic-exception-raised

**Exception and BaseException should not be raised.** Callers can't catch a generic exception without catching everything.

SonarQube: S112

Noncompliant:

```python
raise Exception('bad')
```

Compliant:

```python
raise ValueError('bad')
```

## py/duplicated-string

**String literals should not be duplicated.** A literal repeated across the file is easy to change in one place and miss in the others.

SonarQube: S1192

Noncompliant:

```python
a = 'connection failed'
b = 'connection failed'
c = 'connection failed'
```

Compliant:

```python
MSG = 'connection failed'
a = MSG
b = MSG
c = MSG
```

## py/inverted-boolean

**Boolean checks should not be inverted.** `not a == b` is clearer as `a != b`.

SonarQube: S1940

Noncompliant:

```python
if not a == b:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if a != b:
    pass
```

## py/redundant-parentheses

**Unnecessary parentheses should be removed.** Parentheses around a whole condition add noise.

SonarQube: S1110

Noncompliant:

```python
if (x > 1):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x > 1:
    pass
```

## py/mergeable-if

**Mergeable if statements should be combined.** An if whose only content is another if can use `and`.

SonarQube: S1066

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    if b:
        f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a and b:
    f()
```

## py/constant-condition

**Constants should not be used as conditions.** The branch is either always or never taken.

SonarQube: S5797

Noncompliant:

```python
if True:
    f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if ready:
    f()
```

## py/statement-without-effect

**Non-empty statements should change control flow or have a side effect.** An expression whose value is thrown away does nothing.

SonarQube: S905

Noncompliant:

```python
x == 1
```

Compliant:

```python
assert x == 1
```

## py/builtin-shadowed

**Builtins should not be shadowed by local variables.** Reusing `list` or `id` as a name hides the builtin for the rest of the scope.

SonarQube: S5806

Noncompliant:

```python
list = [1, 2]
```

Compliant:

```python
items = [1, 2]
```

## py/method-without-self

**Instance and class methods should have a self or cls parameter.** Without it, calling the method on an instance fails.

SonarQube: S5719

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def run():
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
```

## py/implicit-string-concatenation

**Implicit string concatenations should not be confusing.** In a list or call, a missing comma silently joins two strings.

SonarQube: S5799

Noncompliant:

```python
names = ['a' 'b', 'c']
```

Compliant:

```python
names = ['a', 'b', 'c']
```

## py/startswith-slice

**startswith and endswith should be used instead of string slicing.** `s[:3] == 'abc'` hides what it checks and builds a new string.

SonarQube: S6659

Noncompliant:

```python
if s[:3] == 'abc':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if s.startswith('abc'):
    pass
```

## py/type-comparison

**isinstance() should be preferred to direct type comparisons.** `type(x) == T` fails for subclasses.

SonarQube: S6660

Noncompliant:

```python
if type(x) == int:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if isinstance(x, int):
    pass
```

## py/empty-literal

**Literal syntax should be preferred for empty collections.** `[]` and `{}` are faster and clearer than `list()` and `dict()`.

SonarQube: S7498

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list()
```

Compliant:

```python
x = []
```

## py/generator-in-constructor

**Comprehensions should be used instead of constructors around generator expressions.** `list(x for x in y)` builds a generator to throw it away.

SonarQube: S7494

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list(a for a in b)
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [a for a in b]
```

## py/comprehension-copy

**Comprehensions only used to copy should be replaced.** `[x for x in y]` is `list(y)`.

SonarQube: S7500

Noncompliant:

```python
x = [a for a in b]
```

Compliant:

```python
x = list(b)
```

## py/list-in-for

**list() should be avoided when iterating over an iterable.** Copying the iterable first costs memory for nothing, unless the loop changes it.

SonarQube: S7504

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in list(items):
    f(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
for x in items:
    f(x)
```

## py/redundant-collection-call

**Redundant collection functions should be avoided.** `sorted(list(x))` converts twice.

SonarQube: S7508

Noncompliant:

```python
x = sorted(list(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = sorted(y)
```

## py/reversed-sorted

**sorted() should not be passed to reversed().** `sorted(x, reverse=True)` does it in one step.

SonarQube: S7510

Noncompliant:

```python
x = reversed(sorted(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = sorted(y, reverse=True)
```

## py/set-of-sorted

**sorted should not be wrapped directly inside set.** A set has no order, so sorting first is wasted.

SonarQube: S7516

Noncompliant:

```python
x = set(sorted(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = set(y)
```

## py/reversed-wrapper

**Passing a reversed iterable to set(), sorted() or reversed() is pointless.** The order is thrown away or restored.

SonarQube: S7511

Noncompliant:

```python
x = set(reversed(y))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = set(y)
```

## py/dict-keys-iteration

**The .keys() method should not be called when testing or iterating over a dictionary.** A dictionary already tests and iterates its keys.

SonarQube: S8521

Noncompliant:

```python
if k in d.keys():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if k in d:
    pass
```

## py/list-first-element

**list(...)[0] should not be used to get the first element.** It builds a whole list for one item.

SonarQube: S8519

Noncompliant:

```python
first = list(items)[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
first = next(iter(items))
```

## py/sorted-for-extreme

**sorted() should not be used with indexing to find a minimum or maximum.** Sorting is O(n log n); min() and max() are O(n).

SonarQube: S8517

Noncompliant:

```python
lowest = sorted(xs)[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
lowest = min(xs)
```

## py/sum-concatenates-lists

**sum() should not be used with an empty list to concatenate lists.** It copies the growing list on every step.

SonarQube: S8520

Noncompliant:

```python
flat = sum(lists, [])
```

Compliant:

```python
flat = list(itertools.chain.from_iterable(lists))
```

## py/startswith-or

**startswith and endswith calls should use tuples instead of multiple calls.** `s.startswith(('a', 'b'))` replaces two calls.

SonarQube: S8513

Noncompliant:

```python
ok = s.startswith('a') or s.startswith('b')
```

Compliant:

```python
ok = s.startswith(('a', 'b'))
```

## py/loop-variable-reused

**Loop variables should not be reused in nested loops.** The inner loop overwrites the outer variable.

SonarQube: S8510

Noncompliant:

```python
for i in a:
    for i in b:
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
for i in a:
    for j in b:
        pass
```

## py/unittest-specific-assertion

**The most specific unittest assertion should be used.** assertEqual shows both values when it fails; assertTrue(a == b) shows only False.

SonarQube: S5906

Noncompliant:

```python
self.assertTrue(a == b)
```

Compliant:

```python
self.assertEqual(a, b)
```

## py/skip-without-reason

**A reason should be provided when skipping a test.** Without a reason, nobody knows when the skip can go.

SonarQube: S1607

Noncompliant:

```python
@unittest.skip
def test_a():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@unittest.skip('flaky on CI')
def test_a():
    pass
```

## py/numpy-deprecated-alias

**Deprecated NumPy aliases of built-in types should not be used.** np.int, np.float and similar were removed in NumPy 1.24.

SonarQube: S6730

Noncompliant:

```python
x = np.int(3)
```

Compliant:

```python
x = int(3)
```

## py/numpy-randomstate

**numpy.random.Generator should be preferred to RandomState.** Generator has better statistical quality and is faster.

SonarQube: S6711

Noncompliant:

```python
rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
```

Compliant:

```python
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
```

## py/pandas-inplace

**inplace=True should not be used when modifying a Pandas DataFrame.** It often copies anyway, and it blocks method chaining.

SonarQube: S6734

Noncompliant:

```python
df.drop('a', axis=1, inplace=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
df = df.drop('a', axis=1)
```

## py/numpy-where-condition

**np.nonzero should be preferred over np.where with only a condition.** where(cond) is nonzero(cond) under a name that suggests it selects between values.

SonarQube: S6729

Noncompliant:

```python
idx = np.where(mask)
```

Compliant:

```python
idx = np.nonzero(mask)
```

## py/numpy-array-generator

**Passing a list to np.array should be preferred over passing a generator.** np.array can't consume a generator and creates a 0-d object array.

SonarQube: S6714

Noncompliant:

```python
a = np.array(x for x in xs)
```

Compliant:

```python
a = np.array([x for x in xs])
```

## py/pytz-timezone-argument

**pytz.timezone should not be passed to the datetime constructor.** The result is an odd historical offset, not the zone's real one.

SonarQube: S6887

Noncompliant:

```python
d = datetime(2024, 1, 1, tzinfo=pytz.timezone('Europe/Amsterdam'))
```

Compliant:

```python
d = datetime(2024, 1, 1, tzinfo=ZoneInfo('Europe/Amsterdam'))
```

## py/pytz-import

**zoneinfo should be preferred to pytz.** zoneinfo is in the standard library since Python 3.9.

SonarQube: S6890

Noncompliant:

```python
import pytz
```

Compliant:

```python
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
```

## py/naive-utc-datetime

**Timezone-aware datetime objects should be preferred.** utcnow() returns a naive datetime that looks like local time.

SonarQube: S6903

Noncompliant:

```python
now = datetime.utcnow()
```

Compliant:

```python
now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
```

## py/typing-union

**Union type expressions should be preferred over typing.Union.** `int | str` is shorter and needs no import.

SonarQube: S6546

Noncompliant:

```python
x: Union[int, str] = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
x: int | str = 1
```

## py/django-null-string-field

**null=True should not be used on string-based fields.** Strings then have two empty values, '' and NULL.

SonarQube: S6553

Noncompliant:

```python
name = models.CharField(max_length=10, null=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
name = models.CharField(max_length=10)
```

## py/django-locals-render

**locals() should not be passed to a Django render() function.** It exposes every local variable to the template.

SonarQube: S6556

Noncompliant:

```python
render(request, 'a.html', locals())
```

Compliant:

```python
render(request, 'a.html', {'a': a})
```

## py/django-modelform-all

**Fields of a Django ModelForm should be defined explicitly.** '__all__' exposes any field added to the model later.

SonarQube: S6559

Noncompliant:

```python
fields = '__all__'
```

Compliant:

```python
fields = ['name', 'email']
```

## py/typevar-name

**TypeVar names should match their assigned variable.** A mismatch confuses readers and type checkers.

SonarQube: S8507

Noncompliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('U')
```

Compliant:

```python
T = TypeVar('T')
```

## py/duplicate-base-class

**Classes should not inherit from the same base class multiple times.** The second listing raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S8509

Noncompliant:

```python
class A(B, B):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A(B, C):
    pass
```

## py/duplicate-class-field

**Class fields should not be defined multiple times.** Only the last assignment survives.

SonarQube: S8512

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    x = 1
    x = 2
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    x = 1
    y = 2
```

## py/torch-variable

**torch.tensor should be used instead of torch.autograd.Variable.** Variable has been merged into Tensor since PyTorch 0.4.

SonarQube: S6979

Noncompliant:

```python
v = torch.autograd.Variable(t)
```

Compliant:

```python
v = torch.tensor(t)
```

## py/dataloader-workers

**The num_workers parameter should be specified for DataLoader.** The default loads data on the main process.

SonarQube: S6983

Noncompliant:

```python
loader = DataLoader(ds)
```

Compliant:

```python
loader = DataLoader(ds, num_workers=4)
```

## py/aws-region-hardcoded

**AWS region should not be set with a hardcoded string.** A literal region makes the code awkward to deploy elsewhere.

SonarQube: S6262

Noncompliant:

```python
s3 = boto3.client('s3', region_name='eu-west-1')
```

Compliant:

```python
s3 = boto3.client('s3')
```

## py/re-sub-literal

**str.replace should be preferred to re.sub.** When the pattern has no special characters, replace() is simpler and faster.

SonarQube: S5361

Noncompliant:

```python
s = re.sub('foo', 'bar', t)
```

Compliant:

```python
s = t.replace('foo', 'bar')
```

## py/defaultdict-keyword

**defaultdict should not be initialized with default_factory as a keyword.** defaultdict takes the factory as its first positional argument.

SonarQube: S7507

Noncompliant:

```python
d = defaultdict(default_factory=list)
```

Compliant:

```python
d = defaultdict(list)
```

## py/dict-comprehension-static-key

**Dictionary comprehensions should not use a static key.** Every iteration overwrites the same entry.

SonarQube: S7506

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {'k': v for v in vs}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {v: 1 for v in vs}
```

## py/dict-fromkeys-comprehension

**Populating a dictionary with a constant value should be done with dict.fromkeys.** A comprehension that maps every key to the same constant is fromkeys.

SonarQube: S7519

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {k: 0 for k in keys}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = dict.fromkeys(keys, 0)
```

## py/map-filter-lambda

**Generators and comprehensions should be preferred over map and filter with a lambda.** A comprehension says the same without the lambda.

SonarQube: S7505

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list(map(lambda a: a + 1, xs))
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [a + 1 for a in xs]
```

## py/any-all-list

**List comprehensions should not be used with any() and all().** A list is built completely before any() can stop at the first hit.

SonarQube: S7492

Noncompliant:

```python
ok = any([x > 1 for x in xs])
```

Compliant:

```python
ok = any(x > 1 for x in xs)
```

## py/literal-in-constructor

**Creation of collections with literals or comprehensions should not be wrapped.** `list([...])` copies a list it just made.

SonarQube: S7496

Noncompliant:

```python
x = list([a for a in b])
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [a for a in b]
```

## py/empty-collection-membership

**Membership tests should not use empty collections.** Nothing is a member of an empty collection.

SonarQube: S8503

Noncompliant:

```python
if x in []:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x in allowed:
    pass
```

## py/logging-error-in-except

**logging.exception() should be used instead of logging.error() in exception handlers.** exception() logs the traceback too.

SonarQube: S8572

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    logger.error('failed')
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    logger.exception('failed')
```

## py/python2-print

**The print statement should not be used.** It is Python 2 syntax.

SonarQube: PrintStatementUsage

Noncompliant:

```python
print 'hello'
```

Compliant:

```python
print('hello')
```

## py/python2-exec

**The exec statement should not be used.** It is Python 2 syntax, and exec runs arbitrary code.

SonarQube: ExecStatementUsage

Noncompliant:

```python
exec 'x = 1'
```

Compliant:

```python
exec('x = 1')
```

## py/python2-inequality

**<> should not be used to test inequality.** It is Python 2 syntax.

SonarQube: InequalityUsage

Noncompliant:

```python
if a <> b:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if a != b:
    pass
```

## py/except-only-reraises

**except clauses should do more than re-raise.** An except that only raises again changes nothing, unless it shields a later clause.

SonarQube: S2737

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    raise
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    log()
    raise
```

## py/all-not-strings

**Only strings should be listed in __all__.** Any other entry makes `from m import *` fail.

SonarQube: S2823

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    pass
__all__ = [f]
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    pass
__all__ = ['f']
```

## py/iter-returns-non-iterator

**__iter__ should return an iterator.** Returning a list or other container makes iter() raise TypeError.

SonarQube: S2876

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __iter__(self):
        return [1, 2]
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __iter__(self):
        return iter([1, 2])
```

## py/is-with-literal

**Identity comparisons should not be used with cached types.** `x is 'a'` and `x is 1` depend on interning, which is an implementation detail.

SonarQube: S5795

Noncompliant:

```python
if x is 'a':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x == 'a':
    pass
```

## py/is-with-new-object

**New objects should not be created only to check their identity.** `x is []` is always False: the literal is a fresh object.

SonarQube: S5796

Noncompliant:

```python
if x is []:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if not x:
    pass
```

## py/identity-dissimilar-types

**Identity operators should not be used with dissimilar types.** Operands of different kinds never share an identity.

SonarQube: S3403

Noncompliant:

```python
if 1 is 'a':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x is None:
    pass
```

## py/inconsistent-return

**Functions should use return consistently.** Some paths return a value and others a bare return, so callers get None unexpectedly.

SonarQube: S3801

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1
    return
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1
    return 0
```

## py/return-in-generator

**return with a value should not be used in an async generator.** An async generator cannot return a value: it is a SyntaxError.

SonarQube: S2712

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    yield 1
    return 2
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    yield 1
    return
```

## py/collection-replaced

**Collection content should not be replaced unconditionally.** The first assignment to the same key is overwritten before it is read.

SonarQube: S4143

Noncompliant:

```python
d['k'] = 1
d['k'] = 2
```

Compliant:

```python
d['k'] = 2
```

## py/dead-store

**Unused assignments should be removed.** The value assigned first is overwritten before anything reads it.

SonarQube: S1854

Noncompliant:

```python
x = compute()
x = other()
```

Compliant:

```python
x = other()
```

## py/exception-cause-literal

**Exception causes must be exceptions or None.** `raise A from 'text'` fails with TypeError.

SonarQube: S5707

Noncompliant:

```python
raise ValueError('x') from 'reason'
```

Compliant:

```python
raise ValueError('x') from err
```

## py/except-boolean-expression

**Boolean expressions of exceptions should not be used in except statements.** `except A or B` catches only A: or evaluates to its first operand.

SonarQube: S5714

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError or TypeError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except (ValueError, TypeError):
    pass
```

## py/bare-raise-in-finally

**Bare raise statements should not be used in finally blocks.** Outside a handler a bare raise fails, and after one it hides the real error.

SonarQube: S5704

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
finally:
    raise
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
finally:
    cleanup()
```

## py/exit-reraises

**__exit__ should not re-raise the exception it receives.** Returning a falsy value already lets the exception propagate.

SonarQube: S5706

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __exit__(self, t, v, tb):
        raise v
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __exit__(self, t, v, tb):
        return False
```

## py/exception-base-class

**Custom exception classes should inherit from Exception.** A class with no base cannot be raised or caught.

SonarQube: S5709

Noncompliant:

```python
class AppError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class AppError(Exception):
    pass
```

## py/not-implemented-error

**Special methods should return NotImplemented, not raise NotImplementedError.** Operators fall back to the other operand only when NotImplemented is returned.

SonarQube: S5712

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __eq__(self, o):
        raise NotImplementedError
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __eq__(self, o):
        return NotImplemented
```

## py/special-method-arity

**Special methods should have an expected number of parameters.** Python calls them with a fixed signature.

SonarQube: S5722

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self, extra):
        return 0
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self):
        return 0
```

## py/property-arity

**Property getter, setter and deleter methods should have the expected number of parameters.** A getter takes self, a setter self and the value.

SonarQube: S5724

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def x(self, y):
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @property
    def x(self):
        return 1
```

## py/call-non-callable

**Calls should not be made to non-callable values.** Calling a literal raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S5756

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 'abc'()
```

Compliant:

```python
x = 'abc'
```

## py/in-on-non-container

**in and not in should be used on objects supporting them.** A number or None has no members to test.

SonarQube: S5642

Noncompliant:

```python
if x in 5:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x in (5,):
    pass
```

## py/subscript-on-scalar

**Item operations should be done on objects supporting them.** Indexing a number or None raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S5644

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 5[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
x = [5][0]
```

## py/iterate-scalar

**Iteration should use an Iterable object.** Looping over a number or None raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S3862

Noncompliant:

```python
for i in 5:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
for i in range(5):
    pass
```

## py/incompatible-operands

**Operators should be used on compatible types.** Adding a string and a number, or doing arithmetic on None, raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S5607

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 'a' + 1
```

Compliant:

```python
x = 'a' + str(1)
```

## py/unhashable-member

**Set members and dictionary keys should be hashable.** Lists, dicts and sets raise TypeError as keys.

SonarQube: S6662

Noncompliant:

```python
d = {[1]: 'a'}
```

Compliant:

```python
d = {(1,): 'a'}
```

## py/float-index

**Sequence indexes must have an __index__ method.** A float cannot index a sequence.

SonarQube: S6663

Noncompliant:

```python
x = items[1.5]
```

Compliant:

```python
x = items[1]
```

## py/string-format-count

**String formatting should not lead to runtime errors.** Fewer arguments than placeholders raises TypeError or IndexError.

SonarQube: S2275

Noncompliant:

```python
msg = '%s and %s' % (a,)
```

Compliant:

```python
msg = '%s and %s' % (a, b)
```

## py/fstring-without-fields

**String formatting should be used correctly.** An f-string with no field is just a string.

SonarQube: S3457

Noncompliant:

```python
msg = f'hello'
```

Compliant:

```python
msg = 'hello'
```

## py/infinite-recursion

**Recursion should not be infinite.** A function that calls itself with no branch can never stop.

SonarQube: S2190

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(n):
    return f(n - 1)
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(n):
    if n == 0:
        return 0
    return f(n - 1)
```

## py/overwritten-parameter

**Function parameters initial values should not be ignored.** The first thing the function does is replace the argument it was given.

SonarQube: S1226

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    x = 1
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    y = x + 1
    return y
```

## py/private-method-unused

**Unused class-private methods should be removed.** Nothing in the class calls it, and name mangling hides it from outside.

SonarQube: S1144

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __hidden(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __hidden(self):
        pass
    def run(self):
        self.__hidden()
```

## py/private-attribute-unread

**Unread private attributes should be removed.** The value is written and never read back.

SonarQube: S4487

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.__x = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.__x = 1
    def x(self):
        return self.__x
```

## py/identical-functions

**Functions and methods should not have identical implementations.** Two copies of a body drift apart.

SonarQube: S4144

Noncompliant:

```python
def a():
    x = 1
    return x
def b():
    x = 1
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
def a():
    x = 1
    return x
def b():
    return a()
```

## py/duplicated-branch

**Two branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** Merge the conditions instead of repeating the body.

SonarQube: S1871

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    x = 1
    y = 2
elif b:
    x = 1
    y = 2
else:
    z = 3
```

Compliant:

```python
if a or b:
    x = 1
    y = 2
else:
    z = 3
```

## py/loop-variable-closure

**Functions and lambdas should not reference variables defined in enclosing loops.** The closure sees the variable's final value, not the one from its iteration.

SonarQube: S1515

Noncompliant:

```python
fs = []
for i in r:
    fs.append(lambda: i)
```

Compliant:

```python
fs = []
for i in r:
    fs.append(lambda i=i: i)
```

## py/class-name

**Class names should comply with a naming convention.** PascalCase makes classes recognisable.

SonarQube: S101

Noncompliant:

```python
class my_class:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class MyClass:
    pass
```

## py/method-name

**Method names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S100

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def doThing(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def do_thing(self):
        pass
```

## py/function-name

**Function names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S1542

Noncompliant:

```python
def doThing():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def do_thing():
    pass
```

## py/field-name

**Field names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S116

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.myField = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __init__(self):
        self.my_field = 1
```

## py/local-name

**Local variable and function parameter names should comply with a naming convention.** snake_case is the Python convention.

SonarQube: S117

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(myArg):
    return myArg
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(my_arg):
    return my_arg
```

## py/self-parameter-name

**self should be the first argument to instance methods.** Another name confuses readers and tools.

SonarQube: S5720

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(this):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
```

## py/cls-parameter-name

**The first argument to class methods should follow the naming convention.** Use cls.

SonarQube: S2710

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @classmethod
    def make(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @classmethod
    def make(cls):
        pass
```

## py/wildcard-import

**Wildcard imports should not be used.** They hide where names come from and can shadow each other.

SonarQube: S2208

Noncompliant:

```python
from os import *
```

Compliant:

```python
from os import path
```

## py/unused-import

**Unnecessary imports should be removed.** An import nothing uses only slows start-up.

SonarQube: S1128

Noncompliant:

```python
import os
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
print(os.name)
```

## py/unused-local

**Unused local variables should be removed.** A variable that is never read is dead code or a typo.

SonarQube: S1481

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    x = compute()
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f():
    x = compute()
    return x
```

## py/unused-parameter

**Unused function parameters should be removed.** Callers pass a value that is ignored.

SonarQube: S1172

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(a, b):
    return a
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(a):
    return a
```

## py/too-many-returns

**Functions should not contain too many return statements.** Many exits make the flow hard to follow.

SonarQube: S1142

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x == 1:
        return 1
    if x == 2:
        return 2
    if x == 3:
        return 3
    return 4
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    return x
```

## py/nesting-depth

**Control flow statements should not be nested too deeply.** Deep nesting hides the main path.

SonarQube: S134

Noncompliant:

```python
if a:
    if b:
        if c:
            if d:
                if e:
                    f()
```

Compliant:

```python
if a and b:
    f()
```

## py/function-complexity

**Cyclomatic Complexity of functions should not be too high.** Every branch is another path to test.

SonarQube: FunctionComplexity

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(a, b, c, d, e, g, h, i, j, k):
    if a: pass
    if b: pass
    if c: pass
    if d: pass
    if e: pass
    if g: pass
    if h: pass
    if i: pass
    if j: pass
    if k: pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(a):
    if a:
        return 1
    return 0
```

## py/hardcoded-ip

**IP addresses should not be hardcoded.** A fixed address has to be changed in the code when the network moves.

SonarQube: S1313

Noncompliant:

```python
host = '192.168.1.10'
```

Compliant:

```python
host = os.environ['HOST']
```

## py/noqa-comment

**Uses of noqa comments should be tracked.** Each one silences a check.

SonarQube: S1309

Noncompliant:

```python
import os  # noqa
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
```

## py/trailing-comment

**Comments should not be located at the end of lines of code.** They push lines past the screen edge and are easy to miss.

Off by default: add `"py/trailing-comment"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S139

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 1  # the count
```

Compliant:

```python
# the count
x = 1
```

## py/semicolon-statements

**Statements should be on separate lines.** Several statements on one line are easy to misread.

SonarQube: OneStatementPerLine

Noncompliant:

```python
a = 1; b = 2
```

Compliant:

```python
a = 1
b = 2
```

## py/increment-operator

**Increment and decrement operators should not be used.** `++x` is two unary plus signs and does nothing.

SonarQube: PreIncrementDecrement

Noncompliant:

```python
x = 1
++x
```

Compliant:

```python
x = 1
x += 1
```

## py/lambda-assigned

**Assignments of lambdas to variables should be replaced by function definitions.** A def has a name in tracebacks and can carry a docstring.

SonarQube: S6661

Noncompliant:

```python
f = lambda x: x + 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    return x + 1
```

## py/hint-any

**Any should not be used as a type hint.** It switches off type checking for that value.

SonarQube: S6542

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x: Any):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: int):
    pass
```

## py/hint-bare-generic

**Type hints of generic types should specify their type parameters.** A bare list or dict says nothing about its contents.

SonarQube: S6543

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x: list):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: list[int]):
    pass
```

## py/hint-typing-alias

**Built-in generic types should be preferred over the typing module in type hints.** List[int] is list[int] since Python 3.9.

SonarQube: S6545

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x: List[int]):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: list[int]):
    pass
```

## py/nested-fstring

**f-strings should not be nested too deeply.** Three levels of quotes and braces are unreadable.

SonarQube: S6799

Noncompliant:

```python
s = f"{f'{f"{x}"}'}"
```

Compliant:

```python
inner = f'{x}'
s = f'{inner}'
```

## py/dataclass-annotation

**Dataclass attributes should use type annotations.** Without an annotation the attribute is a class variable, not a field.

SonarQube: S8514

Noncompliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x: int = 1
```

## py/dataclass-mutable-default

**Mutable values should not be used as default values in dataclass attributes.** dataclass rejects list, dict and set defaults at class creation.

SonarQube: S8685

Noncompliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x: list = []
```

Compliant:

```python
@dataclass
class A:
    x: list = field(default_factory=list)
```

## py/pydantic-optional-default

**Optional Pydantic fields should have explicit default values.** In Pydantic v2 an Optional field without a default is still required.

SonarQube: S8396

Noncompliant:

```python
class M(BaseModel):
    x: Optional[int]
```

Compliant:

```python
class M(BaseModel):
    x: Optional[int] = None
```

## py/django-model-str

**Django models should define a __str__ method.** Without it the admin and shell show 'Model object (1)'.

SonarQube: S6554

Noncompliant:

```python
class P(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField()
```

Compliant:

```python
class P(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField()
    def __str__(self):
        return self.name
```

## py/decorator-order

**The @receiver (Django) and @route (Flask) decorators should be the outermost decorators.** Decorators applied above them never reach the registered function.

SonarQube: S6552

Noncompliant:

```python
@login_required
@app.route('/')
def home():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.route('/')
@login_required
def home():
    pass
```

## py/fastapi-path-parameter

**FastAPI path parameters should be included in route function signatures.** FastAPI fails to pass the value, and the endpoint returns 422.

SonarQube: S8411

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/items/{item_id}')
def read():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/items/{item_id}')
def read(item_id):
    pass
```

## py/fastapi-annotated

**FastAPI dependencies should use Annotated type hints.** Annotated keeps the default free for a real default and works with type checkers.

SonarQube: S8410

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(db=Depends(get_db)):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(db: Annotated[Db, Depends(get_db)]):
    pass
```

## py/bs4-parser

**BeautifulSoup parser should be explicitly specified.** Without one the result depends on which parsers are installed.

SonarQube: S8905

Noncompliant:

```python
soup = BeautifulSoup(html)
```

Compliant:

```python
soup = BeautifulSoup(html, 'html.parser')
```

## py/bs4-deprecated

**Deprecated Beautiful Soup methods should be replaced with their modern equivalents.** The camelCase names were renamed in Beautiful Soup 4.

SonarQube: S8900

Noncompliant:

```python
links = soup.findAll('a')
```

Compliant:

```python
links = soup.find_all('a')
```

## py/logging-formatted-message

**Logging statements should follow Python best practices.** Formatting the message up front costs time even when the level is off.

SonarQube: S8554

Noncompliant:

```python
logger.info(f'user {name}')
```

Compliant:

```python
logger.info('user %s', name)
```

## py/test-fail-in-try

**Dedicated exception assertions should be used instead of try-except with fail().** assertRaises states the intent and reports it better.

SonarQube: S8714

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
    self.fail('no error')
except ValueError:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
    f()
```

## py/raises-multiple-statements

**Only one method invocation is expected when testing runtime exceptions.** Any other statement in the block could be the one that raises.

SonarQube: S5778

Noncompliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    a = make()
    a.run()
```

Compliant:

```python
a = make()
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    a.run()
```

## py/assert-in-raises

**Assertions should not be made at the end of blocks expecting an exception.** Code after the raising call never runs.

SonarQube: S5915

Noncompliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    run()
    assert flag
```

Compliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    run()
assert flag
```

## py/raises-too-generic

**Tests should check which exception is thrown.** Exception matches every failure, including test bugs.

SonarQube: S5958

Noncompliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(Exception):
    run()
```

Compliant:

```python
with pytest.raises(ValueError):
    run()
```

## py/async-timeout-parameter

**Asynchronous functions should not accept timeout parameters.** Callers can cancel with the event loop's own timeouts.

SonarQube: S7483

Noncompliant:

```python
async def fetch(url, timeout=5):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
async def fetch(url):
    pass
```

## py/async-without-await

**Async functions should use async features.** Without await, async for or async with there is nothing asynchronous.

SonarQube: S7503

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    return await g()
```

## py/checkpoint-sleep-zero

**Checkpoints should be used instead of sleep(0).** trio and anyio have a dedicated checkpoint call.

SonarQube: S7491

Noncompliant:

```python
await trio.sleep(0)
```

Compliant:

```python
await trio.lowlevel.checkpoint()
```

## py/items-unused-half

**Using .items() to iterate over a dictionary should be avoided if possible.** If the key or the value is never used, iterate the keys or values.

SonarQube: S7512

Noncompliant:

```python
for k, v in d.items():
    print(k)
```

Compliant:

```python
for k in d:
    print(k)
```

## py/dict-lookup-in-loop

**Iteration over a dictionary key value pairs should be done with the items() method call.** Looking each key up again costs a hash per iteration.

SonarQube: S7517

Noncompliant:

```python
for k in d:
    print(d[k])
```

Compliant:

```python
for k, v in d.items():
    print(v)
```

## py/enumerate-index-lookup

**enumerate() values should be unpacked instead of using index lookups.** The loop already has the element.

SonarQube: S8518

Noncompliant:

```python
for i, x in enumerate(items):
    print(items[i])
```

Compliant:

```python
for i, x in enumerate(items):
    print(x)
```

## py/set-discard

**set.discard() should be used instead of checking membership before removal.** discard does not raise when the item is absent.

SonarQube: S8492

Noncompliant:

```python
if x in s:
    s.remove(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
s.discard(x)
```

## py/set-update-loop

**set.update() should be used instead of a for-loop with add().** One call replaces the loop.

SonarQube: S8502

Noncompliant:

```python
for x in xs:
    s.add(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
s.update(xs)
```

## py/datetime-range

**Constructor attributes of date and time objects should be in the range of possible values.** Month 13 or day 32 raises ValueError.

SonarQube: S6882

Noncompliant:

```python
d = datetime(2020, 13, 1)
```

Compliant:

```python
d = datetime(2020, 12, 1)
```

## py/pandas-merge-args

**When using pandas.merge or pandas.join, the parameters on, how and validate should be provided.** The defaults hide intent and let duplicate keys through.

SonarQube: S6735

Noncompliant:

```python
out = pd.merge(a, b)
```

Compliant:

```python
out = pd.merge(a, b, on='k', how='inner', validate='one_to_one')
```

## py/read-csv-dtype

**dtype parameter should be provided when using pandas.read_csv or pandas.read_table.** Guessing types per chunk is slow and inconsistent.

SonarQube: S6740

Noncompliant:

```python
df = pd.read_csv('a.csv')
```

Compliant:

```python
df = pd.read_csv('a.csv', dtype={'id': 'int64'})
```

## py/dataframe-values

**The DataFrame.to_numpy() method should be preferred to the DataFrame.values attribute.** values has no defined result type for mixed columns.

SonarQube: S6741

Noncompliant:

```python
a = df.values
```

Compliant:

```python
a = df.to_numpy()
```

## py/ecb-mode

**Encryption algorithms should be used with secure mode and padding scheme.** ECB encrypts equal blocks to equal ciphertext and leaks structure.

SonarQube: S5542

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_ECB)
```

Compliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_GCM, nonce=n)
```

## py/static-iv

**Cipher Block Chaining IVs should be unpredictable.** A fixed IV makes equal plaintexts encrypt to equal ciphertexts.

SonarQube: S3329

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, b'0123456789abcdef')
```

Compliant:

```python
c = AES.new(key, AES.MODE_CBC, os.urandom(16))
```

## py/static-salt

**Password hashing functions should use an unpredictable salt.** A fixed salt lets one precomputed table crack every password.

SonarQube: S2053

Standards: [CWE-759](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/759.html), [CWE-760](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/760.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
h = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', pw, b'salt', 100000)
```

Compliant:

```python
h = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', pw, os.urandom(16), 100000)
```

## py/ldap-anonymous-bind

**LDAP connections should be authenticated.** An anonymous bind lets anyone read the directory.

SonarQube: S4433

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
conn.simple_bind_s()
```

Compliant:

```python
conn.simple_bind_s(dn, secret)
```

## py/password-fast-hash

**Passwords should not be stored in plaintext or with a fast hashing algorithm.** MD5 and SHA-2 are cheap to brute-force.

SonarQube: S5344

Standards: [CWE-256](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/256.html), [CWE-916](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/916.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
h = hashlib.sha256(password.encode()).hexdigest()
```

Compliant:

```python
h = bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode(), bcrypt.gensalt())
```

## py/jump-outside-loop

**break and continue should not be used outside a loop.** Outside a loop they are a SyntaxError, and in a loop's else clause they belong to the enclosing loop.

SonarQube: S1716

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    break
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(xs):
    for x in xs:
        break
```

## py/silly-equality

**Unnecessary equality checks should not be made.** Values of different kinds are never equal, so the comparison has a fixed result.

SonarQube: S2159

Noncompliant:

```python
if 1 == 'a':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x == 'a':
    pass
```

## py/ignored-pure-call

**Return values from functions without side effects should not be ignored.** Calling len() or sorted() and dropping the result does nothing.

SonarQube: S2201

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(xs):
    sorted(xs)
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(xs):
    return sorted(xs)
```

## py/method-could-be-static

**Methods and properties that don't access instance data should be static.** A method that never uses self does not need an instance.

Off by default: add `"py/method-could-be-static"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2325

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def double(self, x):
        return x * 2
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @staticmethod
    def double(x):
        return x * 2
```

## py/invariant-return

**Functions returns should not be invariant.** If every return gives the same constant, the return value carries no information.

SonarQube: S3516

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 0
    return 0
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 0
    return 1
```

## py/result-of-no-return-function

**The output of functions that don't return anything should not be used.** The function always yields None.

SonarQube: S3699

Noncompliant:

```python
def log(x):
    print(x)
r = log(1)
```

Compliant:

```python
def double(x):
    return x * 2
r = double(1)
```

## py/route-mixed-methods

**HTTP routes should restrict allowed HTTP methods.** One route that serves GET and a state-changing verb makes CSRF and caching mistakes easy.

SonarQube: S3752

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def x():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['GET'])
def x():
    pass
```

## py/undefined-name

**Variables, classes and functions should be either defined or imported.** Using a name nothing defines raises NameError.

SonarQube: S5953 (related)

Noncompliant:

```python
print(missing_name)
```

Compliant:

```python
print(len('a'))
```

## py/private-class-unused

**Unused private nested classes should be removed.** Nothing in the class uses it, and name mangling hides it from outside.

SonarQube: S3985

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    class __Hidden:
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    class __Hidden:
        pass
    def make(self):
        return self.__Hidden()
```

## py/nested-definition-unused

**Unused scope-limited definitions should be removed.** A function or class defined inside another and never used is dead code.

SonarQube: S5603

Noncompliant:

```python
def outer():
    def helper():
        pass
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def outer():
    def helper():
        pass
    return helper()
```

## py/walrus-as-statement

**Walrus operator should not make code confusing.** As a statement, := is just a harder-to-read assignment.

SonarQube: S5685

Noncompliant:

```python
(x := f())
```

Compliant:

```python
x = f()
```

## py/except-non-exception

**Caught Exceptions must derive from BaseException.** Catching a string or number raises TypeError once an exception is raised.

SonarQube: S5708

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except 'error':
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except ValueError:
    pass
```

## py/except-parent-and-child

**A subclass should not be in the same except statement as a parent class.** The parent already catches the subclass.

SonarQube: S5713

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except (Exception, ValueError):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    f()
except (ValueError, KeyError):
    pass
```

## py/none-comparison-constant

**Comparison to None should not be constant.** The result never changes.

SonarQube: S5727

Noncompliant:

```python
if [] is None:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if x is None:
    pass
```

## py/all-undefined-name

**Only defined names should be listed in __all__.** An undefined entry makes `from m import *` fail.

SonarQube: S5807

Noncompliant:

```python
__all__ = ['Missing']
```

Compliant:

```python
class Missing:
    pass
__all__ = ['Missing']
```

## py/assert-incompatible-types

**Assertions comparing incompatible types should not be made.** Values of different kinds are never equal.

SonarQube: S5845

Noncompliant:

```python
self.assertEqual(1, 'a')
```

Compliant:

```python
self.assertEqual(1, x)
```

## py/confusing-type-check

**Type checks shouldn't be confusing.** isinstance needs a class, and a type compared with a string is never equal.

SonarQube: S5864

Noncompliant:

```python
if isinstance(x, 'str'):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
if isinstance(x, str):
    pass
```

## py/return-type-mismatch

**Function return types should be consistent with their type hint.** The literal returned is not what the signature promises.

SonarQube: S5886

Noncompliant:

```python
def f() -> int:
    return 'a'
```

Compliant:

```python
def f() -> int:
    return 1
```

## py/annotation-mismatch

**Values assigned to variables should match their type annotations.** The literal does not fit the annotation.

SonarQube: S5890

Noncompliant:

```python
x: int = 'a'
```

Compliant:

```python
x: int = 1
```

## py/test-name-case

**Test methods should be discoverable.** unittest only collects methods that start with a lowercase test.

SonarQube: S5899

Noncompliant:

```python
import unittest
class T(unittest.TestCase):
    def Test_a(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
import unittest
class T(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a(self):
        pass
```

## py/test-returns-early

**Tests should be skipped explicitly.** A bare return makes a test pass without checking anything.

SonarQube: S5918

Noncompliant:

```python
def test_a():
    return
    assert False
```

Compliant:

```python
import unittest
@unittest.skip('not ready')
def test_a():
    assert False
```

## py/field-named-after-class

**A field should not duplicate the name of its containing class.** It reads like a constructor call.

SonarQube: S1700

Noncompliant:

```python
class Point:
    point = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class Point:
    origin = 1
```

## py/names-differ-by-case

**Methods and field names should not differ only by capitalization.** They are easy to confuse.

SonarQube: S1845

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
    def Run(self):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def run(self):
        pass
    def stop(self):
        pass
```

## py/jsonresponse-unsafe

**The safe flag should be set to False when serializing non-dictionary objects.** JsonResponse raises TypeError for a list unless safe=False.

SonarQube: S6560

Noncompliant:

```python
return JsonResponse([1, 2])
```

Compliant:

```python
return JsonResponse([1, 2], safe=False)
```

## py/random-state-missing

**Results that depend on random number generation should be reproducible.** Without a seed, every run gives different results.

SonarQube: S6709

Noncompliant:

```python
X_train, X_test = train_test_split(X)
```

Compliant:

```python
X_train, X_test = train_test_split(X, random_state=0)
```

## py/pandas-long-chain

**pandas.pipe method should be preferred over long chains of instructions.** Long chains hide intermediate steps.

SonarQube: S6742

Noncompliant:

```python
out = df.a().b().c().d().e().f()
```

Compliant:

```python
out = df.pipe(step_one).pipe(step_two)
```

## py/generic-class-syntax

**Generic classes should be defined using the type parameter syntax.** Python 3.12 declares type parameters on the class.

SonarQube: S6792

Noncompliant:

```python
class A(Generic[T]):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A[T]:
    pass
```

## py/type-alias-annotation

**Type aliases should be declared with a type statement.** Python 3.12 has a dedicated statement.

SonarQube: S6794

Noncompliant:

```python
X: TypeAlias = int
```

Compliant:

```python
type X = int
```

## py/error-handler-without-status

**Flask error handlers should set HTTP status code.** Returning only a body sends status 200 for an error.

SonarQube: S6863

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.errorhandler(404)
def missing(e):
    return 'not found'
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.errorhandler(404)
def missing(e):
    return 'not found', 404
```

## py/time-format-mix

**The 12-hour format should be used with the AM/PM marker.** %I without %p is ambiguous, and %p with %H is ignored.

SonarQube: S6883

Noncompliant:

```python
s = now.strftime('%I:%M')
```

Compliant:

```python
s = now.strftime('%I:%M %p')
```

## py/weekmask-invalid

**Numpy weekmask should have a valid value.** It must be seven 0/1 flags, or day abbreviations.

SonarQube: S6900

Noncompliant:

```python
n = np.busday_count(a, b, weekmask='11111')
```

Compliant:

```python
n = np.busday_count(a, b, weekmask='1111100')
```

## py/tf-gather-validate-indices

**The validate_indices argument should not be set for tf.gather.** It is deprecated and ignored.

SonarQube: S6925

Noncompliant:

```python
y = tf.gather(x, i, validate_indices=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
y = tf.gather(x, i)
```

## py/tf-reduction-axis

**The reduction axis should be specified when using reduction operations.** Without an axis everything is reduced to a scalar, which is rarely meant.

SonarQube: S6929

Noncompliant:

```python
y = tf.reduce_sum(x)
```

Compliant:

```python
y = tf.reduce_sum(x, axis=0)
```

## py/tf-function-recursive

**tf.function should not be recursive.** A traced function cannot call itself.

SonarQube: S6908

Noncompliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(n):
    return f(n - 1)
```

Compliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(n):
    return n
```

## py/tf-function-side-effects

**Python side effects should not be used inside a tf.function.** They run once during tracing, not on every call.

SonarQube: S6928

Noncompliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(x):
    print(x)
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(x):
    tf.print(x)
    return x
```

## py/tf-variable-in-function

**tf.Variable objects should be singletons when created inside of a tf.function.** A new variable is created on every trace.

SonarQube: S6918

Noncompliant:

```python
@tf.function
def f(x):
    v = tf.Variable(1)
    return v
```

Compliant:

```python
v = tf.Variable(1)
@tf.function
def f(x):
    return v
```

## py/sklearn-pipeline-memory

**memory parameter should be specified for Scikit-Learn Pipeline.** Caching avoids refitting unchanged steps.

SonarQube: S6969

Noncompliant:

```python
p = Pipeline([('s', s)])
```

Compliant:

```python
p = Pipeline([('s', s)], memory='cache')
```

## py/ml-hyperparameters

**Important hyperparameters should be specified for machine learning estimators.** Library defaults rarely suit a given problem.

SonarQube: S6973

Noncompliant:

```python
m = RandomForestClassifier()
```

Compliant:

```python
m = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=200)
```

## py/estimator-init-attribute

**Subclasses of BaseEstimator should not set attributes ending with _ in __init__.** Scikit-learn reserves them for values learned in fit.

SonarQube: S6974

Noncompliant:

```python
class E(BaseEstimator):
    def __init__(self):
        self.coef_ = None
```

Compliant:

```python
class E(BaseEstimator):
    def __init__(self):
        self.alpha = 1
```

## py/torch-state-without-mode

**model.eval() or model.train() should be called after loading the state of a PyTorch model.** Dropout and batch norm behave differently in each mode.

SonarQube: S6982

Noncompliant:

```python
model.load_state_dict(state)
use(model)
```

Compliant:

```python
model.load_state_dict(state)
model.eval()
```

## py/async-polling-loop

**Events should be used instead of sleep in asynchronous loops.** Polling wakes up for nothing and reacts late.

SonarQube: S7484

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    while not done:
        await asyncio.sleep(1)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await done_event.wait()
```

## py/sleep-forever

**Long sleep durations should use sleep_forever() instead of arbitrary intervals.** A huge sleep is a forever-wait in disguise.

SonarQube: S7486

Noncompliant:

```python
await trio.sleep(86400)
```

Compliant:

```python
await trio.sleep_forever()
```

## py/cancel-scope-without-checkpoint

**Cancellation scopes should contain checkpoints.** A scope can only cancel code at an await.

SonarQube: S7490

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    with trio.fail_after(1):
        work()
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    with trio.fail_after(1):
        await work()
```

## py/cancellation-swallowed

**Cancellation exceptions should be re-raised after cleanup.** Swallowing it breaks timeouts and task groups.

SonarQube: S7497

Noncompliant:

```python
try:
    await f()
except asyncio.CancelledError:
    cleanup()
```

Compliant:

```python
try:
    await f()
except asyncio.CancelledError:
    cleanup()
    raise
```

## py/nursery-single-start

**TaskGroup/Nursery should not be used for a single start call.** A group of one is just a call.

SonarQube: S7513

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with trio.open_nursery() as n:
        n.start_soon(g)
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    await g()
```

## py/nursery-control-flow

**Control flow statements should not be used inside TaskGroup or Nursery blocks.** A return or break inside cancels sibling tasks in surprising ways.

SonarQube: S7514

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with trio.open_nursery() as n:
        n.start_soon(g)
        return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with trio.open_nursery() as n:
        n.start_soon(g)
    return 1
```

## py/sync-with-async-manager

**async with should be used for asynchronous resource management.** A plain `with` never calls the async enter and exit.

SonarQube: S7515

Noncompliant:

```python
async def f():
    with aiohttp.ClientSession() as s:
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
async def f():
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as s:
        pass
```

## py/not-implemented-boolean

**NotImplemented should not be used in boolean contexts.** It is truthy, and a DeprecationWarning since Python 3.9.

SonarQube: S7931

Noncompliant:

```python
if NotImplemented:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def __eq__(self, o):
    return NotImplemented
```

## py/compression-namespace

**Compression modules should be imported from the compression namespace.** Python 3.14 moved them under compression.

Off by default: add `"py/compression-namespace"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S7941

Noncompliant:

```python
import gzip
```

Compliant:

```python
from compression import gzip
```

## py/post-reads-query-args

**Query parameters should not be used in Flask POST requests.** POST data belongs in the body.

SonarQube: S8370

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['POST'])
def x():
    return request.args.get('a')
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['POST'])
def x():
    return request.form.get('a')
```

## py/preprocess-request-ignored

**Flask preprocess_request return values should be handled.** A returned response means the request is already answered.

SonarQube: S8375

Noncompliant:

```python
app.preprocess_request()
```

Compliant:

```python
rv = app.preprocess_request()
```

## py/send-file-metadata

**send_file should specify mimetype or download_name when used with file-like objects.** Without a name Flask cannot guess either.

SonarQube: S8385

Noncompliant:

```python
return send_file(io.BytesIO(data))
```

Compliant:

```python
return send_file(io.BytesIO(data), mimetype='text/plain')
```

## py/upload-with-body

**FastAPI file upload endpoints should use Form() with Pydantic validators instead of Body() or Depends().** File uploads are multipart, so Body() and Depends() can't carry the other fields.

SonarQube: S8389

Standards: [CWE-20](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/20.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```python
def up(f: UploadFile, meta: Meta = Body()):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
def up(f: UploadFile, meta: str = Form()):
    pass
```

## py/no-content-with-body

**Endpoints returning 204 status should have an empty response body.** A 204 response must not carry a body.

SonarQube: S8400

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', status_code=204)
def x():
    return {'a': 1}
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', status_code=204)
def x():
    return None
```

## py/router-included-late

**Child routers should be included before parent router registration.** A router copies its routes when it is included.

SonarQube: S8401

Noncompliant:

```python
app.include_router(parent)
parent.include_router(child)
```

Compliant:

```python
parent.include_router(child)
app.include_router(parent)
```

## py/testclient-data

**TestClient requests should use the content parameter for bytes or text.** data= is for form fields.

SonarQube: S8405

Noncompliant:

```python
client.post('/x', data='raw')
```

Compliant:

```python
client.post('/x', content='raw')
```

## py/redundant-response-model

**FastAPI routes should not specify redundant response_model parameters.** The return annotation already says it.

SonarQube: S8409

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', response_model=Item)
def x() -> Item:
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x')
def x() -> Item:
    pass
```

## py/include-router-prefix

**Router prefixes should be defined during APIRouter initialization.** Declaring it with the router keeps it in one place.

SonarQube: S8413

Noncompliant:

```python
app.include_router(router, prefix='/v1')
```

Compliant:

```python
router = APIRouter(prefix='/v1')
app.include_router(router)
```

## py/cors-not-last

**CORSMiddleware should be added last in the middleware chain.** Middleware added later runs first, so errors from outer layers skip CORS headers.

SonarQube: S8414

Noncompliant:

```python
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware)
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware)
```

Compliant:

```python
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware)
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware)
```

## py/http-exception-undocumented

**HTTPException responses should be documented in endpoint metadata.** Without responses= the OpenAPI document hides the error.

SonarQube: S8415

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.get('/x')
def x():
    raise HTTPException(404)
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x', responses={404: {}})
def x():
    raise HTTPException(404)
```

## py/slots-assignment

**Attributes should only be assigned if they are declared in __slots__.** Other names raise AttributeError.

SonarQube: S8494

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    __slots__ = ('a',)
    def __init__(self):
        self.b = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    __slots__ = ('a',)
    def __init__(self):
        self.a = 1
```

## py/tuple-length-inconsistent

**Functions should return tuples of consistent length.** Callers unpack a fixed number of values.

SonarQube: S8495

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1, 2
    return 1, 2, 3
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x):
    if x:
        return 1, 2
    return 3, 4
```

## py/comparison-methods-incomplete

**Comparison methods should be defined completely.** Defining only some of <, <=, > and >= gives surprising errors.

SonarQube: S8500

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __lt__(self, o):
        return True
```

Compliant:

```python
import functools
@functools.total_ordering
class A:
    def __eq__(self, o):
        return True
    def __lt__(self, o):
        return True
```

## py/singledispatch-mixup

**@singledispatch and @singledispatchmethod should not be confused.** The wrong one dispatches on self.

SonarQube: S8505

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    @singledispatch
    def f(self, x):
        pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    @singledispatchmethod
    def f(self, x):
        pass
```

## py/mro-conflict

**Multiple inheritance should not create Method Resolution Order conflicts.** A base listed before its own subclass makes class creation fail.

SonarQube: S8511

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    pass
class B(A):
    pass
class C(A, B):
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    pass
class B(A):
    pass
class C(B, A):
    pass
```

## py/groupby-iterator-reused

**Group iterators from itertools.groupby should not be reused.** A group is consumed when the next key is requested, and only once.

SonarQube: S8516

Noncompliant:

```python
for k, g in groupby(xs):
    a = list(g)
    b = list(g)
```

Compliant:

```python
for k, g in groupby(xs):
    a = list(g)
```

## py/bs4-html-string

**HTML elements should be created with new_tag() instead of inserting raw HTML strings.** Inserting a string makes escaped text, not an element.

SonarQube: S8903

Noncompliant:

```python
soup.body.append('<b>hi</b>')
```

Compliant:

```python
tag = soup.new_tag('b')
soup.body.append(tag)
```

## py/bs4-find-unchecked

**BeautifulSoup elements should be checked for None before accessing.** find() returns None when nothing matches.

SonarQube: S8904

Noncompliant:

```python
title = soup.find('h1').text
```

Compliant:

```python
h = soup.find('h1')
title = h.text if h else ''
```

## py/bs4-class-selector

**CSS selectors should be used when selecting elements with multiple required classes.** class_='a b' matches only that exact attribute string.

SonarQube: S8906

Noncompliant:

```python
els = soup.find_all('div', class_='a b')
```

Compliant:

```python
els = soup.select('div.a.b')
```

## py/hint-missing-return

**Function returns should have type hints.** A signature without a return type tells callers nothing.

Off by default: add `"py/hint-missing-return"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6538

Noncompliant:

```python
def f():
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
def f() -> int:
    return 1
```

## py/hint-missing-parameter

**Function parameters should have type hints.** An untyped parameter tells callers nothing.

Off by default: add `"py/hint-missing-parameter"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6540

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(x):
    return x
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(x: int):
    return x
```

## py/cdk-bucket-plain-http

**S3 buckets should enforce HTTPS-only access.** Without a policy that denies plain HTTP, requests can travel unencrypted.

SonarQube: S6249

Standards: [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', versioned=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', enforce_ssl=True)
```

## py/cdk-bucket-unversioned

**Amazon S3 buckets should have versioning enabled.** Without versioning an overwrite or delete cannot be undone.

SonarQube: S6252

Standards: [CWE-16](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/16.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', enforce_ssl=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', versioned=True)
```

## py/cdk-bucket-public

**S3 buckets should not grant access to all users or authenticated users.** A public bucket exposes its objects to the whole internet.

SonarQube: S6265

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Site', public_read_access=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Site')
```

## py/cdk-bucket-public-access-not-blocked

**Amazon S3 bucket public access should be fully blocked.** Without a block, a later policy or ACL can still make objects public.

SonarQube: S6281

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', versioned=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
b = s3.Bucket(self, 'Data', block_public_access=s3.BlockPublicAccess.BLOCK_ALL)
```

## py/cdk-policy-for-everyone

**AWS resource-based policies should not grant public access.** A policy for every principal lets anyone use the resource.

SonarQube: S6270

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(principals=[iam.AnyPrincipal()], actions=a)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(principals=[iam.AccountRootPrincipal()], actions=a)
```

## py/cdk-policy-grants-everything

**Policies should not grant all privileges.** An action of * allows every operation on the resource.

SonarQube: S6302

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['*'], resources=[arn])
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=[arn])
```

## py/cdk-policy-all-resources

**IAM policies should not grant access to all account resources.** A resource of * covers every resource in the account.

SonarQube: S6304

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=['*'])
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=[arn])
```

## py/cdk-policy-privilege-escalation

**AWS IAM policies should limit the scope of permissions given.** These actions let a principal grant itself more access.

SonarQube: S6317

Standards: [CWE-269](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/269.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['iam:PassRole'], resources=['*'])
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
s = iam.PolicyStatement(actions=['s3:GetObject'], resources=['*'])
```

## py/cdk-volume-unencrypted

**EBS volumes should be encrypted.** An unencrypted volume exposes data if a snapshot or disk leaks.

SonarQube: S6275

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
v = ec2.Volume(self, 'Disk', size=Size.gibibytes(10))
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
v = ec2.Volume(self, 'Disk', size=Size.gibibytes(10), encrypted=True)
```

## py/cdk-database-unencrypted

**Amazon RDS resources should be encrypted at rest.** An unencrypted database exposes data if storage leaks.

SonarQube: S6303

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.DatabaseInstance(self, 'Db', engine=e)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.DatabaseInstance(self, 'Db', engine=e, storage_encrypted=True)
```

## py/cdk-search-unencrypted

**OpenSearch domains should have encryption at rest enabled.** An unencrypted domain exposes data if storage leaks.

SonarQube: S6308

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = opensearch.Domain(self, 'Search', version=v)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = opensearch.Domain(self, 'Search', version=v, encryption_at_rest=opensearch.EncryptionAtRestOptions(enabled=True))
```

## py/cdk-notebook-unencrypted

**SageMaker notebook instances should be encrypted at rest.** The notebook volume holds datasets and models.

SonarQube: S6319

Standards: [CWE-312](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/312.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
n = sagemaker.CfnNotebookInstance(self, 'Nb', instance_type=t, role_arn=r)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
n = sagemaker.CfnNotebookInstance(self, 'Nb', instance_type=t, role_arn=r, kms_key_id=k)
```

## py/cdk-admin-port-open

**Administration services access should be restricted to specific IP addresses.** SSH or RDP open to the world invites brute force.

SonarQube: S6321

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg.add_ingress_rule(ec2.Peer.any_ipv4(), ec2.Port.tcp(22))
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg.add_ingress_rule(ec2.Peer.ipv4('10.0.0.0/8'), ec2.Port.tcp(22))
```

## py/cdk-topic-unencrypted
