**Amazon SNS topics should be encrypted at rest.** Messages are stored unencrypted otherwise.

SonarQube: S6327

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
t = sns.Topic(self, 'Events')
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
t = sns.Topic(self, 'Events', master_key=key)
```

## py/cdk-publicly-accessible

**Public network access to cloud resources should be disabled.** A public database or cluster is reachable from the internet.

SonarQube: S6329

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), [CWE-668](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/668.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), [CWE-1327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1327.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.CfnDBInstance(self, 'Db', publicly_accessible=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
d = rds.CfnDBInstance(self, 'Db', publicly_accessible=False)
```

## py/cdk-queue-unencrypted

**SQS queues should be encrypted.** Messages are stored unencrypted otherwise.

SonarQube: S6330

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
q = sqs.Queue(self, 'Jobs', encryption=sqs.QueueEncryption.UNENCRYPTED)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
q = sqs.Queue(self, 'Jobs')
```

## py/cdk-filesystem-unencrypted

**Amazon EFS file systems should be encrypted.** An unencrypted file system exposes data if storage leaks.

SonarQube: S6332

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
f = efs.FileSystem(self, 'Fs', vpc=vpc, encrypted=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
f = efs.FileSystem(self, 'Fs', vpc=vpc)
```

## py/cdk-api-method-no-auth

**AWS API Gateway should require authentication.** A method without authorization is callable by anyone.

SonarQube: S6333

Standards: [CWE-306](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/306.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
api.root.add_method('GET', i, authorization_type=apigateway.AuthorizationType.NONE)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
api.root.add_method('GET', i, authorization_type=apigateway.AuthorizationType.IAM)
```

## py/cdk-unrestricted-outbound

**Security groups should not allow unrestricted outbound traffic.** A compromised host can reach any destination.

SonarQube: S6463

Standards: [CWE-284](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/284.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg = ec2.SecurityGroup(self, 'Sg', vpc=vpc, allow_all_outbound=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
import aws_cdk
sg = ec2.SecurityGroup(self, 'Sg', vpc=vpc, allow_all_outbound=False)
```

## py/lambda-async-handler

**AWS Lambda handlers must not be an async function.** The runtime calls the handler without an event loop, so it never runs.

SonarQube: S7614

Noncompliant:

```python
async def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {}
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {}
```

## py/lambda-client-in-handler

**Reusable resources should be initialized at construction time of Lambda functions.** Creating a client on every invocation wastes the warm start.

SonarQube: S6243

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    s3 = boto3.client('s3')
    return {}
```

Compliant:

```python
s3 = boto3.client('s3')
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {}
```

## py/lambda-reserved-variable

**Reserved environment variable names should not be overridden in Lambda functions.** The runtime sets these, and overriding them breaks it.

SonarQube: S7617

Noncompliant:

```python
import os
os.environ['AWS_REGION'] = 'x'
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
os.environ['APP_REGION'] = 'x'
```

## py/lambda-request-timeout

**Network calls in AWS Lambda functions shouldn't be made without explicit timeout parameters.** A hung call burns the whole invocation budget.

SonarQube: S7618

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    r = requests.get(url)
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    r = requests.get(url, timeout=5)
```

## py/cloudwatch-reserved-namespace

**AWS CloudWatch metrics namespace should not begin with AWS/.** That prefix is reserved for AWS services.

SonarQube: S7609

Noncompliant:

```python
cw.put_metric_data(Namespace='AWS/Custom', MetricData=[])
```

Compliant:

```python
cw.put_metric_data(Namespace='App/Custom', MetricData=[])
```

## py/s3-bucket-owner

**S3 operations should verify bucket ownership using ExpectedBucketOwner parameter.** Without it a request can reach another account's bucket with the same name.

SonarQube: S7608

Standards: [CWE-441](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/441.html), [CWE-693](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/693.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```python
s3.get_object(Bucket='b', Key='k')
```

Compliant:

```python
s3.get_object(Bucket='b', Key='k', ExpectedBucketOwner=acct)
```

## py/lambda-sync-invoke

**Lambdas should not invoke other lambdas synchronously.** The caller pays for the callee's whole run time.

SonarQube: S6246

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    lambda_client.invoke(FunctionName='f', Payload=b'{}')
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    lambda_client.invoke(FunctionName='f', InvocationType='Event')
```

## py/boto-manual-pagination

**boto3 operations that support pagination should be performed using paginators.** A single call returns only the first page.

SonarQube: S7622

Noncompliant:

```python
r = s3.list_objects_v2(Bucket='b')
```

Compliant:

```python
r = s3.get_paginator('list_objects_v2').paginate(Bucket='b')
```

## py/regex-invalid

**Regular expressions should be syntactically valid.** An invalid pattern raises re.error when it is compiled.

SonarQube: S5856

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a)')
```

## py/regex-empty-group

**Regular expressions should not contain empty groups.** An empty group matches nothing and captures nothing useful.

SonarQube: S6331

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a()b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a(b)')
```

## py/regex-empty-alternative

**Alternation in regular expressions should not contain empty alternatives.** An empty alternative matches everywhere, which is rarely meant.

SonarQube: S6323

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|b')
```

## py/regex-single-character-class

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain only one character.** [a] is just a.

SonarQube: S6397

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[a]b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('ab')
```

## py/regex-useless-group

**Non-capturing groups without quantifier should not be used.** The group groups nothing.

SonarQube: S6395

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?:ab)c')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?:ab)+c')
```

## py/regex-superfluous-braces

**Superfluous curly brace quantifiers should be avoided.** {1} repeats once.

SonarQube: S6396

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a{1}')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a{2}')
```

## py/regex-concise-syntax

**Regular expression quantifiers and character classes should be used concisely.** \d is shorter than [0-9], and + than {1,}.

SonarQube: S6353

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[0-9]{1,}')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\d+')
```

## py/regex-octal-escape

**Octal escape sequences should not be used in regular expressions.** They are easy to mistake for backreferences.

SonarQube: S6537

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\012')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\x0a')
```

## py/regex-backreference-order

**Back references in regular expressions should only refer to capturing groups that are matched before.** A group cannot refer to itself, so the reference never matches.

SonarQube: S6001

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a\\1)')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a)\\1')
```

## py/regex-replacement-groups

**Replacement strings should reference existing regular expression groups.** A missing group raises re.error at runtime.

SonarQube: S6328

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
s = re.sub('(a)', '\\2', t)
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
s = re.sub('(a)', '\\1', t)
```

## py/regex-single-character-alternation

**Single-character alternations in regular expressions should be replaced with character classes.** a|b|c is [abc].

SonarQube: S6035

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|b|c')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[abc]')
```

## py/regex-multiple-spaces

**Regular expressions should not contain multiple spaces.** Several spaces in a row are easy to miscount.

SonarQube: S6326

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a   b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a {3}b')
```

## py/regex-duplicate-class-member

**Character classes in regular expressions should not contain the same character twice.** The repeated member does nothing.

SonarQube: S5869

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[aa]')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[ab]')
```

## py/regex-grapheme-in-class

**Unicode Grapheme Clusters should be avoided inside regex character classes.** A class matches one code point, so a cluster is split.

SonarQube: S5868

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('[e\u0301]')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?:e\u0301)')
```

## py/regex-repeat-matches-empty

**Repeated patterns in regular expressions should not match the empty string.** The loop can spin without consuming anything.

SonarQube: S5842

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a*)*')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a+)*')
```

## py/regex-anchor-alternation

**Alternatives in regular expressions should be grouped when used with anchors.** In ^a|b|c$ the anchors bind only the first and last alternative.

SonarQube: S5850

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('^a|b|c$')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('^(?:a|b|c)$')
```

## py/regex-redundant-alternative

**Regex alternatives should not be redundant.** An alternative another one already covers never decides the match.

SonarQube: S5855

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|a')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a|b')
```

## py/regex-possessive-always-fails

**Regex patterns following a possessive quantifier should not always fail.** A possessive quantifier takes everything it can match, leaving nothing for the next item.

SonarQube: S5994

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a*+a')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a*+b')
```

## py/regex-unmatchable-boundary

**Regex boundaries should not be used in a way that can never be matched.** Text after $ or before ^ cannot match.

SonarQube: S5996

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('a$b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('^ab$')
```

## py/regex-contradictory-lookaround

**Regex lookahead assertions should not be contradictory.** The next character is required and forbidden at once.

SonarQube: S6002

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?=a)b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(?=a)ab')
```

## py/regex-reluctant-at-end

**Reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions should be followed by an expression that can't match the empty string.** At the end of a pattern a reluctant quantifier matches as little as possible.

SonarQube: S6019

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('ab.*?')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('ab.*?c')
```

## py/regex-reluctant-instead-of-class

**Character classes should be preferred over reluctant quantifiers in regular expressions.** `".*?"` backtracks where `"[^"]*"` does not.

SonarQube: S5857

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('"(.*?)"')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('"([^"]*)"')
```

## py/regex-too-complex

**Regular expressions should not be too complicated.** A pattern nobody can read is a pattern nobody can fix.

SonarQube: S5843

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('((a|b)+(c|d)+)+((e|f)+(g|h)+)+((i|j)+(k|l)+)+')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a|b)+')
```

## py/regex-catastrophic-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause catastrophic backtracking.** Nested repetitions that can match the same text take exponential time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S5852

Standards: [CWE-1333](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1333.html)

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a+)+b')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('(a+b)+')
```

## py/regex-polynomial-backtracking

**Regular expressions should not cause non-linear backtracking.** Adjacent repetitions over the same characters take polynomial time on input that almost matches.

SonarQube: S8786

Noncompliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\d*\\d*x')
```

Compliant:

```python
import re
p = re.compile('\\d+x')
```

## py/missing-docstring

**Docstrings should be defined.** Public modules, classes and functions are documented in their docstring.

Off by default: add `"py/missing-docstring"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1720

Noncompliant:

```python
def run():
    return 1
```

Compliant:

```python
"""Tools."""
def run():
    """Run it."""
    return 1
```

## py/missing-license-header

**Track lack of copyright and license headers.** A header states who owns the file and under which terms it is shared.

Off by default: add `"py/missing-license-header"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S1451

Noncompliant:

```python
import os
```

Compliant:

```python
# Copyright 2026 Example Ltd.
import os
```

## py/aws-polling-loop

**AWS waiters should be used instead of custom polling loops.** A waiter polls with the right delays and gives up cleanly.

SonarQube: S7621

Noncompliant:

```python
import boto3
while True:
    s = ec2.describe_instances()
    time.sleep(5)
```

Compliant:

```python
import boto3
ec2.get_waiter('instance_running').wait()
```

## py/lambda-tmp-not-cleaned

**AWS Lambda handlers should clean up temporary files in /tmp directory.** The directory survives between invocations and fills up.

SonarQube: S7620

Noncompliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    open('/tmp/out', 'w').write('x')
```

Compliant:

```python
import os
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    open('/tmp/out', 'w').write('x')
    os.remove('/tmp/out')
```

## py/lambda-non-json-return

**AWS Lambda handlers should return only JSON serializable values.** The runtime serializes the return value with json.

SonarQube: S7613

Noncompliant:

```python
import datetime
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {'at': datetime.datetime.now()}
```

Compliant:

```python
def lambda_handler(event, context):
    return {'at': 'now'}
```

## py/flask-single-method-route

**Generic route decorators should not be used.** @app.get says the method without a keyword argument.

SonarQube: S8412

Noncompliant:

```python
@app.route('/x', methods=['GET'])
def x():
    pass
```

Compliant:

```python
@app.get('/x')
def x():
    pass
```

## py/flask-view-decorator

**Flask class-based view decorators should be applied using the decorators attribute.** Wrapping as_view() hides the view's name and breaks routing.

SonarQube: S8374

Noncompliant:

```python
app.add_url_rule('/x', view_func=login_required(V.as_view('x')))
```

Compliant:

```python
class V(View):
    decorators = [login_required]
app.add_url_rule('/x', view_func=V.as_view('x'))
```

## py/pandas-datetime-format

**Dates should be formatted correctly when using pandas.to_datetime with dayfirst or yearfirst.** An explicit format makes dayfirst and yearfirst ignored.

SonarQube: S6894

Noncompliant:

```python
d = pd.to_datetime(s, format='%d/%m/%Y', dayfirst=True)
```

Compliant:

```python
d = pd.to_datetime(s, format='%d/%m/%Y')
```

## py/einops-pattern

**Einops pattern should be valid.** A pattern needs an arrow and balanced parentheses.

SonarQube: S6984

Noncompliant:

```python
import einops
y = einops.rearrange(x, 'b c h w')
```

Compliant:

```python
import einops
y = einops.rearrange(x, 'b c h w -> b (c h) w')
```

## py/special-method-return

**Functions and methods should only return expected values.** Python rejects a __len__ that returns text or a __bool__ that returns a number.

SonarQube: S935

Noncompliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self):
        return 'a'
```

Compliant:

```python
class A:
    def __len__(self):
        return 1
```

## py/call-argument-mismatch

**The number and name of arguments passed to a function should match its parameters.** A wrong count or name raises TypeError.

SonarQube: S930

Noncompliant:

```python
def f(a, b):
    return a
f(1, 2, 3)
```

Compliant:

```python
def f(a, b):
    return a
f(1, 2)
```

## py/builtin-argument-type

**Arguments given to functions should be of an expected type.** A number is not iterable and has no length.

SonarQube: S5655

Noncompliant:

```python
n = len(5)
```

Compliant:

```python
n = len([5])
```

## py/used-before-defined

**Variables, classes and functions should be defined before being used.** At module level a name must exist when the statement runs.

SonarQube: S3827

Noncompliant:

```python
print(later)
later = 1
```

Compliant:

```python
later = 1
print(later)
```

## py/typevar-generic-function

**Generic functions should be defined using the type parameter syntax.** Python 3.12 declares type parameters on the function.

SonarQube: S6796

Noncompliant:

```python
from typing import TypeVar
T = TypeVar('T')
def first(x: list[T]) -> T:
    return x[0]
```

Compliant:

```python
def first[T](x: list[T]) -> T:
    return x[0]
```

## py/keras-input-shape

**The input_shape parameter should not be specified for tf.keras.Model subclasses.** A subclassed model builds on the first call; input_shape is ignored.

SonarQube: S6919

Noncompliant:

```python
class M(tf.keras.Model):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.d = Dense(1, input_shape=(3,))
```

Compliant:

```python
class M(tf.keras.Model):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.d = Dense(1)
```

## py/xml-signature-no-x509

**XML signatures should be validated securely.** Without a certificate check anyone can sign the document.

SonarQube: S6377

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```python
import signxml
XMLVerifier().verify(data, require_x509=False)
```

Compliant:

```python
import signxml
XMLVerifier().verify(data, x509_cert=cert)
```

## java/string-reference-equality

**Strings should be compared with equals().** `==` compares references, not contents, so equal strings may compare unequal.

SonarQube: S4973

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return s == "x"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return "x".equals(s); } }
```

## java/weak-hash

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD2, MD5 and SHA-1 are broken for security purposes.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256"); } }
```

## java/empty-catch

**Exceptions should not be silently swallowed.** An empty catch block hides failures. Handle the error or explain with a comment.

SonarQube: S108 (related)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) { log(e); } } }
```

## java/print-stack-trace

**printStackTrace() should not be used.** Stack traces printed to stderr bypass logging and may leak internals.

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Exception e) { e.printStackTrace(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Exception e) { log.error("failed", e); } }
```

## java/system-exit

**System.exit() should not be called.** System.exit() shuts down the whole JVM, which is rarely what library or server code wants.

SonarQube: S1147

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.exit(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new IllegalStateException(); } }
```

## java/system-out

**System.out and System.err should not be used for logging.** Use a logger so output can be filtered, formatted and routed.

SonarQube: S106

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.out.println("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { log.info("x"); } }
```

## java/sql-built-dynamically

**SQL queries should not be built from strings.** Concatenating values into a query lets an attacker change what it does; pass them as parameters.

SonarQube: S2077 (related)

Standards: [CWE-89](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html), [CWE-943](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/943.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A03:2021 Injection

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stmt.executeQuery("SELECT * FROM t WHERE id = " + id); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { stmt.executeQuery("SELECT 1"); } }
```

## java/cookie-not-secure

**Cookies should have the secure flag.** Without `secure`, the cookie is also sent over plain HTTP.

SonarQube: S2092

Standards: [CWE-311](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/311.html), [CWE-315](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/315.html), [CWE-614](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/614.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cookie.setSecure(false); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cookie.setSecure(true); } }
```

## java/cookie-not-httponly

**Cookies should have the HttpOnly flag.** Without HttpOnly, scripts on the page can read the cookie.

SonarQube: S3330

Standards: [CWE-1004](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1004.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cookie.setHttpOnly(false); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { cookie.setHttpOnly(true); } }
```

## java/trust-manager-accepts-all

**Server certificates should be verified.** A trust manager whose check methods do nothing accepts every certificate.

SonarQube: S4830

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { public void checkServerTrusted(X509Certificate[] c, String a) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { public void checkServerTrusted(X509Certificate[] c, String a) { validate(c); } }
```

## java/hostname-verifier-accepts-all

**Server hostnames should be verified.** A hostname verifier that always returns true accepts a valid certificate for any other site.

SonarQube: S5527

Standards: [CWE-297](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/297.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class V { public boolean verify(String h, SSLSession s) { return true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class V { public boolean verify(String h, SSLSession s) { return check(h); } }
```

## java/weak-hash-library

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD5 and SHA-1 have known collisions and are unsuitable for security purposes.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DigestUtils.md5Hex(data); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DigestUtils.sha256Hex(data); } }
```

## java/weak-cipher

**Cipher algorithms should be robust.** DES, RC2, RC4 and Blowfish are broken or have small blocks.

SonarQube: S5547

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Cipher.getInstance("DES/CBC/PKCS5Padding"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding"); } }
```

## java/insecure-cipher-mode

**Encryption algorithms should be used with secure mode and padding.** ECB leaks patterns, and CBC with PKCS5 padding is open to padding-oracle attacks.

SonarQube: S5542

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Cipher.getInstance("AES/ECB/PKCS5Padding"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding"); } }
```

## java/weak-tls-version

**Weak SSL/TLS protocols should not be used.** SSL, SSLv3, TLS 1.0 and TLS 1.1 have known attacks.

SonarQube: S4423

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { SSLContext.getInstance("TLSv1"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { SSLContext.getInstance("TLSv1.3"); } }
```

## java/weak-key-size

**Cryptographic keys should be robust.** RSA and DSA keys under 2048 bits can be factored with modest resources.

SonarQube: S4426

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { KeyPairGenerator gen = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA"); gen.initialize(1024); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { KeyPairGenerator gen = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance("RSA"); gen.initialize(3072); } }
```

## java/jwt-unverified

**JWTs should be signed and verified.** Decoding without verifying accepts tokens anyone could have forged.

SonarQube: S5659

Standards: [CWE-347](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/347.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { JWT.decode(token); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { JWT.require(alg).build().verify(token); } }
```

## java/csrf-disabled

**CSRF protections should not be disabled.** Turning the check off lets other sites make requests as the signed-in user.

SonarQube: S4502 (related)

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.csrf().disable(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.csrf(); } }
```

## java/session-fixation-disabled

**A new session should be created during user authentication.** Keeping the pre-login session lets an attacker who planted its id take over the account.

SonarQube: S5876

Standards: [CWE-384](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/384.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.sessionManagement().sessionFixation().none(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { http.sessionManagement().sessionFixation().newSession(); } }
```

## java/cors-any-origin

**CORS policies should be restricted to trusted origins.** Allowing every origin lets any site read the responses on behalf of a signed-in user.

SonarQube: S5122 (related)

Standards: [CWE-346](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/346.html), [CWE-942](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/942.html), OWASP A02:2025 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "https://app.example.com"); } }
```

## java/hardcoded-credential

**Credentials should not be hard-coded.** A password or key in source is visible to everyone with the code and stays in its history.

SonarQube: S6437 (related), S2068 (related), S6418 (related)

Standards: [CWE-259](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/259.html), [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String password = "Tr0ub4dor&3"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String password = System.getenv("DB_PASSWORD"); } }
```

## java/empty-db-password

**A secure password should be used when connecting to a database.** An empty password means anyone who can reach the database can use it.

SonarQube: S2115

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DriverManager.getConnection(url, user, ""); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DriverManager.getConnection(url, user, pw); } }
```

## java/world-permissions

**File permissions should not be world-accessible.** Permissions for 'other' let every user on the machine use the file.

SonarQube: S2612

Standards: [CWE-266](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/266.html), [CWE-732](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/732.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rwxrwxrwx"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { PosixFilePermissions.fromString("rw-------"); } }
```

## java/public-tmp

**Temporary files should not be created in publicly writable directories.** Other users can pre-create or swap files in /tmp, so a predictable path can be hijacked.

SonarQube: S5443

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String p = "/tmp/report.csv"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Path p = Files.createTempFile("report", ".csv"); } }
```

## java/create-temp-file

**Insecure temporary file creation methods should not be used.** File.createTempFile creates the file with default permissions in a shared directory.

SonarQube: S5445

Standards: [CWE-377](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/377.html), [CWE-379](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/379.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { File.createTempFile("a", ".b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Files.createTempFile("a", ".b"); } }
```

## java/random-secret

**Pseudorandom generators should not produce secrets.** Random and Math.random are predictable; tokens and passwords built from them can be guessed.

SonarQube: S2245 (related)

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-330](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/330.html), [CWE-338](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/338.html), [CWE-1241](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1241.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String sessionToken = Long.toString(new Random().nextLong()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String sessionToken = Long.toString(new SecureRandom().nextLong());
int jitter = new Random().nextInt(10); } }
```

## java/secure-random-seed

**Secure random number generators should not output predictable values.** Seeding SecureRandom with a fixed value makes its output reproducible.

SonarQube: S4347

Standards: [CWE-330](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/330.html), [CWE-332](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/332.html), [CWE-336](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/336.html), [CWE-337](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/337.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { SecureRandom r = new SecureRandom("seed".getBytes()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { SecureRandom r = new SecureRandom(); } }
```

## java/ldap-anonymous

**LDAP connections should be authenticated.** Authentication 'none' lets anyone read the directory.

SonarQube: S4433

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { env.put(Context.SECURITY_AUTHENTICATION, "none"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { env.put(Context.SECURITY_AUTHENTICATION, "simple"); } }
```

## java/cleartext-url

**Clear-text protocols should not be used.** http, ftp and telnet send data, including credentials, unencrypted. Loopback and private addresses are not reported.

SonarQube: S5332 (related)

Standards: [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String api = "http://api.example-shop.io/v1"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String api = "https://api.example-shop.io/v1";
String dev = "http://localhost:8080"; } }
```

## java/os-command-path

**OS commands should not rely on PATH resolution.** A bare command name is looked up on PATH, which an attacker may control.

SonarQube: S4036

Standards: [CWE-426](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/426.html), [CWE-427](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/427.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Runtime.getRuntime().exec("ls -l"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Runtime.getRuntime().exec("/bin/ls -l"); } }
```

## java/requested-session-id

**HttpServletRequest.getRequestedSessionId() should not be used.** It returns the id the client sent, which may be stale or forged.

SonarQube: S2254

Standards: [CWE-807](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/807.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String id = request.getRequestedSessionId(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String id = request.getSession().getId(); } }
```

## java/jackson-unsafe-typing

**Jackson deserialization should not use unsafe configuration.** Default typing lets the payload choose which classes to instantiate.

SonarQube: S4544

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { mapper.enableDefaultTyping(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { mapper.disableDefaultTyping(); } }
```

## java/aws-access-key

**Long-term AWS access keys should not be used directly in code.** An access key ID in source identifies a credential that can be abused if the code leaks.

SonarQube: S6263

Standards: [CWE-798](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/798.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String key = "AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String key = System.getenv("AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID"); } }
```

## java/predictable-iv

**Cipher Block Chaining IVs should be unpredictable.** A fixed IV lets an attacker recognize repeated messages and mount chosen-plaintext attacks.

SonarQube: S3329

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new IvParameterSpec("0123456789abcdef".getBytes()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new IvParameterSpec(randomBytes); } }
```

## java/request-mapping-any-method

**HTTP routes should restrict allowed HTTP methods.** Without a method, the route also answers state-changing verbs such as DELETE.

SonarQube: S3752

Standards: [CWE-352](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html), OWASP A01:2025 Broken Access Control, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @RequestMapping("/x") void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @GetMapping("/x") void f() {} }
```

## java/webview-file-access

**WebViews should not allow unrestricted file access.** File access from a WebView lets pages read local files.

SonarQube: S6363

Standards: [CWE-79](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html), OWASP A05:2025 Injection, OWASP A01:2021 Broken Access Control

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { settings.setAllowFileAccess(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { settings.setAllowFileAccess(false); } }
```

## java/android-external-storage

**Sensitive data should not be stored in Android external storage.** Any app with storage permission can read external storage.

SonarQube: S5324

Standards: [CWE-312](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/312.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design, OWASP A04:2021 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { File d = Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { File d = context.getFilesDir(); } }
```

## java/broadcast-without-permission

**Intents should not be broadcast or registered without a permission.** Without a permission, any app can receive or send the broadcast.

SonarQube: S5320

Standards: [CWE-927](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/927.html)

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { context.sendBroadcast(intent); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { context.sendBroadcast(intent, "com.example.PERM"); } }
```

## java/finalize-called

**Object.finalize() should not be called.** Calling finalize by hand runs cleanup that the garbage collector will run again.

SonarQube: S1111

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { obj.finalize(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { obj.close(); } }
```

## java/finalize-signature

**The signature of finalize() should match that of Object.finalize().** A method named finalize with another signature is never called by the JVM.

SonarQube: S1175

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void finalize(int x) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { protected void finalize() throws Throwable {} }
```

## java/equals-parameter

**equals overrides should accept Object parameters.** An equals(Foo) overloads equals instead of overriding it, so collections never call it.

SonarQube: S1201

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(A other) { return true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(Object other) { return true; } }
```

## java/equals-without-hashcode

**equals and hashCode should be overridden in pairs.** Objects that are equal must have the same hash code, or hash-based collections misbehave.

SonarQube: S1206

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } public int hashCode() { return 1; } }
```

## java/thread-run-direct

**Thread.run() should not be called directly.** Calling run() executes the code on the current thread; start() starts a new one.

SonarQube: S1217

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new Thread(task).run(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new Thread(task).start(); } }
```

## java/misspelled-method-name

**Methods should not be named tostring, hashcode or equal.** These names look like the Object methods but are different methods that nothing calls.

SonarQube: S1221

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public String tostring() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public String toString() { return ""; } }
```

## java/string-builder-char

**StringBuilder and StringBuffer should not be instantiated with a character.** new StringBuilder('x') creates a builder with the char's numeric value as its capacity.

SonarQube: S1317

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder('x'); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("x"); } }
```

## java/self-assignment

**Variables should not be self-assigned.** Assigning a value to itself does nothing and usually hides a typo.

SonarQube: S1656

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int x; void f() { x = x; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int x; void f(int y) { x = y; } }
```

## java/single-iteration-loop

**Loops with at most one iteration should be refactored.** A loop whose body always ends in a jump runs its body once.

SonarQube: S1751

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { return i; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (ok(i)) { return i; } } } }
```

## java/identical-operands

**Identical expressions should not be used on both sides of a binary operator.** `a == a` is always true and `a - a` always zero: one side is almost certainly a typo.

SonarQube: S1764

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = a > a; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = a > b; } }
```

## java/hasnext-calls-next

**Iterator.hasNext() should not call Iterator.next().** hasNext must not change the iterator's state, or checking twice skips an element.

SonarQube: S1849

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public boolean hasNext() { return it.next() != null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public boolean hasNext() { return it.hasNext(); } }
```

## java/synchronized-on-literal

**Synchronization should not be done on shared literals or boxed values.** Equal literals and cached boxes are the same object everywhere, so unrelated code shares the lock.

SonarQube: S1860

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { synchronized ("lock") { work(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { synchronized (lock) { work(); } } }
```

## java/duplicate-condition

**Related if/else if statements should not have the same condition.** The second branch can never run.

SonarQube: S1862

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else if (b) { g(); } else if (a) { h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else if (b) { g(); } else if (c) { h(); } } }
```

## java/class-compared-by-name

**Classes should not be compared by name.** Two different classes can share a name when loaded by different class loaders.

SonarQube: S1872

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = a.getClass().getName().equals(b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = a.getClass() == b.getClass(); } }
```

## java/serialization-signature

**Custom serialization methods should have the required signatures.** The JVM only calls writeObject, readObject and friends when their signatures are exact.

SonarQube: S2061

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { void writeObject(ObjectOutputStream o) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private void writeObject(ObjectOutputStream o) {} }
```

## java/invalid-date

**Invalid Date values should not be used.** Month 12 or day 32 silently rolls over into the next period.

SonarQube: S2110

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Date d = new Date(2024, 12, 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Date d = new Date(2024, 11, 1); } }
```

## java/bigdecimal-double

**BigDecimal(double) should not be used.** The double is already inexact, and BigDecimal faithfully keeps every wrong digit.

SonarQube: S2111

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal(0.1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("0.1"); } }
```

## java/collection-self-argument

**Collections should not be passed as arguments to their own methods.** list.removeAll(list) empties it and list.addAll(list) doubles it, which is rarely intended.

SonarQube: S2114

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { list.addAll(list); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { list.addAll(other); } }
```

## java/random-per-call

**Random objects should be reused.** A new Random each time can return the same value when created in the same millisecond.

SonarQube: S2119

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = new Random().nextInt(10); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = random.nextInt(10); } }
```

## java/scheduled-zero-threads

**ScheduledThreadPoolExecutor should not have 0 core threads.** With no core threads, scheduled tasks never run.

SonarQube: S2122

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new ScheduledThreadPoolExecutor(0); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new ScheduledThreadPoolExecutor(1); } }
```

## java/useless-increment

**Values should not be uselessly incremented.** `i = i++` assigns the old value back, so the increment is lost.

SonarQube: S2123

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = 0; i = i++; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = 0; i++; } }
```

## java/thread-without-run

**Classes extending Thread should provide a specific run behavior.** A Thread subclass that doesn't override run does nothing when started.

SonarQube: S2134

Noncompliant:

```java
class W extends Thread { void other() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class W extends Thread { public void run() { work(); } }
```

## java/interrupt-swallowed

**InterruptedException should not be ignored.** Swallowing the interrupt hides a request to stop from the code above.

SonarQube: S2142

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { work(); } catch (InterruptedException e) { log(e); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { work(); } catch (InterruptedException e) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); } } }
```

## java/run-finalizers-on-exit

**runFinalizersOnExit should not be called.** It is deprecated, unsafe and was removed.

SonarQube: S2151

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.runFinalizersOnExit(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.exit(0); } }
```

## java/boxing-roundtrip

**Unnecessary boxing and unboxing should be avoided.** Boxing a value only to unbox it again wastes time.

SonarQube: S2153

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = Integer.valueOf(s).intValue(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = Integer.parseInt(s); } }
```

## java/compareto-min-value

**compareTo should not return Integer.MIN_VALUE.** Negating or taking the absolute value of MIN_VALUE overflows.

SonarQube: S2167

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public int compareTo(A o) { return Integer.MIN_VALUE; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public int compareTo(A o) { return -1; } }
```

## java/double-checked-locking

**Double-checked locking should not be used.** Without volatile, another thread can see a half-built object.

SonarQube: S2168

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static Object inst; static Object get() { if (inst == null) { synchronized (A.class) { if (inst == null) { inst = new Object(); } } } return inst; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static volatile Object inst; static Object get() { if (inst == null) { synchronized (A.class) { if (inst == null) { inst = new Object(); } } } return inst; } }
```

## java/shift-out-of-range

**Ints and longs should not be shifted by zero or more than their number of bits.** Shifting by 0 does nothing, and shifting by 64 or more is the same as shifting by the remainder.

SonarQube: S2183

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int r = x << 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int r = x << 3; } }
```

## java/compareto-specific-value

**compareTo results should not be checked for specific values.** compareTo may return any negative or positive number, not just -1 and 1.

SonarQube: S2200

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = a.compareTo(b) == 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = a.compareTo(b) > 0; } }
```

## java/null-returned-by-object-method

**toString and clone should not return null.** Callers use the result without checking it.

SonarQube: S2225

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public String toString() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public String toString() { return ""; } }
```

## java/loop-direction

**A for loop update clause should move the counter in the right direction.** A counter that moves away from its bound loops until it overflows.

SonarQube: S2251

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 10; i--) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {} } }
```

## java/iterator-next-exception

**Iterator.next() methods should throw NoSuchElementException.** The Iterator contract requires it when no element is left.

SonarQube: S2272

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Iterator<String> { public boolean hasNext() { return true; } public String next() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Iterator<String> { public boolean hasNext() { return true; } public String next() { throw new NoSuchElementException(); } }
```

## java/format-arguments

**Printf-style format strings should not lead to unexpected behavior at runtime.** Too few arguments throw at runtime, and extra ones are silently ignored.

SonarQube: S2275

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = String.format("%s and %s and %s", a, b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = String.format("%s and %s", a, b); } }
```

## java/sleep-while-locked

**wait(...) should be used instead of Thread.sleep(...) when a lock is held.** sleep keeps the lock while it waits, blocking everyone else.

SonarQube: S2276

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { synchronized (lock) { Thread.sleep(10); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { synchronized (lock) { lock.wait(10); } } }
```

## java/notify-single

**notifyAll() should be preferred over notify().** notify wakes one arbitrary waiter, which may not be the one that can proceed.

SonarQube: S2446

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { lock.notify(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { lock.notifyAll(); } }
```

## java/regex-metacharacter

**Inappropriate regular expressions should not be used.** split(".") splits on every character and split("|") on every position, both giving unexpected results.

SonarQube: S2639

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String[] p = s.split("."); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String[] p = s.split("\\."); } }
```

## java/read-result-ignored

**The value returned from a stream read should be checked.** read(buffer) may fill only part of the buffer, and readLine returns null at the end.

SonarQube: S2674

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { in.read(buffer); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int n = in.read(buffer); } }
```

## java/abs-of-unbounded

**Math.abs and negation should not be used on numbers that could be MIN_VALUE.** abs(Integer.MIN_VALUE) is still negative, and hashCode and nextInt can return it.

SonarQube: S2676

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = Math.abs(key.hashCode()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int i = Math.floorMod(key.hashCode(), size); } }
```

## java/prepared-statement-index

**PreparedStatement methods should be called with valid indices.** Parameter indices start at 1; index 0 throws.

SonarQube: S2695

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ps.setString(0, name); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ps.setString(1, name); } }
```

## java/repeated-unary-operator

**Unary prefix operators should not be repeated.** `!!x` and `~~x` cancel out.

SonarQube: S2761

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = !!flag; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = flag; } }
```

## java/optional-null

**null should not be used with Optional.** An Optional that can itself be null defeats its purpose.

SonarQube: S2789

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Optional<String> o = null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Optional<String> o = Optional.empty(); } }
```

## java/non-thread-safe-static

**Non-thread-safe fields should not be static.** SimpleDateFormat and Calendar keep internal state, so sharing one corrupts results.

SonarQube: S2885

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static final SimpleDateFormat F = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy"); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static final DateTimeFormatter F = DateTimeFormatter.ISO_DATE; }
```

## java/volatile-mutable-field

**Non-primitive fields should not be volatile.** volatile protects the reference, not the contents of a collection or array.

SonarQube: S3077

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { volatile List<String> items; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { volatile boolean ready; }
```

## java/assert-side-effects

**Expressions used in assert should not produce side effects.** Assertions are disabled by default, so code relying on their side effects silently doesn't run.

SonarQube: S3346

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assert list.remove(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean removed = list.remove(x); assert removed; } }
```

## java/double-brace-initialization

**Double Brace Initialization should not be used.** It creates an anonymous subclass for every use and keeps the enclosing instance alive.

SonarQube: S3599

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> l = new ArrayList<>() {{ add("a"); }}; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { List<String> l = List.of("a"); } }
```

## java/size-comparison

**Collection sizes and array length comparisons should make sense.** A size is never negative, so `size() < 0` is always false and `size() >= 0` always true.

SonarQube: S3981

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = list.size() < 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean r = list.isEmpty(); } }
```

## java/exception-not-thrown

**Exceptions should not be created without being thrown.** `new IllegalStateException(...)` on its own builds an object and drops it.

SonarQube: S3984

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new IllegalStateException("bad"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new IllegalStateException("bad"); } }
```

## java/week-year

**Week Year (YYYY) should not be used for date formatting.** YYYY is the week-based year, which differs from the calendar year in the last days of December.

SonarQube: S3986

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { SimpleDateFormat f = new SimpleDateFormat("YYYY-MM-dd"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { SimpleDateFormat f = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd"); } }
```

## java/map-value-replaced

**Map values should not be replaced unconditionally.** Putting twice under the same key discards the first value.

SonarQube: S4143

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { m.put(k, 1); m.put(k, 2); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { m.put(k, 1); m.put(j, 2); } }
```

## java/iterator-returns-this

**iterator() should not return this.** A second iteration would continue where the first stopped.

SonarQube: S4348

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Iterable<String>, Iterator<String> { public Iterator<String> iterator() { return this; } public boolean hasNext() { return false; } public String next() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Iterable<String> { public Iterator<String> iterator() { return list.iterator(); } }
```

## java/assertion-compares-same-object

**Assertions should not compare an object to itself.** assertEquals(x, x) always passes.

SonarQube: S5863

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(actual, actual); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(expected, actual); } }
```

## java/record-array-component

**Equals should be overridden in records containing array fields.** The generated equals compares arrays by reference.

SonarQube: S6218

Noncompliant:

```java
record R(int[] values) {}
```

Compliant:

```java
record R(int[] values) { public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
```

## java/async-return-type

**Async methods should return void or Future.** The caller can't see the result of any other return type.

SonarQube: S6810

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Async String work() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Async CompletableFuture<String> work() { return null; } }
```

## java/autowired-constructors

**@Autowired should only be used on a single constructor.** Spring can't choose between several required constructors.

SonarQube: S6818

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired A(B b) {} @Autowired A(C c) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired A(B b) {} A(C c) {} }
```

## java/qualifier-on-bean

**@Qualifier should not be used on @Bean methods.** On a @Bean method it has no effect.

SonarQube: S6831

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Bean @Qualifier("x") B b() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Bean B b() { return null; } }
```

## java/scheduled-with-arguments

**@Scheduled annotation should only be applied to no-arg methods.** Spring can't supply arguments to a scheduled call.

SonarQube: S7184

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Scheduled(fixedRate = 1000) void tick(int x) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Scheduled(fixedRate = 1000) void tick() {} }
```

## java/event-listener-parameters

**@EventListener methods should have one parameter at most.** Spring passes the event as the only argument.

SonarQube: S7185

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @EventListener void on(Foo f, Bar b) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @EventListener void on(Foo f) {} }
```

## java/clamp-range

**Math.clamp should be used with correct ranges.** clamp throws when the minimum is above the maximum.

SonarQube: S6913

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int r = Math.clamp(v, 10, 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int r = Math.clamp(v, 1, 10); } }
```

## java/class-name

**Class names should comply with a naming convention.** PascalCase marks a name as a type.

SonarQube: S101

Noncompliant:

```java
class my_widget {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class MyWidget {}
```

## java/interface-name

**Interface names should comply with a naming convention.** PascalCase marks a name as a type.

SonarQube: S114

Noncompliant:

```java
interface my_api {}
```

Compliant:

```java
interface MyApi {}
```

## java/method-name

**Method names should comply with a naming convention.** Methods are camelCase, which tells them apart from types.

SonarQube: S100

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void Do_it() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void doIt() {} }
```

## java/constant-name

**Constant names should comply with a naming convention.** UPPER_SNAKE_CASE marks a static final field as a constant.

SonarQube: S115

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static final int maxSize = 3; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static final int MAX_SIZE = 3; }
```

## java/field-name

**Field names should comply with a naming convention.** Fields are camelCase.

SonarQube: S116

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int My_field; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int myField; }
```

## java/local-variable-name

**Local variable and parameter names should comply with a naming convention.** Variables are camelCase.

SonarQube: S117

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int My_param) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int myParam) {} }
```

## java/type-parameter-name

**Type parameter names should comply with a naming convention.** A single capital letter, optionally followed by a digit, is the convention.

SonarQube: S119

Noncompliant:

```java
class A<Item> {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A<T> {}
```

## java/utility-class-constructor

**Utility classes should not have public constructors.** A class with only static members is never instantiated.

SonarQube: S1118

Noncompliant:

```java
class Util { static int f() { return 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class Util { private Util() {} static int f() { return 1; } }
```

## java/labels

**Labels should not be used.** Labels make the control flow hard to follow.

SonarQube: S1119

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { outer: for (;;) { break outer; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (;;) { break; } } }
```

## java/assignment-in-subexpression

**Assignments should not be made from within sub-expressions.** `if ((x = f()) > 0)` hides an assignment in a condition.

SonarQube: S1121

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x; if ((x = g()) > 0) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x = g(); if (x > 0) {} } }
```

## java/modifier-order

**Modifiers should be declared in the correct order.** The Java Language Specification recommends public, protected, private, abstract, static, final, transient, volatile, synchronized, native, strictfp.

SonarQube: S1124

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { final static int X = 1; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static final int X = 1; }
```

## java/redundant-boolean-literal

**Boolean literals should not be redundant.** `x == true` is `x`, and `cond ? true : false` is `cond`.

SonarQube: S1125

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean x) { return x == true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(boolean x) { return x; } }
```

## java/return-boolean-if

**Return of boolean expressions should not be wrapped into an if-then-else.** `if (c) return true; else return false;` is `return c;`.

SonarQube: S1126

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int x) { if (x > 1) { return true; } else { return false; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int x) { return x > 1; } }
```

## java/unused-import

**Unnecessary imports should be removed.** An unused import misleads readers about the file's dependencies.

SonarQube: S1128

Noncompliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
class A { List<String> l; }
```

## java/nested-try

**Try-catch blocks should not be nested.** A try-catch inside a try block is easier to follow as its own method.

SonarQube: S1141

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) {} } catch (Error e) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Exception e) {} } }
```

## java/enumeration

**Enumeration should not be implemented.** Enumeration is a legacy interface; Iterator replaced it.

SonarQube: S1150

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Enumeration<String> { public boolean hasMoreElements() { return false; } public String nextElement() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Iterator<String> { public boolean hasNext() { return false; } public String next() { return null; } }
```

## java/value-of-in-concatenation

**String.valueOf() should not be appended to a String.** Concatenation already converts the value.

SonarQube: S1153

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f(int x) { return "a" + String.valueOf(x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f(int x) { return "a" + x; } }
```

## java/size-compared-to-zero

**Collection.isEmpty() should be used to test for emptiness.** isEmpty() says what is meant, and is cheaper on some collections.

SonarQube: S1155

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(List<String> l) { return l.size() == 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(List<String> l) { return l.isEmpty(); } }
```

## java/case-conversion-before-equals

**Case insensitive string comparisons should be made without intermediate allocations.** `a.toLowerCase().equals(b)` builds a throwaway string.

SonarQube: S1157

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String a, String b) { return a.toLowerCase().equals(b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String a, String b) { return a.equalsIgnoreCase(b); } }
```

## java/boxing-for-to-string

**Primitive wrappers should not be instantiated only for toString.** `new Integer(x).toString()` is `Integer.toString(x)`.

SonarQube: S1158

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f(int x) { return new Integer(x).toString(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f(int x) { return Integer.toString(x); } }
```

## java/throw-in-finally

**Exceptions should not be thrown in finally blocks.** A throw in finally replaces any exception already propagating.

SonarQube: S1163

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } finally { throw new RuntimeException(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } finally { close(); } } }
```

## java/exception-field-not-final

**Exception classes should have final fields.** Exceptions are passed between threads; mutable state in them invites races.

SonarQube: S1165

Noncompliant:

```java
class MyException extends Exception { int code; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class MyException extends Exception { final int code = 1; }
```

## java/null-instead-of-empty

**Empty arrays and collections should be returned instead of null.** Callers must then null-check before every use.

SonarQube: S1168

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { List<String> f() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { List<String> f() { return List.of(); } }
```

## java/final-field-should-be-static

**Public constants and fields initialized at declaration should be static final.** A final field with a constant value is the same in every instance.

SonarQube: S1170

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public final int MAX = 3; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public static final int MAX = 3; }
```

## java/instance-initializer

**Only static class initializers should be used.** An instance initializer block runs before every constructor and is easy to overlook.

SonarQube: S1171

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { { init(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { A() { init(); } }
```

## java/throwable-caught

**Throwable and Error should not be caught.** They include out-of-memory and other unrecoverable conditions.

SonarQube: S1181

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (Throwable t) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) {} } }
```

## java/useless-override

**Overriding methods should do more than simply call the same method in the super class.** An override that only forwards adds nothing.

SonarQube: S1185

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Override public String toString() { return super.toString(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Override public String toString() { return "A"; } }
```

## java/empty-method

**Methods should not be empty.** An empty body gives no hint whether it is unfinished or deliberate.

SonarQube: S1186

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { /* intentionally empty */ } }
```

## java/sun-package

**Classes from sun.* packages should not be used.** They are internal, unsupported and removed in newer JDKs.

SonarQube: S1191

Noncompliant:

```java
import sun.misc.Unsafe;
class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
import java.util.List;
class A {}
```

## java/array-designator-on-method

**Array designators [] should be located after the type in method signatures.** `int foo()[]` is legal but obscure.

SonarQube: S1195

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int foo()[] { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int[] foo() { return null; } }
```

## java/array-designator-on-variable

**Array designators [] should be on the type, not the variable.** `int a[]` mixes the type into the name.

SonarQube: S1197

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(String args[]) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(String[] args) {} }
```

## java/nested-block

**Nested code blocks should not be used.** A bare block inside a block adds a level of nesting without meaning.

SonarQube: S1199

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { { g(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g(); } }
```

## java/compareto-without-equals

**equals(Object) should be overridden along with compareTo.** compareTo that disagrees with equals makes sorted collections behave differently from hash collections.

SonarQube: S1210

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Comparable<A> { public int compareTo(A o) { return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Comparable<A> { public int compareTo(A o) { return 0; } public boolean equals(Object o) { return true; } }
```

## java/constants-interface

**Interfaces should not solely consist of constants.** An interface is a contract, not a place to hold constants.

SonarQube: S1214

Noncompliant:

```java
interface K { int A = 1; }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface K { void f(); }
```

## java/explicit-gc

**Execution of the Garbage Collector should be triggered only by the JVM.** Forcing a collection hurts performance and rarely helps.

SonarQube: S1215

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.gc(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g(); } }
```

## java/default-package

**The default unnamed package should not be used.** Classes in the default package cannot be imported from named packages.

SonarQube: S1220

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {}
```

Compliant:

```java
package com.acme;
class A {}
```

## java/method-named-like-class

**Non-constructor methods should not have the same name as the enclosing class.** It reads like a constructor but is not one.

SonarQube: S1223

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void A() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void init() {} }
```

## java/for-as-while

**A while loop should be used instead of a for loop.** A for with only a condition is a while.

SonarQube: S1264

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (; i < 3; ) { i++; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { while (i < 3) { i++; } } }
```

## java/switch-too-few-cases

**switch statements should have at least 3 case clauses.** A switch with one or two cases is an if.

SonarQube: S1301

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: g(); break; default: h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { if (x == 1) { g(); } else { h(); } } }
```

## java/concrete-collection-type

**Declarations should use Java collection interfaces.** Declaring ArrayList or HashMap ties the code to one implementation.

SonarQube: S1319

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { ArrayList<String> items; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { List<String> items; }
```

## java/wildcard-return-type

**Generic wildcard types should not be used in return types.** The caller can't do much with a wildcard result.

SonarQube: S1452

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { List<?> f() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { List<String> f() { return null; } }
```

## java/switch-too-many-cases

**switch statements should not have too many case clauses.** A very long switch is hard to read.

SonarQube: S1479

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 0: g(); break; case 1: g(); break; case 2: g(); break; case 3: g(); break; case 4: g(); break; case 5: g(); break; case 6: g(); break; case 7: g(); break; case 8: g(); break; case 9: g(); break; case 10: g(); break; case 11: g(); break; case 12: g(); break; case 13: g(); break; case 14: g(); break; case 15: g(); break; case 16: g(); break; case 17: g(); break; case 18: g(); break; case 19: g(); break; case 20: g(); break; case 21: g(); break; case 22: g(); break; case 23: g(); break; case 24: g(); break; case 25: g(); break; case 26: g(); break; case 27: g(); break; case 28: g(); break; case 29: g(); break; case 30: g(); break; default: h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: g(); break; default: h(); } } }
```

## java/return-after-assignment

**Local variables should not be declared and then immediately returned.** The variable adds nothing.

SonarQube: S1488

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f() { int x = g(); return x; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f() { return g(); } }
```

## java/empty-collection-constant

**Collections.EMPTY_LIST, EMPTY_MAP and EMPTY_SET should not be used.** They are raw types that lose their generics.

SonarQube: S1596

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return Collections.EMPTY_LIST; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return Collections.emptyList(); } }
```

## java/lambda-single-statement-block

**Lambdas containing only one statement should not nest this statement in a block.** `x -> { return f(x); }` is `x -> f(x)`.

SonarQube: S1602

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { run(x -> { return g(x); }); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { run(x -> g(x)); } }
```

## java/anonymous-class-to-lambda

**Anonymous inner classes containing only one method should become lambdas.** A lambda is shorter and says the same.

SonarQube: S1604

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Runnable r = new Runnable() { public void run() { g(); } }; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Runnable r = () -> g(); }
```

## java/lambda-parenthesized-parameter

**Parentheses should be removed from a single lambda parameter when its type is inferred.** `(x) -> ...` is `x -> ...`.

SonarQube: S1611

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { run((x) -> g(x)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { run(x -> g(x)); } }
```

## java/lambda-to-method-reference

**Lambdas should be replaced with method references.** `x -> f(x)` is `this::f`, and `x -> x.m()` is `X::m`.

SonarQube: S1612

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { run(x -> g(x)); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { run(this::g); } }
```

## java/string-concatenation-in-loop

**Strings should not be concatenated using + in a loop.** Each += copies the whole string again.

SonarQube: S1643

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f() { String s = ""; for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) { s += "x"; } return s; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f() { StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder(); for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) { s.append("x"); } return s.toString(); } }
```

## java/multiple-variables-per-line

**Multiple variables should not be declared on the same line.** One declaration per line is easier to read and to change.

SonarQube: S1659

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int a = 1, b = 2; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int a = 1; int b = 2; } }
```

## java/field-named-like-class

**A field should not duplicate the name of its containing class.** It reads like a constructor call or a type.

SonarQube: S1700

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int A; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int count; }
```

## java/names-differ-by-case

**Method and field names should not be the same or differ only by capitalization.** `getValue` and `getvalue` are easy to confuse.

SonarQube: S1845

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int value; int Value() { return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int value; int size() { return 0; } }
```

## java/to-string-on-string

**toString() should never be called on a String object.** It returns the same string.

SonarQube: S1858

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f() { return "x".toString(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f() { return "x"; } }
```

## java/inverted-boolean

**Boolean checks should not be inverted.** `!(a == b)` is `a != b`.

SonarQube: S1940

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int a, int b) { return !(a == b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int a, int b) { return a != b; } }
```

## java/empty-class

**Classes should not be empty.** An empty class gives no hint what it is for.

SonarQube: S2094

Noncompliant:

```java
class Empty {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class Marker { int id; }
```

## java/wrapper-constructor

**Constructors should not be used to instantiate String and primitive wrappers.** They always create a new object, where valueOf can share one.

SonarQube: S2129

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f() { return new String("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f() { return "x"; } }
```

## java/object-for-get-class

**Objects should not be created only to invoke getClass.** Use the class literal.

SonarQube: S2133

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return new Date().getClass(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return Date.class; } }
```

## java/legacy-date-time

**java.time classes should be used for dates and times.** Date and Calendar are mutable and error-prone.

SonarQube: S2143

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return new Date(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return Instant.now(); } }
```

## java/combine-catches

**Catches should be combined.** Catch blocks with the same body can use a multi-catch.

SonarQube: S2147

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { log(e); } catch (SQLException e) { log(e); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException | SQLException e) { log(e); } } }
```

## java/exception-class-name

**Classes named like Exception should extend Exception or a subclass.** A name ending in Exception promises something it doesn't deliver.

SonarQube: S2166

Noncompliant:

```java
class FooException {}
```

Compliant:

