```java
class FooException extends Exception {}
```

## java/class-name-shadows-java-lang

**Class names should not shadow interfaces or superclasses.** A class named String or List confuses every reader.

SonarQube: S2176

Noncompliant:

```java
class List {}
```

Compliant:

```java
class Items {}
```

## java/non-short-circuit

**Short-circuit logic should be used in boolean contexts.** & and | evaluate both sides, even when the first decides the result.

SonarQube: S2178

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int a, int b) { return a > 1 & b > 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(int a, int b) { return a > 1 && b > 1; } }
```

## java/result-set-is-last

**ResultSet.isLast() should not be used.** It is not supported by all drivers and can be expensive.

SonarQube: S2232

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(ResultSet rs) throws Exception { return rs.isLast(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(ResultSet rs) throws Exception { return rs.next(); } }
```

## java/illegal-monitor-caught

**IllegalMonitorStateException should not be caught.** It signals a programming error, not a condition to handle.

SonarQube: S2235

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { w(); } catch (IllegalMonitorStateException e) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { w(); } catch (InterruptedException e) {} } }
```

## java/mutable-public-static

**Mutable fields should not be public static.** Anyone can change the array or collection, for everyone.

SonarQube: S2386

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public static final String[] NAMES = { "a" }; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private static final String[] NAMES = { "a" }; }
```

## java/null-from-boolean-method

**null should not be returned from a Boolean method.** A caller that unboxes the result gets a NullPointerException.

SonarQube: S2447

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Boolean f() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Boolean f() { return false; } }
```

## java/control-character-in-literal

**Whitespace and control characters in literals should be explicit.** A literal tab is invisible and easy to lose in an editor.

SonarQube: S2479

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String s = "a	b"; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String s = "a\tb"; }
```

## java/log-argument-concatenation

**Logging arguments should not require evaluation.** A concatenated message is built even when the level is off.

SonarQube: S2629

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int id) { logger.debug("id=" + id); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int id) { logger.debug("id={}", id); } }
```

## java/index-of-positive

**indexOf checks should not be for positive numbers.** `indexOf(x) > 0` misses a match at index 0.

SonarQube: S2692

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return s.indexOf("x") > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return s.contains("x"); } }
```

## java/catch-only-rethrows

**catch clauses should do more than rethrow.** A catch that just rethrows is noise.

SonarQube: S2737

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { throw e; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { log(e); throw e; } } }
```

## java/nested-enum-static

**Nested enums should not be declared static.** Nested enums are implicitly static.

SonarQube: S2786

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static enum E { X } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { enum E { X } }
```

## java/key-set-iteration

**entrySet() should be iterated when both the key and value are needed.** Looking each key up again costs a second hash lookup.

SonarQube: S2864

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Map<String, String> m) { for (String k : m.keySet()) { use(m.get(k)); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Map<String, String> m) { for (Map.Entry<String, String> e : m.entrySet()) { use(e.getValue()); } } }
```

## java/clone-overridden

**clone should not be overridden.** clone is fragile; a copy constructor is clearer.

SonarQube: S2975

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public Object clone() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { A(A other) {} }
```

## java/set-accessible

**Reflection should not be used to increase accessibility.** setAccessible(true) bypasses the access rules the author set.

SonarQube: S3011

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Field fld) { fld.setAccessible(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Field fld) { g(fld); } }
```

## java/thread-group

**ThreadGroup should not be used.** Its methods are deprecated or unsafe.

SonarQube: S3014

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { ThreadGroup g; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { ExecutorService e; }
```

## java/append-concatenation

**Arguments to append should not be concatenated.** `sb.append(a + b)` builds a string just to copy it.

SonarQube: S3024

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(StringBuilder sb, int id) { sb.append("id=" + id); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(StringBuilder sb, int id) { sb.append("id=").append(id); } }
```

## java/enum-public-mutable-field

**enum fields should not be publicly mutable.** Shared constants should not change.

SonarQube: S3066

Noncompliant:

```java
enum E { X; public int count; }
```

Compliant:

```java
enum E { X; private int count; }
```

## java/nested-ternary

**Ternary operators should not be nested.** A ternary inside a ternary is hard to read.

SonarQube: S3358

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f(int a) { return a > 1 ? 1 : a > 0 ? 2 : 3; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f(int a) { return a > 1 ? 1 : 2; } }
```

## java/method-returns-constant

**Methods should not return constants.** A private method that returns a literal could be a constant.

SonarQube: S3400

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int f() { return 3; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int f() { return g(); } }
```

## java/logger-class

**Loggers should be named for their enclosing classes.** A logger named for another class sends output to the wrong place.

SonarQube: S3416

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Logger l = LoggerFactory.getLogger(Other.class); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Logger l = LoggerFactory.getLogger(A.class); }
```

## java/redundant-jump

**Jump statements should not be redundant.** A return at the end of a void method, or a continue at the end of a loop, changes nothing.

SonarQube: S3626

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g(); return; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g(); } }
```

## java/stream-for-each

**stream should not be used for Collection forEach calls.** Collection.forEach does the same without the stream.

SonarQube: S3706

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { l.stream().forEach(g); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(List<String> l) { l.forEach(g); } }
```

## java/private-request-mapping

**@RequestMapping methods should not be private.** Spring's proxies can't call a private method.

SonarQube: S3751

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @GetMapping("/x") private String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @GetMapping("/x") public String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/stream-peek

**Stream.peek should be used with caution.** peek may not run at all if the stream ends without needing the element.

SonarQube: S3864

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Stream<String> s) { s.peek(System.out::println).count(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Stream<String> s) { s.forEach(System.out::println); } }
```

## java/conditional-on-new-line

**Conditionals should start on new lines.** `if (a) {} if (b) {}` on one line reads like a missing else.

SonarQube: S3972

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { g(); } if (b) { h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { g(); }
if (b) { h(); } } }
```

## java/stream-find-first-present

**Stream call chains should be simplified when possible.** `filter(p).findFirst().isPresent()` is `anyMatch(p)`.

SonarQube: S4034

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Stream<String> s) { return s.filter(x -> x.isEmpty()).findFirst().isPresent(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Stream<String> s) { return s.anyMatch(x -> x.isEmpty()); } }
```

## java/thread-local-initial-value

**ThreadLocal.withInitial should be preferred.** An anonymous subclass just to set the initial value is heavy.

SonarQube: S4065

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { ThreadLocal<String> t = new ThreadLocal<String>() { protected String initialValue() { return ""; } }; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { ThreadLocal<String> t = ThreadLocal.withInitial(() -> ""); }
```

## java/identical-methods

**Methods should not have identical implementations.** Two methods with the same body are one method written twice.

SonarQube: S4144

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
int a() {
int x = 1;
return x + 1;
}
int b() {
int x = 1;
return x + 1;
}
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
int a() {
int x = 1;
return x + 1;
}
int b() {
return 2;
}
}
```

## java/null-check-with-instanceof

**Null checks should not be used with instanceof.** instanceof is already false for null.

SonarQube: S4201

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Object o) { return o != null && o instanceof String; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Object o) { return o instanceof String; } }
```

## java/specialised-functional-interface

**Functional interfaces should be as specialised as possible.** `Function<T, Boolean>` is a Predicate and `Function<T, T>` a UnaryOperator.

SonarQube: S4276

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Function<String, Boolean> f; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Predicate<String> f; }
```

## java/default-not-last

**default clauses should be last.** A default in the middle is easy to miss.

SonarQube: S4524

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { default: g(); break; case 1: h(); break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: h(); break; default: g(); break; } } }
```

## java/charset-constant

**StandardCharsets constants should be preferred.** A charset name in a string can be misspelled; a constant cannot.

SonarQube: S4719

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { byte[] f(String s) throws Exception { return s.getBytes("UTF-8"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { byte[] f(String s) { return s.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8); } }
```

## java/dangling-else

**else statements should be clearly matched with an if.** Without braces, an else binds to the nearest if.

SonarQube: S5261

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) if (b) g(); else h(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { if (b) { g(); } else { h(); } } } }
```

## java/replace-all-literal

**String#replace should be preferred to String#replaceAll.** With a plain-text pattern, replace is simpler and faster.

SonarQube: S5361

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String f(String s) { return s.replaceAll("a", "b"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { String f(String s) { return s.replace("a", "b"); } }
```

## java/junit-assert-simplifiable

**JUnit assertTrue/assertFalse should be simplified to the corresponding dedicated assertion.** assertEquals shows both values when it fails; assertTrue(a == b) shows only false.

SonarQube: S5785

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertTrue(a == b); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(a, b); } }
```

## java/abstract-class-public-constructor

**Constructors of an abstract class should not be declared public.** Only subclasses can call them.

SonarQube: S5993

Noncompliant:

```java
abstract class A { public A() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
abstract class A { protected A() {} }
```

## java/class-isinstance

**Operator instanceof should be used instead of A.class.isInstance().** instanceof is shorter and checked at compile time.

SonarQube: S6202

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Object o) { return String.class.isInstance(o); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(Object o) { return o instanceof String; } }
```

## java/collectors-to-list

**Stream.toList() should be used instead of collectors when unmodifiable lists are fine.** Stream.toList() is shorter.

Off by default: add `"java/collectors-to-list"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6204

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { List<String> f(Stream<String> s) { return s.collect(Collectors.toList()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { List<String> f(Stream<String> s) { return s.toList(); } }
```

## java/deprecated-without-reason

**Deprecated annotations should include explanations.** Without a reason or a replacement, callers don't know what to do.

SonarQube: S6355

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Deprecated void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Deprecated(since = "2.0", forRemoval = true) void f() {} }
```

## java/field-injection

**Field dependency injection should be avoided.** Constructor injection makes dependencies visible and testable.

SonarQube: S6813

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired private B b; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private final B b; A(B b) { this.b = b; } }
```

## java/redundant-response-body

**Superfluous @ResponseBody annotations should be removed.** @RestController already applies it to every method.

SonarQube: S6837

Noncompliant:

```java
@RestController class A { @ResponseBody String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@RestController class A { String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/static-field-injection

**Injecting data into static fields is not supported by Spring.** Spring injects instances, so the static field is never set.

SonarQube: S7178

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired static B b; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Autowired B b; }
```

## java/init-binder-return

**@InitBinder methods should have void return type.** Spring ignores any other return value.

SonarQube: S7183

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @InitBinder String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @InitBinder void f() {} }
```

## java/now-without-zone

**Time-based .now() methods should specify a ZoneId or a Clock.** The system default zone makes results depend on the server.

SonarQube: S8688

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return LocalDate.now(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { Object f() { return LocalDate.now(clock); } }
```

## java/generic-exception-thrown

**Generic exceptions should never be thrown.** Callers can't catch a generic exception without catching everything.

SonarQube: S112

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new RuntimeException("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { throw new IllegalStateException("x"); } }
```

## java/unused-label

**Unused labels should be removed.** A label that nothing jumps to is noise.

SonarQube: S1065

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { outer: for (;;) { break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { outer: for (;;) { break outer; } } }
```

## java/mergeable-if

**Mergeable if statements should be combined.** An if whose only content is another if can use &&.

SonarQube: S1066

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { if (b) { g(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a && b) { g(); } } }
```

## java/redundant-parentheses

**Unnecessary parentheses should be removed.** Parentheses around a whole expression add noise.

SonarQube: S1110

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f() { return (1 + 2); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f() { return 1 + 2; } }
```

## java/finalize-overridden

**Object.finalize() should not be overridden.** Finalizers run unpredictably and are deprecated for removal.

SonarQube: S1113

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { protected void finalize() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void close() {} }
```

## java/finalize-visibility

**Object.finalize() should remain protected when overridden.** Public finalize invites callers to run it.

SonarQube: S1174

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public void finalize() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { protected void finalize() {} }
```

## java/deprecated-code

**Deprecated code should be removed.** Deprecated code that stays forever is clutter.

SonarQube: S1133

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Deprecated void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() {} }
```

## java/identical-branches-in-chain

**Two branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** Repeating a block in two branches suggests they should be merged.

SonarQube: S1871

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { g(); h(); } else if (b) { g(); h(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a || b) { g(); h(); } } }
```

## java/switch-without-default

**switch statements should have default clauses.** Without a default, an unexpected value is silently ignored.

SonarQube: S131

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: g(); break; case 2: h(); break; case 3: i(); break; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: g(); break; default: h(); } } }
```

## java/multiple-jumps-in-loop

**Loops should not contain more than a single break or continue statement.** Several exits make a loop hard to follow.

SonarQube: S135

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (;;) { if (a) { break; } if (b) { continue; } } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (;;) { if (a) { break; } } } }
```

## java/unused-private-method

**Unused private methods should be removed.** Nothing calls it.

SonarQube: S1144

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private void f() {} void g() { f(); } }
```

## java/unused-private-field

**Unused private fields should be removed.** Nothing reads or writes it.

SonarQube: S1068

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private int count; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private int count; int get() { return count; } }
```

## java/unused-private-class

**Unused private classes should be removed.** Nothing instantiates it.

SonarQube: S3985

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private static class B {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private static class B {} B b; }
```

## java/unused-local-variable

**Unused local variables should be removed.** A variable nothing reads is dead code.

SonarQube: S1481

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int unused = 3; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f() { int used = 3; return used; } }
```

## java/duplicated-string

**String literals should not be duplicated.** A literal repeated across the file is easy to change in one place and miss in the others.

SonarQube: S1192

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { String a = "connection failed"; String b = "connection failed"; String c = "connection failed"; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static final String MSG = "connection failed"; String a = MSG; String b = MSG; }
```

## java/instanceof-then-cast

**Pattern matching for instanceof should be used instead of a separate cast.** `if (o instanceof Foo f)` binds the cast value for you.

Off by default: add `"java/instanceof-then-cast"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S6201

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Object o) { if (o instanceof String) { String s = (String) o; use(s); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Object o) { if (o instanceof String s) { use(s); } } }
```

## java/autowired-with-many-constructors

**@Autowired should be used when multiple constructors are provided.** Spring needs to be told which constructor to use.

SonarQube: S6829

Noncompliant:

```java
@Component class A { A() {} A(B b) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Component class A { A() {} @Autowired A(B b) {} }
```

## java/empty-string-comparison

**String.isEmpty() should be used to test for emptiness.** isEmpty() says what is meant.

SonarQube: S7158

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return s.length() == 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { boolean f(String s) { return s.isEmpty(); } }
```

## java/jump-in-finally

**Jump statements should not occur in finally blocks.** A return, break or continue in finally discards any exception that is propagating.

SonarQube: S1143

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int f() { try { return g(); } finally { return 1; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int f() { try { return g(); } finally { close(); } } }
```

## java/externalizable-constructor

**Externalizable classes should have no-arguments constructors.** Deserialization creates the object with its public no-argument constructor.

SonarQube: S2060

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Externalizable { A(int x) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Externalizable { public A() {} }
```

## java/serializable-inner-class

**Serializable inner classes of non-serializable outer classes should be static.** Serializing an inner class drags in the outer instance it cannot serialize.

SonarQube: S2066

Noncompliant:

```java
class O { class I implements Serializable { } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class O { static class I implements Serializable { } }
```

## java/equals-without-type-check

**equals(Object obj) should test the argument's type.** Casting without a check throws ClassCastException for other types.

SonarQube: S2097

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(Object o) { A a = (A) o; return true; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public boolean equals(Object o) { if (!(o instanceof A)) return false; A a = (A) o; return true; } }
```

## java/predictable-string-operation

**String operations with predictable outcomes should be avoided.** Comparing two literals, or a literal with itself, always gives the same answer.

SonarQube: S2121

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean b = "a".equals("a"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { boolean b = x.equals("a"); } }
```

## java/return-value-ignored

**Return values from functions without side effects should not be ignored.** Calling toLowerCase() and dropping the result does nothing.

SonarQube: S2201

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { s.toLowerCase(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { s = s.toLowerCase(); } }
```

## java/servlet-mutable-field

**Servlets should not have mutable instance fields.** One servlet instance serves every request, so its fields are shared state.

SonarQube: S2226

Noncompliant:

```java
class S extends HttpServlet { int hits; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class S extends HttpServlet { final int limit = 3; }
```

## java/proxy-annotation-visibility

**Methods with Spring proxying annotations should be public.** Spring's proxies cannot intercept non-public methods.

SonarQube: S2230

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional public void f() {} }
```

## java/wait-on-thread

**wait, notify and notifyAll should not be called on Thread instances.** Thread uses its own monitor internally, so these calls interfere with join().

SonarQube: S2236

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(Thread thread) throws Exception { thread.wait(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(Object lock) throws Exception { lock.wait(); } }
```

## java/loop-never-runs

**Loop conditions should be true at least once.** A condition that is false on entry means the body never runs.

SonarQube: S2252

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 10; i < 5; i++) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {} } }
```

## java/wait-outside-synchronized

**wait, notify and notifyAll should only be called from synchronized code.** Without the monitor they throw IllegalMonitorStateException.

SonarQube: S2273

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { lock.wait(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (lock) { lock.wait(); } } }
```

## java/misleading-operator

**Non-existent operators like =+ should not be used.** `x =+ 1` assigns +1, which is almost never what was meant.

SonarQube: S2757

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x = 0; x =+ 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { int x = 0; x += 1; } }
```

## java/to-array-cast

**Collection.toArray() should be passed an array of the proper type.** Casting the Object[] result to a typed array throws ClassCastException.

SonarQube: S3020

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String[] a = (String[]) list.toArray(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String[] a = list.toArray(new String[0]); } }
```

## java/identical-branches

**All branches in a conditional structure should not have exactly the same implementation.** If every branch does the same thing, the condition decides nothing.

SonarQube: S3923

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else { f(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (a) { f(); } else { g(); } } }
```

## java/default-package-application

**@SpringBootApplication and @ComponentScan should not be used in the default package.** Spring would scan every class on the classpath.

SonarQube: S4602

Noncompliant:

```java
@SpringBootApplication class App {}
```

Compliant:

```java
package com.acme;
@SpringBootApplication class App {}
```

## java/thread-local-never-removed

**ThreadLocal variables should be cleaned up when no longer used.** In a thread pool a value left behind leaks into the next task.

SonarQube: S5164

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { static ThreadLocal<String> t = new ThreadLocal<>(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { static ThreadLocal<String> t = new ThreadLocal<>(); void done() { t.remove(); } }
```

## java/nested-test-class-without-nested

**JUnit5 inner test classes should be annotated with @Nested.** JUnit 5 only runs non-static inner test classes that are marked @Nested.

SonarQube: S5790

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { class Inner { @Test void a() {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Nested class Inner { @Test void a() {} } }
```

## java/test-silently-ignored

**JUnit5 test methods should not be silently ignored.** JUnit 5 skips private and static test methods without a warning.

SonarQube: S5810

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test private void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/competing-test-annotations

**Test methods should not be annotated with competing annotations.** A method with both @Test and @ParameterizedTest runs twice, or not as intended.

SonarQube: S5967

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test @ParameterizedTest void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @ParameterizedTest void a() {} }
```

## java/compute-if-absent-null

**Map computeIfAbsent() and computeIfPresent() should not be used to add null values.** A mapping function that returns null adds nothing.

SonarQube: S6104

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { m.computeIfAbsent(k, x -> null); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { m.computeIfAbsent(k, x -> new ArrayList<>()); } }
```

## java/async-in-configuration

**@Async should not be used on methods declared within a @Configuration class.** Configuration classes are proxied in a way that prevents @Async from working.

SonarQube: S6817

Noncompliant:

```java
@Configuration class C { @Async void f() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Component class C { @Async void f() {} }
```

## java/path-variable-not-bound

**Path template variables should be properly bound to method parameters.** A {variable} with no matching @PathVariable parameter is never read.

SonarQube: S6856

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @GetMapping("/users/{id}") String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @GetMapping("/users/{id}") String f(@PathVariable String id) { return id; } }
```

## java/duplicate-bean-name

**Beans in a @Configuration class should have different names.** Two @Bean methods with one name register one bean and silently override the other.

SonarQube: S6862

Noncompliant:

```java
@Configuration class C { @Bean A a() { return null; } @Bean B a(int x) { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@Configuration class C { @Bean A a() { return null; } @Bean B b() { return null; } }
```

## java/pbe-literal-salt

**Password hashing functions should use an unpredictable salt.** A fixed salt lets attackers precompute hashes for every user.

SonarQube: S2053

Standards: [CWE-759](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/759.html), [CWE-760](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/760.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new PBEKeySpec(pw, "salt".getBytes(), 10000, 256); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new PBEKeySpec(pw, randomSalt, 10000, 256); } }
```

## java/custom-crypto

**Custom cryptographic algorithms should not be used.** Home-made ciphers and digests are almost always weaker than the standard ones.

SonarQube: S2257

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class MyDigest extends MessageDigest { MyDigest() { super("x"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class MyDigest { }
```

## java/ldap-object-deserialization

**Deserialization of Java objects from LDAP should be disabled.** Returning objects from a search lets a malicious directory run code in the client.

SonarQube: S4434

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { controls.setReturningObjFlag(true); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { controls.setReturningObjFlag(false); } }
```

## java/activemq-untrusted-classes

**ActiveMQConnectionFactory should not be vulnerable to malicious code deserialization.** By default it will instantiate any class named in a message.

SonarQube: S5301

Standards: [CWE-502](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html), OWASP A08:2025 Software or Data Integrity Failures, OWASP A08:2021 Software and Data Integrity Failures

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { new ActiveMQConnectionFactory(url); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { ActiveMQConnectionFactory f = new ActiveMQConnectionFactory(url); f.setTrustedPackages(List.of("com.acme")); } }
```

## java/hardcoded-ip

**IP addresses should not be hard-coded.** A literal address breaks when the network changes and can't be overridden per environment.

SonarQube: S1313

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String host = "203.0.113.7"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String host = System.getenv("HOST");
String local = "127.0.0.1"; } }
```

## java/missing-override

**@Override should be used on overriding and implementing methods.** Without it, a typo in the signature silently creates a new method.

SonarQube: S1161

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public String toString() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Override public String toString() { return ""; } }
```

## java/unused-method-parameter

**Unused method parameters should be removed.** A parameter nothing reads misleads callers.

SonarQube: S1172

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private void f(int unused) { g(); } void h() { f(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private void f(int used) { g(used); } void h() { f(1); } }
```

## java/ignored-test-without-reason

**JUnit4 @Ignore and JUnit5 @Disabled annotations should be used to disable tests with a reason.** Without a reason, nobody knows when the test can come back.

SonarQube: S1607

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Disabled void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Disabled("flaky on CI") void a() {} }
```

## java/for-update-unrelated

**for loop increment clauses should modify the loops' counters.** An update clause that touches other variables than the ones the condition tests usually never ends the loop.

SonarQube: S1994

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 10; j++) {} } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {} } }
```

## java/transient-in-plain-class

**Fields in non-serializable classes should not be transient.** transient means nothing in a class that is never serialized.

SonarQube: S2065

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { transient int x; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { transient int x; }
```

## java/close-in-finally

**Try-with-resources should be used.** A resource closed in finally leaks if close() itself throws, and the code is longer.

SonarQube: S2093

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { use(in); } finally { in.close(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try (InputStream in = open()) { use(in); } } }
```

## java/log-and-rethrow

**Exceptions should be either logged or rethrown but not both.** The same failure then appears twice in the logs.

SonarQube: S2139

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { log.error("x", e); throw e; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { g(); } catch (IOException e) { throw new UncheckedIOException(e); } } }
```

## java/synchronized-read-object

**readObject should not be synchronized.** Deserialization already has exclusive access to the object.

SonarQube: S2675

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { private synchronized void readObject(ObjectInputStream in) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { private void readObject(ObjectInputStream in) {} }
```

## java/multiline-without-braces

**Multiline blocks should be enclosed in curly braces.** A second statement indented like the first looks guarded by the condition, but isn't.

SonarQube: S2681

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
  void f() {
    if (ok)
      a();
      b();
  }
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
  void f() {
    if (ok) {
      a();
      b();
    }
  }
}
```

## java/sleep-in-test

**Thread.sleep should not be used in tests.** Sleeping makes tests slow and flaky.

SonarQube: S2925

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() throws Exception { Thread.sleep(100); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { void a() throws Exception { Thread.sleep(100); } }
```

## java/incomplete-assertion

**Assertions should be complete.** assertThat(x) on its own asserts nothing.

SonarQube: S2970

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(actual); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(actual).isEqualTo(1); } }
```

## java/array-copy-loop

**Arrays and lists should not be copied using loops.** System.arraycopy and Arrays.copyOf are faster and clearer.

SonarQube: S3012

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) { b[i] = a[i]; } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { System.arraycopy(a, 0, b, 0, a.length); } }
```

## java/assertion-argument-order

**Assertion arguments should be passed in the correct order.** The expected value goes first, or the failure message is backwards.

SonarQube: S3415

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(result, 4); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertEquals(4, result); } }
```

## java/test-class-name

**Test classes should comply with a naming convention.** Build tools only run classes whose names match their pattern.

SonarQube: S3577

Noncompliant:

```java
class Checks { @Test void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class ChecksTest { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/assert-on-parameters

**Asserts should not be used to check the parameters of a public method.** Assertions are disabled by default, so the check silently disappears.

SonarQube: S4274

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public void f(int x) { assert x > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public void f(int x) { if (x <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException(); } }
```

## java/composed-request-mapping

**Composed @RequestMapping variants should be preferred.** @GetMapping says it in one word.

SonarQube: S4488

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @RequestMapping(value = "/x", method = RequestMethod.GET) String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @GetMapping("/x") String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/jdbc-driver-for-name

**Class.forName() should not load JDBC 4.0+ drivers.** Drivers register themselves, so the call is dead weight.

SonarQube: S4925

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Class.forName("com.mysql.cj.jdbc.Driver"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Class.forName("com.acme.Plugin"); } }
```

## java/expected-in-test-annotation

**Exception testing via the @Test annotation should be avoided.** @Test(expected = X.class) passes if any line of the test throws X.

SonarQube: S5777

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test(expected = IOException.class) void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/expected-exception-rule

**Exception testing via the JUnit ExpectedException rule should not be mixed.** The rule is deprecated in favour of assertThrows.

SonarQube: S5776

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Rule ExpectedException thrown = ExpectedException.none(); }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { }
```

## java/jupiter-public-test

**JUnit5 test classes and methods should have default package visibility.** public adds nothing in JUnit 5.

SonarQube: S5786

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test public void a() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
class T { @Test void a() {} }
```

## java/too-many-assertions

**Test methods should not contain too many assertions.** A test with dozens of assertions fails at the first and hides the rest.

SonarQube: S5961

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { assertEquals(0, x); assertEquals(1, x); assertEquals(2, x); assertEquals(3, x); assertEquals(4, x); assertEquals(5, x); assertEquals(6, x); assertEquals(7, x); assertEquals(8, x); assertEquals(9, x); assertEquals(10, x); assertEquals(11, x); assertEquals(12, x); assertEquals(13, x); assertEquals(14, x); assertEquals(15, x); assertEquals(16, x); assertEquals(17, x); assertEquals(18, x); assertEquals(19, x); assertEquals(20, x); assertEquals(21, x); assertEquals(22, x); assertEquals(23, x); assertEquals(24, x); assertEquals(25, x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { assertEquals(1, x); } }
```

## java/region-not-set

**Region should be set explicitly when creating a new AWS client.** The default region comes from the environment and differs between machines.

SonarQube: S6241

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().build(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().region(r).build(); } }
```

## java/credentials-provider-not-set

**Credentials Provider should be set explicitly when creating a new AWS client.** The default provider chain tries many sources and picks different ones in different places.

SonarQube: S6242

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().region(r).build(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { S3Client c = S3Client.builder().region(r).credentialsProvider(p).build(); } }
```

## java/aws-region-hardcoded

**AWS region should not be set with a hardcoded String.** A literal region makes the code awkward to deploy elsewhere.

SonarQube: S6262

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Region r = Region.of("eu-west-1"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Region r = Region.of(config.region()); } }
```

## java/value-annotation-literal

**@Value annotation should inject property or SpEL expression.** @Value("text") injects the literal text, which is rarely intended.

SonarQube: S6804

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Value("plain") String s; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Value("${app.name}") String s; }
```

## java/optional-primitive-request-param

**Optional REST parameters should have an object type.** A primitive cannot hold the null that a missing parameter produces.

SonarQube: S6814

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { String f(@RequestParam(required = false) int page) { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { String f(@RequestParam(required = false) Integer page) { return ""; } }
```

## java/controller-should-be-rest

**@Controller should be replaced with @RestController.** When every method has @ResponseBody, @RestController says it once.

SonarQube: S6833

Noncompliant:

```java
@Controller class C { @ResponseBody String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
@RestController class C { String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/cacheable-and-cacheput

**@Cacheable and @CachePut should not be combined.** One reads from the cache, the other always writes; together they contradict.

SonarQube: S7179

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @Cacheable("a") @CachePut("a") String f() { return ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @Cacheable("a") String f() { return ""; } }
```

## java/pageable-parameter

**Methods returning Page or Slice must take Pageable as an input parameter.** Spring Data needs the Pageable to know which page to fetch.

SonarQube: S7186

Noncompliant:

```java
interface R { Page<A> find(String name); }
```

Compliant:

```java
interface R { Page<A> find(String name, Pageable p); }
```

## java/transaction-callback-signature

**@BeforeTransaction and @AfterTransaction methods must be void and take no arguments.** Spring invokes them without arguments and ignores their result.

SonarQube: S7190

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeTransaction int f() { return 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeTransaction void f() {} }
```

## java/junit4-assert-in-jupiter

**JUnit Jupiter tests should not use JUnit 4 assertions.** Jupiter and JUnit 4 put the message in different positions.

SonarQube: S8715

Noncompliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.Assert;
class T { }
```

Compliant:

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions;
class T { }
```

## java/duplicate-lifecycle-method

**Test classes should not contain multiple lifecycle methods of the same type.** JUnit does not define the order of two @BeforeEach methods.

SonarQube: S8745

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeEach void a() {} @BeforeEach void b() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @BeforeEach void a() {} @AfterEach void b() {} }
```

## java/commented-out-code

**Sections of code should not be commented out.** Commented-out code rots, and version control already remembers it.

SonarQube: S125

Noncompliant:

```java
class A {
// int x = compute(1);
}
```

Compliant:

```java
class A {
// Compute the total price.
}
```

## java/package-name

**Package names should comply with a naming convention.** Packages are lower case.

SonarQube: S120

Noncompliant:

```java
package com.Acme;
class A { }
```

Compliant:

```java
package com.acme;
class A { }
```

## java/switch-case-falls-through

**Switch cases should end with an unconditional break statement.** A case that runs into the next one is usually a forgotten break.

SonarQube: S128

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: a(); case 2: b(); break; default: c(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f(int x) { switch (x) { case 1: a(); break; case 2: b(); break; default: c(); } } }
```

## java/local-shadows-field

**Local variables should not shadow class fields.** The field is then unreachable by its plain name.

SonarQube: S1117

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { int count; void f() { int count = 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { int count; void f() { int total = 1; } }
```

## java/empty-statement

**Empty statements should be removed.** A stray semicolon is either noise or a bug.

SonarQube: S1116

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g();; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { g(); } }
```

## java/assertion-in-run

**JUnit assertions should not be used in run methods.** An assertion that fails in another thread is lost; the test still passes.

SonarQube: S2186

Noncompliant:

```java
class W implements Runnable { public void run() { assertEquals(1, x); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class W implements Runnable { public void run() { result = x; } }
```

## java/testcase-without-tests

**TestCases should contain tests.** A JUnit 3 TestCase with no test methods runs nothing.

SonarQube: S2187

Noncompliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { void helper() {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { public void testIt() {} }
```

## java/testcase-lifecycle-super

**JUnit test cases should call super methods.** Skipping super.setUp() or super.tearDown() skips the parent fixture.

SonarQube: S2188

Noncompliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { protected void setUp() { init(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T extends TestCase { protected void setUp() { super.setUp(); init(); } }
```

## java/wait-outside-loop

**Object.wait(...) should be called inside a loop.** A woken thread must re-check its condition, because wake-ups can be spurious.

SonarQube: S2274

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (lock) { lock.wait(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() throws Exception { synchronized (lock) { while (!ready) { lock.wait(); } } } }
```

## java/volatile-compound-operation

**volatile variables should not be used with compound operators.** count++ on a volatile field is a read and a write, so updates are lost.

SonarQube: S3078

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { volatile int count; void f() { count++; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { AtomicInteger count; void f() { count.incrementAndGet(); } }
```

## java/week-year-mismatch

**DateTimeFormatters should not use mismatched year and week numbers.** A calendar year combined with a week number gives the wrong date at the year boundary.

SonarQube: S5917

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DateTimeFormatter f = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-ww"); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { DateTimeFormatter f = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM"); } }
```

## java/model-attribute-name

**Model attributes should follow the Java identifier naming convention.** A name that is not an identifier cannot be referenced from the view.

SonarQube: S6806

Noncompliant:

```java
class C { @ModelAttribute("my-name") Object a() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class C { @ModelAttribute("myName") Object a() { return null; } }
```

## java/hardcoded-path

**URIs should not be hardcoded.** A literal path or file URI breaks on another machine.

SonarQube: S1075

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String p = "/home/app/data"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String p = config.dataDir(); } }
```

## java/redundant-cast

**Redundant casts should not be used.** Casting a value to the type it already has does nothing.

SonarQube: S1905

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = (String) "x"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String s = "x"; } }
```

## java/serializable-field-type

**Fields in a Serializable class should either be transient or serializable.** A List or Map field is only serializable if the instance behind it is.

SonarQube: S1948

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private List<String> items; }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private transient List<String> items; }
```

## java/read-resolve-visibility

**readResolve methods should be inheritable.** A private readResolve is ignored when a subclass is deserialized.

SonarQube: S2062

Noncompliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { private Object readResolve() { return this; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A implements Serializable { protected Object readResolve() { return this; } }
```

## java/pointless-math

**Do not perform unnecessary mathematical operations.** Rounding an integer, or taking the floor of one, changes nothing.

SonarQube: S2185

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { double d = Math.floor(5); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { double d = Math.floor(x); } }
```

## java/test-without-assertion

**Tests should include assertions.** A test that asserts nothing passes whatever the code does.

SonarQube: S2699

Noncompliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { service.run(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class T { @Test void a() { assertEquals(1, service.run()); } }
```

## java/commons-date-truncate

**DateUtils.truncate from Apache Commons Lang should not be used.** It misbehaves around DST changes.

SonarQube: S2718

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Date d = DateUtils.truncate(now, Calendar.DATE); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.now(); } }
```

## java/nullable-primitive

**@CheckForNull or @Nullable should not be used on primitive types.** A primitive can never be null.

SonarQube: S4682

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Nullable int f() { return 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Nullable Integer f() { return null; } }
```

## java/assertj-simplifiable

**AssertJ assertions should be simplified to the corresponding dedicated assertion.** hasSize(n) and isEmpty() report the actual contents when they fail; isEqualTo(0) does not.

SonarQube: S5838

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(list.size()).isEqualTo(3); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(list).hasSize(3); } }
```

## java/assertj-chain

**Consecutive AssertJ assertThat statements should be chained.** Two statements on the same subject can be one chain.

SonarQube: S5853

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(x).isNotNull(); assertThat(x).isEqualTo(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThat(x).isNotNull().isEqualTo(1); } }
```

## java/assertthatthrownby-alone

**AssertJ assertThatThrownBy should not be used alone.** Without a follow-up it only checks that something was thrown.

SonarQube: S5958

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThatThrownBy(() -> f()); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThatThrownBy(() -> f()).isInstanceOf(IOException.class); } }
```

## java/mockito-times-one

**Call to Mockito verify should be simplified.** times(1) is the default.

SonarQube: S6068

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { verify(mock, times(1)).run(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { verify(mock).run(); } }
```

## java/mockito-qualified

**Mockito core methods should be statically imported.** Mockito.when(...) reads worse than when(...).

SonarQube: S8924

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { Mockito.when(x.get()).thenReturn(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { when(x.get()).thenReturn(1); } }
```

## java/proxied-call-through-this

**Methods with Spring proxy should not be called via this.** A call from inside the class bypasses the proxy, so @Transactional and friends do nothing.

SonarQube: S6809

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional void a() {} void b() { a(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { @Transactional void a() {} void b() { other.a(); } }
```

## java/select-star

**SQL queries should retrieve only necessary fields.** SELECT * breaks when columns are added and fetches more than is used.

SonarQube: S6905

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String q = "SELECT * FROM users"; } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { String q = "SELECT id, name FROM users"; } }
```

## java/multiple-main-methods

**Only one main method should be defined in a class.** Two entry points make it unclear which one starts the program.

SonarQube: S8446

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] a) {} public static void main(String a) {} }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { public static void main(String[] a) {} }
```

## java/month-numeric-literal

**Month and DayOfWeek enums should be used instead of numeric literals.** LocalDate.of(2024, 3, 1) hides which month 3 is.

SonarQube: S8694

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.of(2024, 3, 1); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { LocalDate d = LocalDate.of(2024, Month.MARCH, 1); } }
```

## java/try-fail-catch

**Dedicated exception assertions should be used instead of try-catch with fail.** assertThrows states the intent and fails with a clear message.

SonarQube: S8714

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { try { f(); fail(); } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { assertThrows(IOException.class, () -> f()); } }
```

## java/ignored-status-return

**Return values should not be ignored when they contain the operation status code.** mkdir() and createNewFile() report failure only through their result.

SonarQube: S899

Noncompliant:

```java
class A { void f() { dir.mkdirs(); } }
```

Compliant:

```java
class A { void f() { if (!dir.mkdirs()) { fail(); } } }
```

## c/no-gets

**gets() should never be used.** gets() cannot limit input size and always allows a buffer overflow.

Standards: [CWE-120](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/120.html), [CWE-242](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/242.html)

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(char *b) { gets(b); }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(char *b, int n) { fgets(b, n, stdin); }
```

## c/unsafe-string-function

**Unbounded string functions should not be used.** strcpy, strcat, sprintf and vsprintf do not check the destination size.

Standards: [CWE-120](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/120.html), [CWE-676](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/676.html), OWASP A06:2025 Insecure Design

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(char *d, const char *s) { strcpy(d, s); }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(char *d, const char *s, int n) { snprintf(d, n, "%s", s); }
```

## c/assignment-in-condition

**Assignments should not be used as conditions.** `if (x = y)` is almost always a typo for `==`. Use double parentheses if intended.

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(int x) { if (x = 5) { g(); } }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(int x) { if (x == 5) { g(); } if ((x = h())) { g(); } }
```

## c/goto

**goto should not be used.** goto makes control flow hard to follow.

Noncompliant:

```c
void f(void) { goto end; end: return; }
```

Compliant:

```c
void f(void) { return; }
```

## cs/self-assignment

**Variables should not be assigned to themselves.** Assigning a variable to itself does nothing; it usually means the wrong name was typed.

SonarQube: S1656

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; void F() { x = x; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; void F(int x) { this.x = x; } }
```

## cs/lost-increment

**Increments and decrements should not be overwritten.** In `i = i++` the increment happens, then the old value is assigned back, so `i` does not change.

SonarQube: S2123

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int i) { i = i++; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int i) { i++; } }
```

## cs/reversed-operator

**Assignment operators should not be reversed.** `x =+ 1` assigns `+1` to `x`; `x += 1` was almost certainly meant. The same goes for `=-` and `=!`.

SonarQube: S2757

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { x =+ 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int x) { x += 1; } }
```

## cs/double-unary

**'!' and '~' should not be doubled.** `!!x` is `x` and `~~x` is `x`: the doubled operator does nothing, or another operator was meant.

SonarQube: S2761

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b) { return !!b; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b) { return b; } }
```

## cs/for-loop-wrong-direction

**A for loop's update should move its counter towards the end condition.** A counter compared with `<` but decremented (or with `>` but incremented) never reaches the end, so the loop runs until the value wraps around.

SonarQube: S2251

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i--) { G(i); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int n) { for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { G(i); } } }
```

## cs/collection-size-sign

**Collection sizes should not be compared with negative bounds.** `Count`, `Length` and `Count()` are never negative, so `>= 0` is always true and `< 0` always false.

SonarQube: S3981

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int[] a) { return a.Length >= 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int[] a) { return a.Length > 0; } }
```

## cs/async-void

**Async methods should not return void.** Exceptions from an `async void` method can't be caught by the caller and crash the process, and the caller can't await it. Event handlers are the exception, since their signature is fixed.

SonarQube: S3168

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { async void Load() { await Task.Delay(1); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { async Task Load() { await Task.Delay(1); } void OnClick(object sender, EventArgs e) { } async void OnLoad(object sender, EventArgs e) { await Load(); } }
```

## cs/accessor-ignores-value

**Set, init, add and remove accessors should use 'value'.** An accessor that never reads `value` silently drops what the caller passed in.

SonarQube: S3237

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; int X { get { return x; } set { x = 42; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int x; int X { get { return x; } set { x = value; } } int Y { set => throw new NotSupportedException(); } }
```

## cs/destructor-throws

**Finalizers should not throw exceptions.** An exception thrown from a finalizer terminates the process on .NET, unless it is caught.

SonarQube: S1048

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { ~A() { throw new InvalidOperationException(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { ~A() { Release(); } }
```

## cs/to-string-returns-null

**ToString() should not return null.** Callers expect `ToString()` to return text; `null` makes string formatting and concatenation fail later.

SonarQube: S2225

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public override string ToString() { return null; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public override string ToString() { return ""; } }
```

## cs/identical-branches

**Every branch of a conditional should not do the same thing.** When the `if` and every `else`, or both sides of `?:`, are identical, the condition is pointless, or one branch has the wrong code.

SonarQube: S3923

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(bool b) { return b ? 1 : 1; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(bool b) { return b ? 1 : 2; } }
```

## cs/anonymous-unsubscribe

**Anonymous functions should not be used to unsubscribe from events.** Each lambda or anonymous method is a new delegate, so `-=` with one removes nothing and the original handler stays subscribed.

SonarQube: S3244

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b.Changed -= (s, e) => G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(B b) { b.Changed -= OnChanged; } }
```

## cs/duplicate-condition

**Conditions in an if/else if chain should not repeat.** A condition already tested earlier in the chain never selects its branch, so that code is dead.

SonarQube: S1862

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { if (a == 1) { G(); } else if (a == 1) { H(); } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(int a) { if (a == 1) { G(); } else if (a == 2) { H(); } } }
```

## cs/identical-operands

**Identical expressions should not be used on both sides of a binary operator.** `a == a` is always true and `a - a` always zero: one side is probably the wrong expression.

SonarQube: S1764

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a) { return a == a; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return a == b; } }
```

## cs/empty-block

**Nested blocks should not be left empty.** An empty `if`, loop, `try`, `catch` or `finally` block is either unfinished or unneeded. A comment explaining why it is empty is enough.

SonarQube: S108

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { /* already logged by G */ } } }
```

## cs/empty-method

**Methods should not be empty.** An empty method is unfinished or unneeded. If it is empty on purpose, say why in a comment, or throw `NotSupportedException` when callers shouldn't use it.

SonarQube: S1186

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Reset() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public void Reset() { /* stateless: nothing to reset */ } public virtual void OnStart() { } }
```

## cs/rethrow-loses-stack

**Exceptions should be rethrown with 'throw;'.** `throw e;` restarts the exception's stack trace at this line, losing where it was first thrown.

SonarQube: S3445

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); throw e; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException e) { Log(e); throw; } } }
```

## cs/useless-catch

**Catch clauses should do more than rethrow.** A `catch` that only rethrows changes nothing; without it the exception propagates the same way.

SonarQube: S2737

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException) { throw; } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { try { G(); } catch (IOException) { throw; } catch (Exception e) { Log(e); } } }
```

## cs/general-exception

**General or reserved exceptions should not be thrown.** Callers can't catch `Exception` or `ApplicationException` without catching everything else too, and the runtime reserves `NullReferenceException`, `IndexOutOfRangeException` and `OutOfMemoryException` for itself.

SonarQube: S112

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new Exception("failed"); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { throw new InvalidOperationException("failed"); } }
```

## cs/obsolete-without-explanation

**[Obsolete] should say what to use instead.** Callers warned that something is obsolete need to know what replaces it.

SonarQube: S1123

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { [Obsolete] void F() { G(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { [Obsolete("Use G instead.")] void F() { G(); } }
```

## cs/nested-ternary

**Ternary operators should not be nested.** A `?:` inside another is hard to read; an `if` chain or a `switch` expression is clearer.

SonarQube: S3358

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return a > 0 ? 1 : a < 0 ? -1 : 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(int a) { return Math.Sign(a); } }
```

## cs/inverted-comparison

**Boolean checks should not be inverted.** `!(a == b)` reads worse than `a != b`, which says the same thing.

SonarQube: S1940

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return !(a == b); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(int a, int b) { return a != b; } }
```

## cs/redundant-boolean-literal

**Boolean literals should not be redundant.** `b ? true : false` is `b`, and `x && true` is `x`. Comparisons such as `x == true` are left alone: on a nullable `bool` they aren't redundant, and the syntax tree can't tell.

SonarQube: S1125 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b) { return b ? true : false; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(bool b, bool? c) { return b || c == true; } }
```

## cs/count-instead-of-any

**Any() should be used to test whether a sequence is empty.** `Count()` may enumerate the whole sequence to answer a yes-or-no question that `Any()` answers at the first element.

SonarQube: S1155

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(IEnumerable<int> xs) { return xs.Count() > 0; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(IEnumerable<int> xs) { return xs.Any(); } }
```

## cs/where-then-predicate-method

**Where() should not be followed by a method that takes the predicate itself.** `xs.Where(p).First()` is `xs.First(p)`: one call is shorter and does the same.

SonarQube: S2971 (related)

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.Where(x => x > 0).First(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { int F(List<int> xs) { return xs.First(x => x > 0); } }
```

## cs/gc-collect

**GC.Collect() should not be called.** Forcing a garbage collection pauses the application and usually makes overall performance worse than the runtime's own scheduling.

SonarQube: S1215

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { GC.Collect(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { GC.KeepAlive(this); } }
```

## cs/visible-mutable-static-field

**Static fields visible outside their class should be constant or read-only.** Any code can change a public, protected or internal static field, so its value can't be relied on, and changes race between threads.

SonarQube: S2223

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public static int Limit = 10; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public static readonly int Limit = 10; private static int count; }
```

## cs/redundant-base-type

**Inheritance lists should not name the default base type.** Every class already derives from `object` and every enum is already backed by `int`.

SonarQube: S1939

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum Color : int { Red, Green }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
enum Color : byte { Red, Green }
```

## cs/empty-statement

**Empty statements should be removed.** A stray `;` does nothing, and can hide a mistake such as a semicolon after an `if`.

SonarQube: S1116

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G();; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); while (Next()) ; } }
```

## cs/redundant-jump

**Redundant return and continue statements should be removed.** A `return;` at the end of a method, or a `continue;` at the end of a loop, does what would happen anyway.

SonarQube: S3626

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { G(); return; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool b) { if (b) { return; } G(); } }
```

## cs/protected-in-sealed

**Sealed classes should not declare protected members.** A sealed class has no subclasses, so `protected` means `private` there and misleads readers.

Off by default: add `"cs/protected-in-sealed"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S2156

Noncompliant:

```csharp
sealed class A { protected int count; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
sealed class A : B { private int count; protected override void F() { } }
```

## cs/abstract-class-public-constructor

**Abstract classes should not have public constructors.** Only subclasses can call an abstract class's constructor, so `public` promises more than it gives.

SonarQube: S3442

Noncompliant:

```csharp
abstract class A { public A() { Init(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
abstract class A { protected A() { Init(); } }
```

## cs/redundant-constructor

**Redundant constructors and finalizers should be removed.** An empty public parameterless constructor, when it is the class's only constructor, is what the compiler generates anyway. An empty finalizer only slows garbage collection.

Off by default: add `"cs/redundant-constructor"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3253

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { public A() { } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { public A() { } public A(int x) { X = x; } int X; }
```

## cs/mergeable-if

**Nested if statements should be merged.** An `if` whose only statement is another `if`, neither with an `else`, reads more simply as one condition joined with `&&`.

SonarQube: S1066

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) { if (a) { if (b) { G(); } } } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(bool a, bool b) { if (a && b) { G(); } } }
```

## cs/empty-string-comparison

**string.IsNullOrEmpty() should be used to test for empty strings.** `s == ""` misses `null`, which usually needs the same handling; `string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)` covers both and says what is meant.

Off by default: add `"cs/empty-string-comparison"` to `rules.enabled` to run it.

SonarQube: S3256

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return s == ""; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { bool F(string s) { return string.IsNullOrEmpty(s); } }
```

## cs/enum-name-suffix

**Enum names should not end with 'Enum' or 'Flags'.** The suffix repeats what the declaration already says.

SonarQube: S2344

Noncompliant:

```csharp
enum ColorEnum { Red, Green }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
enum Color { Red, Green }
```

## cs/weak-hash

**Weak hashing algorithms should not be used.** MD5, SHA-1 and RIPEMD-160 are broken for security purposes: collisions can be computed.

SonarQube: S4790

Standards: [CWE-1240](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/1240.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { byte[] F(byte[] d) { return MD5.Create().ComputeHash(d); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { byte[] F(byte[] d) { return SHA256.Create().ComputeHash(d); } }
```

## cs/weak-cipher

**Weak cipher algorithms should not be used.** DES, 3DES and RC2 use keys or blocks small enough to break in practice.

SonarQube: S5547

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { SymmetricAlgorithm F() { return DES.Create(); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { SymmetricAlgorithm F() { return Aes.Create(); } }
```

## cs/insecure-cipher-mode

**Encryption should use a secure mode and padding.** ECB mode encrypts equal blocks to equal ciphertext, which leaks patterns, and RSA with PKCS#1 v1.5 padding is open to padding-oracle attacks.

SonarQube: S5542

Standards: [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), [CWE-780](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/780.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(Aes aes) { aes.Mode = CipherMode.ECB; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(RSA rsa, byte[] d) { rsa.Encrypt(d, RSAEncryptionPadding.OaepSHA256); } }
```

## cs/weak-tls-version

**Weak SSL/TLS protocol versions should not be used.** SSL 2, SSL 3, TLS 1.0 and TLS 1.1 have known weaknesses. Leave the choice to the operating system, or require TLS 1.2 or later.

SonarQube: S4423

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), [CWE-327](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/327.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F() { ServicePointManager.SecurityProtocol = SecurityProtocolType.Tls12; } }
```

## cs/weak-key-size

**RSA and DSA keys should be at least 2048 bits long.** Keys shorter than 2048 bits can be factored with today's computing power.

SonarQube: S4426

Standards: [CWE-326](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/326.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { RSA F() { return new RSACryptoServiceProvider(1024); } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { RSA F() { return RSA.Create(3072); } }
```

## cs/certificate-validation-disabled

**Server certificates should be validated.** A certificate validation callback that always returns `true` accepts any certificate, so anyone on the network path can impersonate the server.

SonarQube: S4830

Standards: [CWE-295](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/295.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A05:2021 Security Misconfiguration, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpClientHandler h) { h.ServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback = (m, c, ch, e) => true; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(HttpClientHandler h) { h.ServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback = (m, c, ch, e) => e == SslPolicyErrors.None; } }
```

## cs/ldap-anonymous

**LDAP connections should be authenticated.** An anonymous or unauthenticated LDAP bind lets anyone who can reach the directory read it, and sometimes change it.

SonarQube: S4433

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(DirectoryEntry d) { d.AuthenticationType = AuthenticationTypes.None; } }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { void F(DirectoryEntry d) { d.AuthenticationType = AuthenticationTypes.Secure; } }
```

## cs/empty-db-password

**Database connection strings should not have an empty password.** A database reachable with an empty password is open to anyone who can connect to it.

SonarQube: S2115

Standards: [CWE-521](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/521.html), OWASP A07:2025 Authentication Failures, OWASP A07:2021 Identification and Authentication Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Db = "Server=db;Database=app;User Id=app;Password=;"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Db = "Server=db;Database=app;Integrated Security=true;Password=;"; }
```

## cs/cleartext-url

**Clear-text protocols should not be used.** Data sent over http, ftp or telnet can be read and changed by anyone on the network path.

SonarQube: S5332 (related)

Standards: [CWE-319](https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html), OWASP A04:2025 Cryptographic Failures, OWASP A02:2021 Cryptographic Failures

Noncompliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Api = "http://api.payments.com/v1"; }
```

Compliant:

```csharp
class A { const string Api = "https://api.payments.com/v1"; const string Ns = "http://tempuri.org/"; }
```
